WHOLE PRODUCT FILE M packages/core/CHANGELOG.md
# Changelog

## Unreleased — deterministic schema (P08.2)

- Remove configurable identifier strategies and the twelve table-name options. Models generate UUIDv7 with Laravel HasUuids; migrations and queries use package-owned table literals. Remove IdStrategy, IdColumns, HasConfiguredId and the unused ResolvesCoreOptions schema bridge without aliases. Native Eloquent insert assigns IDs during quiet synchronous audit writes.

## Unreleased — scheduling contracts (P07.5)

- Schema delivery now uses tagged `SchemaSetProvider` declarations and `corex:schema:migrate`; migration publishing is removed.
- Add the schema registry, migrator, scope/lifecycle DTOs, commands and testing trait; remove provider schema-version constants. Add coded schema errors.

- Added kernel module `CoreX\Scheduling`. Moved `WakeupScheduler`, `DueWakeupSource`, `DueWakeupHandler`, `WakeupRequest`, `WakeupWindow`, `WakeupDelivery`, `WakeupHandlerDefinition`, `ScheduleResult`, `CancelResult` and `SweepResult` from `CoreX\Wakeup` with no aliases. `DueWakeupSource::TAG` is `corex.scheduling.due-sources`; `WakeupHandlerDefinition::TAG` is `corex.scheduling.wakeup-handlers`.

## Unreleased — PLAN3.P07.3 mandatory synchronous audit

- Added the `SynchronousAuditWriter` SPI and stock committed database writer for mandatory support-access events. It bypasses optional audit disablement and queues, rejects ambient transactions, and requires an event-time snapshot.

## Unreleased — PLAN3.P07.2 tenancy implementation cleanup

- TenantDatabaseLifecycle documentation refers to the physical database implementation; the kernel contract
  signatures and boxed implementation are unchanged. Module connection documentation names TenancyManager.

## Unreleased — CloudEvents wire (P06.3)

- CloudEvents 1.0 structured JSON codec with internal/external profiles; MessageContract dataSchema, MessageEnvelope source URI-reference validation and withPayload. MessagePublisher wire uses application/cloudevents+json (PLAN3.D90/D92).

## Unreleased — ports instead of facades, helpers and static state (P05.3)

- Runtime code outside the Laravel bridge takes `ConnectionResolverInterface`, `ClockInterface`, `Dispatcher` and the other `Illuminate\Contracts` ports through constructors instead of facades, global helpers and static clocks; `Messaging\Internal\PubSubManager` is gone (the `PubSub` binding selects the driver from `CoreOptions::pubsubDriver`). Added the `CoreX\Database\Laravel\ResolvesCoreOptions` SPI trait for migrations and models that read `CoreOptions` through the container bridge.

## Unreleased — thin providers and registrars (P05.2)

- `CoreServiceProvider` is the only public provider of the kernel: it registers `ModulesServiceProvider` itself (after the kernel bindings, so module-aware services refine them) and delegates bindings to per-module registrars (`Config`, `Context`, `Data`, `Messaging`, `Settings`, `Audit`, `Features`, `Departments`, `Storage`, `Time`, `Observability`, `Errors`, modules). Apps no longer need to list `ModulesServiceProvider` separately. `corex:modules:repair` is a regular command class.

## Unreleased — typed Options and runtime configuration checks (P05.1)

- Removed the static `CoreX\Config\Internal\Config` holder: `CoreOptions` is a lazy shared singleton read once at boot (`app(CoreOptions::class)` in migrations and tests; call `forgetInstance(CoreOptions::class)` after writing `corex` config in a booted test). Added `CoreOptions::$acknowledgedDangerousModes` (`corex.acknowledged_dangerous_modes`, exact config paths only), the `RuntimeConfigCheck` SPI and `RuntimeConfigGate`: checks run when the HTTP application finishes booting or a console command starts, except build commands. `corex:config:validate` runs them too, accepts `--production`, lists acknowledged dangerous modes as warnings and reports the `corex-operations.require_physical_tenancy=false` opt-out as a notice. `CoreOptionsParser::current()` is deprecated and returns the shared `CoreOptions`.

## Unreleased — CI ownership and test isolation repair (P10.2)

- Added `Messaging\Laravel\InboxRetention` for explicit cleanup of a consumer's selected inbox message IDs. Other consumers and IDs remain retained; callers must establish that the selected messages cannot be replayed.

## Unreleased — kernel package and API migration

- Breaking (PLAN3.P02–P04, pre-1.0): CoreX owns the kernel feature namespaces under one PSR-4 root; Api/Spi attributes, CoreOptions, ExtensionContribution and coded error descriptors declare the supported kernel and extension contracts. Runtime models and scanners no longer grant an implicit public contract.
- Package installation uses the `axiomasoft` Composer vendor and PHP 8.4–8.5 / Laravel 12–13 constraints; `resources/contracts/php-api.json` records the explicitly attributed API and SPI. Old names have no compatibility aliases.

## Unreleased — PHP surface strict cutover

- Breaking (PLAN3.P04, pre-1.0): supported PHP symbols are declared individually with Api/Spi attributes. Concrete implementation types moved to Internal and typed ports/DTOs and error contracts replace predecessor surfaces as recorded in PLAN3.D63/D64; namespace metadata no longer grants compatibility.
- Generated `resources/contracts/php-api.json` records supported layers and signatures. Strict source/graph/BC checks enforce the declared surface; Internal has no external BC promise.

## Unreleased — typed error contract (P04.3)

- Added package owned error enums, definitions and safe coded exception descriptors.
- Error constructors require enum identities; existing error wire values are scoped.
- Contributions are validated and frozen at boot; persisted identities use validated lookup.

## Unreleased — P04.2

- Закрыта API/SPI-поверхность: скалярные контракты, JSON-документы и снимки CoreOptions; конкретные реализации принадлежат Internal.


## Unreleased — source API metadata (PLAN3.P04.1)

- Add final readonly `CoreX\Attributes\Api` and `Spi` metadata and the `SpiKind` backed enum.
- Add a source-only API/SPI tooling scanner and deterministic `php-api/1` manifests, with nested PHPDoc, named-parameter and inheritance diagnostics.
- The report command exposes classification debt; existing namespace/package gates remain required until the strict cutover. Parser dependencies remain development-only.


## Unreleased — package identifier alignment

### Changed

- PLAN3.D57: implementation classes moved to `<Module>\Internal\…`, public types to module categories
  (`Contracts|Data|Enums|Events|Exceptions`); see `docs/architecture/constitution.md` STR-003. Drain queues before
  upgrading; stored `*_type` values holding an old model FQCN no longer resolve (no aliases, PLAN3.D01).

- Breaking before 1.0: Move module configuration into `corex.modules` within `config/corex.php`; publish it through `corex-config`, combine kernel migration tags, and rename the PubSub prune command to `corex:messaging:prune-pubsub`. Module cache identifiers use `corex.modules`.

## Unreleased

- Public promoted properties are captured even when their constructor is private. Runtime entity registry bindings now use the shared Internal implementation while the Testing SPI keeps its signatures. Parser and analyser dependencies are declared as optional development tooling.

- PHP API manifests now record direct inheritance, interfaces and traits separately and include only members declared by this package; external dependency members are omitted without changing the `php-api/1` schema. Source scanners move to the Attributes/Errors Tooling bridges and the durable payload checker moves to Messaging/PHPStan; runtime references to development bridges are forbidden.

- Added package-contributed configuration reports through `ConfigReportSection` and public messaging test fixtures. Saved Views default bindings now register with panels.

- Added immutable `DataScope`, `RecordRef`, and `WriteToken` boundary DTOs for optional data services.

Все заметные изменения `corex/core` документируются здесь. Формат — [Keep a Changelog](https://keepachangelog.com/en/1.1.0/).
Пакет pre-1.0 — breaking-изменения публичных контрактов легальны в MINOR до 1.0 (D17).

## [Unreleased]

- CloudEvents 1.0 structured JSON codec with internal/external profiles; MessageContract dataSchema, MessageEnvelope source URI-reference validation and withPayload; MessagePublisher wire now uses application/cloudevents+json (PLAN3.D90/D92, P06.3).

- PHP API manifests now record direct inheritance, interfaces and traits separately and include only members declared by this package; external dependency members are omitted without changing the `php-api/1` schema. Source scanners move to the Attributes/Errors Tooling bridges and the durable payload checker moves to Messaging/PHPStan; runtime references to development bridges are forbidden.

### Changed

- Replaced closure-based `DataScope` authorization with serializable `DataScopeAuthorizer` contract (P12.8, G-API/D41).

### Fixed

- Scoped storage rejects recursively encoded backslashes/NUL and replaced local
  root symlinks, including directory listings and usage measurement.

- Feature definitions isolate duplicate inserts in a savepoint, preserving outer
  PostgreSQL transactions and administrator toggles. Database-poll publishers
  serialize per channel until commit so durable cursors cannot skip pending rows.

### Added

- Exposed durable scope and account/workspace context types as a closed public signature graph; added the `IdentityPasswordRehasher` owner port. The guarded restorer implementation remains private.

- Declared the exact shared configuration support types and tenancy contracts used by package composition as public API; no Support namespace wildcard.

- `TenantStorage` with scoped durable/ephemeral local disks, explicit usage capability and an
  explicit unsupported-export outcome. The boxed adapter derives its root exclusively from the
  current account context and does not require tenancy, auth, Stancl, cloud storage, or archives.

- **`ImpersonationService` contract + `ImpersonationState` + boxed `NullImpersonationService`
  (P2.11, B-11 §5.9, D139/D140).** Lives in `corex/core` — the sole consumer, `CurrentActor`,
  is level 0 and must not import `corex/tenancy`; the cloud implementation rebinds via
  `Config::impersonationServiceClass()` (D10). `ImpersonationGuard` (L3 — semantic layer of the
  read-only invariant, D141) + `ImpersonationRestrictedAction` enum (closed, five cases §5.9) +
  `ImpersonationRestrictedException`. `ActorType::Support` added (D137) — `sys_audit_actor_ck`
  widened by a new additive migration (closed P1.8 file untouched). `CurrentActor` now resolves
  the impersonation contract from the container: `type()`/`id()`/`context()` distinguish a
  support actor from `user`/`system`.
- `FeatureFlagDefinition::make()` — статическая фабрика (+ `module()`/`payload()` fluent-сеттеры),
  зеркалит `SettingDefault`-канон; конструктор не тронут (P3.6).

### Changed

- Публичные тексты пакета продукт-нейтральны: composer-`description` («… for the Flex ecosystem»
  → «… for any Laravel application») и шапка `README.md` — уровень 0 доктрины (D91) не несёт
  привязки к продукту-потребителю (P2.17).
- Продуктовые докблоки публичной поверхности `Contracts/**`: убраны внутренние спека-ссылки
  (B-10 §…) из основного текста, перенесены в трейлинг `@internal spec: …`; `ModuleRegistrar`
  получил контрактный докблок (аддитивность регистрации, канонический путь динамического
  выключения через lifecycle-FSM corex/modules + рестарт воркера) (P3.6).

## [0.1.0] - 2026-07-15

Первый тегированный релиз. Реализует roadmap-фазу P0 / блупринт B-10 (примитивы ядра).

### Requires

- **PostgreSQL 16** для тенантской БД. Миграции ядра (`sys_*`) используют `ltree`, BRIN-индексы,
  `jsonb`, `UNIQUE NULLS NOT DISTINCT`, `pg_advisory_xact_lock` — PG-нативные конструкции без
  SQLite-эквивалента. SQLite-лейн (`composer test`) покрывает только pure-PHP код пакета для
  удобства unit-тестирования — это **не заявленная поддержка SQLite в продакшене** (OQ-12). Все
  гарантии целостности/конкурентности проверяются исключительно на PG-лейне (`composer test:pg`).

### Added

- Конфигурируемая ID-стратегия: `IdStrategy::Uuid7` добавлена и стала дефолтом (нативный PG `uuid`,
  time-sortable), наряду с существующими `ulid`/`int`-швами (P1.1, P1.20).
- `TenantContext`/`AccountRef`/`WorkspaceRef` — контракты для модели tenancy P2 (реализация — фаза P2) (P1.12).
- Settings v2: namespaced, scoped (`sys_settings`), обработка sensitive-значений, события изменений (P1.7);
  каскад скоупов и payload-фолбэк (P1.22).
- Audit v2: очередной `AuditEntry`, `sys_audit_log`, авто-observer, sensitive-фильтр колонок,
  killswitch-guard на шве записи, монотонный курсор `seq`, retention-шов (P1.8, P1.21).
- `DomainEvent` база (eventId/occurredAt/tenant), заменяет пустой маркер `CoreEvent`;
  `Cancellable` — отдельный опциональный интерфейс (P1.9, P1.25).
- FeatureFlags: персистентные `sys_feature_flags`/`sys_feature_overrides` вместо config-driven резолвера (P1.9, P1.22).
- Departments (`sys_departments`, `ltree`): `DepartmentRepository` с lock-then-read `move()`,
  after-commit доменные события, soft-delete (P1.10, P1.23).
- PubSub-контракт + драйверы `redis`/`db-poll`/`sync-null`, рантайм-guard, курсор/retention-шов (P1.11, P1.24).
- Новое доменное исключение `CrossWorkspaceMoveException` (guard в `Departments::move()`, P1.29).

### Changed

- Старый `EntityRegistry` (`CoreX\Registry\EntityRegistry`/`CoreX\Contracts\EntityRegistry`) **удалён** —
  заменён `EntityRegistry` v2 из `flex/modules` (см. его CHANGELOG). Breaking, легально pre-1.0 (P1.2).
- `IdColumns` эмитят нативные PG-типы по сконфигурированной стратегии (P1.20).

### Fixed

- `Audit`: sensitive-фильтр больше не пропускает помеченные атрибуты в `sys_audit_log`;
  killswitch теперь гардит непосредственно шов записи (P1.21).
- `Departments::move()`: теперь проверяет `workspace_id` нового родителя под уже взятым advisory-локом
  до перемещения — устраняет тихий cross-workspace перенос поддерева (M-2, P1.29).

### Deprecated / Will break in 0.2

- **Row-level tenancy** — `CoreX\Concerns\BelongsToTenant`, `CoreX\Scopes\TenantScope`,
  `CoreX\Contracts\TenantService`. Реализуют **опциональную row-level** мульти-тенантность и
  **замещаются физической DB-per-account моделью**, спроектированной для фазы P2 (ADR-004,
  stancl/tenancy v4). В 0.1.0 остаются рабочими (on-prem/boxed-инсталляции могут по-прежнему
  биндить `TenantService`), но новые фичи на этот шов строить не стоит — в 0.2 он будет заменён, не расширен.
- `AuditEntry`, `SettingsScope`, `TenantContext` — контракты расширены волной и будут расширены P2;
  считать их текущую форму предварительной.
- **`int`-стратегия ID** — ожидаемо конфликтует с DB-per-account (у каждой аккаунт-БД своя
  последовательность); избегать для новых tenant-scoped моделей.

### Known limitations

- Каскад скоупов `FeatureFlags` (`findOverride()`) — single-scope exact-match; narrow→wide не
  реализован (ждёт P2 tenancy).
- Secret-колонки вне `password`/`remember_token` (`api_token`, `two_factor_secret`) на
  `$guarded=[]`-моделях могут утекать в аудит, если хост не объявил `auditSensitive()`.


WHOLE PRODUCT FILE M packages/core/README.md
# axiomasoft/corex

Ядро CoreX для любого Laravel-приложения: `BaseModel` (ULID/UUIDv7/int-шов), `Settings` (каскад
System→Tenant→User), append-only `Audit`, `MoneyCast`, департаменты (ltree),
фичефлаги, PubSub-контракт, реестр сущностей и контракты отложенного пробуждения
`CoreX\Scheduling` (исполнение — пакет `corex-wakeup`). MIT.

Пакет ставится как зависимость приложения-потребителя; публичные контракты —
в `<Module>/Contracts/`; DTO помечены `#[Api]`, адаптеры интеграции — `#[Spi]`.
Классы под `Internal` принадлежат своему модулю и не являются контрактом приложения.

## Требование к СУБД

Ядро **требует PostgreSQL 16** для тенантской БД: `sys_*`-миграции используют
`ltree`/BRIN/`jsonb`/`UNIQUE NULLS NOT DISTINCT`/`pg_advisory_xact_lock` —
PG-нативные конструкции без SQLite-эквивалента. SQLite-лейн (`composer test`)
покрывает только pure-PHP код пакета для удобства unit-тестирования — это
НЕ заявленная поддержка SQLite в продакшене (OQ-12). Все гарантии
целостности/конкурентности проверяются исключительно на PG-лейне
(`composer test:pg`).

## Установка схемы

Настройки публикуются через `php artisan vendor:publish --tag=corex-config`.
Наборы `sys_*` и `mod_*` принадлежат ядру и выполняются непосредственно из пакета на PostgreSQL tenant-соединении:

```bash
php artisan corex:schema:migrate --scope=tenant --database=<tenant>
php artisan corex:schema:status --scope=tenant --database=<tenant>
```

В single-DB установке можно опустить `--database`: используется default connection.
В DB-per-account tenant-схема создаётся только на базе аккаунта; central-схема принадлежит tenancy.
Модульные наборы создаёт `ModuleLifecycle::enable()`; `--modules` явно включает их при подготовке шаблона.
Для production команда требует подтверждения или `--force`. Обычный `artisan migrate` создаёт только схему приложения.

### Версия схемы и апгрейды

Поколение каждого набора доступно через `CoreX\Database\Data\SchemaSet::version` в реестре
`CoreX\Database\Contracts\SchemaSets`. Файлы исполняются из пакета; копии в приложении не нужны.
После фиксации baseline изменение схемы поставляется новым файлом с повышением версии набора.
Повторный `corex:schema:migrate` применяет только отсутствующие в журнале Laravel `migrations` файлы.


## Mandatory support audit

Mandatory support-access events use `CoreX\Audit\Contracts\SynchronousAuditWriter`.
The stock writer commits directly to the configured audit table, requires an event-time
snapshot and rejects ambient transactions. It bypasses the optional audit logger and queue.
Custom implementations must provide the same committed, fail-closed behavior.

## Точность времени

Все `timestamptz`-колонки объявлены с явной микросекундной точностью
(`precision: 6`): PostgreSQL при `timestamp(0)` **округляет** значение, что
исказило бы хронологический порядок в юридически значимом append-only аудите
(`sys_audit_log`). `sys_audit_log.seq bigserial` — монотонный курсор аудита:
keyset-пагинация и tie-break сортировки идут **только по `seq`**, а не по
`id`/`occurred_at` (часы принявшего узла монотонности не дают).

## Compatibility evidence

The factual compatibility passport, its local proof class, and explicit limits
are in [PACKAGE.md](PACKAGE.md). It does not extend Composer constraints or
constitute a publication promise.

### Provider composition

Public providers are thin composition roots: they merge configuration, publish assets and register commands, and
delegate every binding to `PackageRegistrar` services listed in a `REGISTRARS` constant. A registrar keeps the
original binding order and lifetimes; a factory closure in a provider or registrar is a single `return`, and
anything with branching moves to a named `<Thing>Factory`. `CoreServiceProvider` registers `ModulesServiceProvider`
itself, so applications list only `CoreX\CoreServiceProvider`.

### Configuration report contributions

`corex:config:validate` collects services tagged with `CoreX\Config\Contracts\ConfigReportSection`.
Each package owns its options types, secret keys and defaults, drivers and adapters. Sections
supply an integer order; the command retains its stable report format and redacts secret values.
Kernel installations require no optional package to build the report.

Each package validates the shape of its configuration once, when its provider boots, through an
internal `<Pkg>Options` type; the shape check passes on an empty environment, so an image build
(`package:discover`, `config:cache`) needs no secrets. Requirements of the running process are
separate `CoreX\Config\Contracts\RuntimeConfigCheck` services tagged by the owning package: they
run when the HTTP application finishes booting or a console command starts (build commands such as
`package:discover`, `config:cache`, `optimize` and `corex:config:validate` are exempt). In
`production`, an enabled dangerous mode (`corex-workflow.enforce_declarations=false`,
`accept_legacy_unstamped=true` of notifications, tasks-workflow or any wakeup handler) stops the
process unless its exact config path is listed in `corex.acknowledged_dangerous_modes`; patterns
are rejected. `corex:config:validate` runs the same checks (`--production` evaluates them as
production), prints acknowledged modes as warnings and the `corex-operations.require_physical_tenancy=false`
opt-out as a notice, and exits non-zero on a violation.

Consumer tests can create messaging adapters through
`CoreX\Messaging\Testing\MessagingFixtures`; the factory returns the public messaging contracts.

The merged module subsystem is documented in [modules/README](docs/modules/README.md) and [modules/PACKAGE](docs/modules/PACKAGE.md). Its original changelog is retained in [history](docs/history/corex-modules-CHANGELOG.md).

## JSON в публичных DTO

Произвольные JSON-данные передаются как неизменяемый `CoreX\Data\Data\JsonDocument`.
Интеграционный адаптер `CoreX\Data\Laravel\JsonDocuments` проверяет входное дерево
и сохраняет различие целых и дробных чисел. Объекты, ресурсы и бесконечные числа
не принимаются. Сохранённый JSON и HTTP-представление продолжают содержать обычные
JSON-значения.

```php
use CoreX\Data\Laravel\JsonDocuments;

$document = JsonDocuments::capture(['enabled' => true, 'weight' => 1.5]);
$value = JsonDocuments::map($document);
```

`SettingsRepository::get()`/`FeatureFlags::value()` возвращают JSON-документ;
`SettingsRepository::set()` принимает его. `CoreOptions` — неизменяемый снимок
текущей конфигурации: новое разрешение из контейнера видит её актуальное состояние.

## Typed errors

Package error identities use backed enums and a boot compiled kernel registry.
Error constructors require `ErrorCode`; descriptors keep scoped string wire codes.
See the monorepo [error contract](../../docs/reference/errors.md) for contributions,
validated replay and safe mapping.


WHOLE PRODUCT FILE M packages/core/config/corex.php
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

use CoreX\Audit\Internal\NullAuditRetention;
use CoreX\Audit\Internal\QueuedAuditLogger;
use CoreX\Context\Internal\NullImpersonationService;
use CoreX\Context\Internal\NullTenancyManager;
use CoreX\Context\Internal\SingleAccountResolver;
use CoreX\Data\Internal\DenyDataScopeAuthorizer;
use CoreX\Departments\Internal\DatabaseDepartmentRepository;
use CoreX\Features\Internal\DatabaseFeatureFlags;
use CoreX\Messaging\Enums\PubSubDriver;
use CoreX\Settings\Internal\DatabaseSettingsRepository;
use CoreX\Settings\Internal\NullSettingDefaultsProvider;

return [

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | Tenant context (B-10 §3.1 / B-11 §3.1)
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | TenantContextResolver binding. Boxed installs default to
    | SingleAccountResolver (constant context, no real tenancy). Real
    | resolvers (host/root, axiomasoft/corex-tenancy) land in P2.
    |
    | `manager` (P2.5): TenancyManager binding — single switch point for
    | connection/cache/queue/fs. Boxed default is a null-manager (no-op,
    | constant context via `resolver` above); axiomasoft/corex-tenancy rebinds this to
    | DatabaseTenancyManager when the package is present (D10 — container
    | binding, never an `if(cloud)` branch).
    |
    | `impersonation` (P2.11, D139): ImpersonationService binding — boxed
    | default has no grants at all (B-11 §6.1); axiomasoft/corex-tenancy rebinds this to
    | DatabaseImpersonationService under `tenancy.oidc.impersonation.enabled`.
    */
    'tenant_context' => [
        'resolver' => SingleAccountResolver::class,
        'manager' => NullTenancyManager::class,
        'impersonation' => NullImpersonationService::class,
    ],

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | Bindings
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | Swap any core contract implementation here.
    */
    'bindings' => [
        'settings' => DatabaseSettingsRepository::class,
        'audit_logger' => QueuedAuditLogger::class,
        'audit_retention' => NullAuditRetention::class,
        'setting_defaults' => NullSettingDefaultsProvider::class,
        'feature_flags' => DatabaseFeatureFlags::class,
        'departments' => DatabaseDepartmentRepository::class,
        'data_scope_authorizer' => DenyDataScopeAuthorizer::class,
    ],

    'storage' => [
        'durable_root' => storage_path('app/corex/durable'),
        'ephemeral_root' => storage_path('app/corex/ephemeral'),
    ],

    'views' => [
        'connection' => null,
    ],

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | Audit log
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    */
    'audit' => [
        'enabled' => true,
    ],

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | Settings cache
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    */
    'settings' => [
        'cache_store' => 'array',
        'cache_ttl' => 3600,
        'cache_prefix' => 'corex.settings',
    ],

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | PubSub (B-10 §3.1/§6.1/§7.3 — no LISTEN/NOTIFY, R-15)
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | 'null' is the boxed-safe default (no infra dependency). 'redis' is the
    | cloud default (B-10 §6.1); 'database' is the boxed fallback when Redis
    | is unavailable.
    |
    | `octane_worker_opt_in` (D44/A29): a dedicated worker command sets this
    | to true before calling subscribe() under Octane — runningInConsole()
    | alone cannot tell an HTTP request from a worker command apart there.
    | Never set it from request-handling code.
    |
    | `retention`: corex:messaging:prune-pubsub's default --older-than window for
    | sys_pubsub_messages. Schedule it (e.g. daily, php artisan schedule)
    | longer than the worst-case subscriber downtime — pruning a message
    | before every durable cursor behind it has passed it is a silent,
    | permanent invalidation loss (D44).
    */
    'pubsub' => [
        'driver' => PubSubDriver::Null,
        'poll_interval_microseconds' => 50_000,
        'octane_worker_opt_in' => false,
        'retention' => '30 days',
    ],

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | Messaging (MSG-001 … MSG-008)
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | Transactional outbox и inbox идемпотентного потребителя.
    |
    | `lease_seconds` обязан превышать максимальную ожидаемую длительность
    | публикации: истёкшая аренда означает перезахват, то есть дубль. `batch_size`
    | ограничивает один ход relay — это же ограничение не даёт одному аккаунту
    | занять воркер целиком.
    |
    | `max_attempts` и `backoff_cap_seconds` задают ограниченный экспоненциальный
    | backoff min(cap, 2 ** attempt) секунд — та же формула, что у
    | packages/wakeup (SweepRunner). Jitter не добавляется: детерминизм важнее,
    | пока на один аккаунт приходится один relay (research/P5-design.md §5.8).
    |
    | `outbox_retention` — окно хранения ОПУБЛИКОВАННЫХ строк. Мёртвые строки
    | хранятся отдельным, более длинным окном `dead_retention`: они нужны
    | расследованию, а не доставке.
    |
    | `inbox_retention` по умолчанию null — inbox НЕ чистится. Удаление отметки
    | заново открывает окно дублей, поэтому включать чистку можно только там, где
    | объявлен конечный горизонт повтора (research/P5-design.md §7.3, D29).
    */
    'messaging' => [
        'lease_seconds' => 120,
        'batch_size' => 100,
        'max_attempts' => 8,
        'backoff_cap_seconds' => 300,
        'outbox_retention' => '14 days',
        'dead_retention' => '90 days',
        'inbox_retention' => null,
    ],

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | Acknowledged dangerous modes
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | Exact config paths of dangerous package modes the operator accepts in
    | `production` (for example `corex-workflow.enforce_declarations`). An
    | enabled dangerous mode that is not listed here blocks process start;
    | patterns and "acknowledge everything" are not supported.
    */
    'acknowledged_dangerous_modes' => [],

    'modules' => [
        /*
        |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
        | Module-system database connection
        |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
        | Connection the module hot-path (mod_modules / mod_records /
        | mod_lifecycle_log) and the registry epoch read from. `null` = the app's
        | default connection — correct for boxed / single-DB P1 installs, and
        | correct under P2 too (TenancyManager rebinds the default connection to the
        | tenant's database per request). The DB-per-account map that lets a single
        | reader serve several accounts from one process arrives with axiomasoft/corex-tenancy
        | (D13) — see phases/P1.md P1.16 Pending Work.
        */
        'connection' => env('COREX_MODULES_CONNECTION'),

        /*
        |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
        | Registry cache
        |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
        | The per-account enabled-set is cached WITHOUT cache tags (D30/A1/A2):
        | file/database stores are not taggable, so `tags()` would throw
        | BadMethodCallException on the default Laravel cache and in the boxed
        | profile (B-10 §6.1). Invalidation is by epoch (D39): a monotonic
        | registry version — the count of applied lifecycle transitions
        | (mod_lifecycle_log), bumped inside the lifecycle transaction — is stamped
        | into the cache key, so a stale key is simply never read.
        |
        | 'store' = null uses the app's default store.
        */
        'cache' => [
            'store' => env('COREX_MODULES_CACHE_STORE'),
            'ttl' => (int) env('COREX_MODULES_CACHE_TTL', 3600),
            'prefix' => (string) env('COREX_MODULES_CACHE_PREFIX', 'corex.modules'),
        ],
    ],

];

WHOLE PRODUCT FILE M packages/core/docs/modules/README.md
# axiomasoft/corex

Модульная система CoreX (namespace `CoreX\Modules\`): манифест модуля, discovery,
`RegistryCompiler` + extenders, `ModuleRegistry` (compiled hot-path),
lifecycle-FSM (`enable`/`disable`/`upgrade`/`archive`/`purge`) и реестр владения
для безопасного purge. Ставится поверх `axiomasoft/corex`. MIT.

## Требование к СУБД

Модульная система **требует PostgreSQL 16**: `mod_*`-таблицы и lifecycle-FSM
используют `pg_advisory_xact_lock`/`CHECK`/`jsonb`. SQLite-лейн покрывает
только pure-PHP код; lifecycle/purge/кеш-инвалидация проверяются
исключительно на `composer test:pg`.

## Установка схемы

Таблицы `mod_modules`, `mod_records`, `mod_lifecycle_log` принадлежат tenant-набору ядра:

```bash
php artisan corex:schema:migrate --scope=tenant --database=<tenant>
```

Соединение lifecycle задаётся `corex.modules.connection` (`null` = default).
Пути Module-наборов объявляют пакеты через `SchemaSetProvider`; lifecycle берёт их из `SchemaSets`.
Версия набора — `SchemaSet::version`; новые файлы исполняются командой схемы без копирования в приложение.

## Идентификаторы и время

Ключевые колонки (`mod_modules.id`, `mod_lifecycle_log.id`/`actor_id`) используют UUID.
Модели генерируют UUIDv7 через Laravel `HasUuids`. Все `timestamptz` — с явной микросекундной точностью
(`precision: 6`). Порядок purge в `mod_records` детерминирован монотонным
`seq bigserial` (не часами) — FK-безопасный обратный порядок удаления.

## Tool declarations (P8)

Modules may expose headless tools through the existing `Api` extender: `Api::tool()` for
standalone handlers and `Api::exposeTool()` to project an already-declared operation. Tool
declarations compile into the same API slice as HTTP operations; they do not create routes or
implicit CRUD. Canonical tuple, wire name, contract hash and schema validation are enforced at
compile time; runtime catalogue and guarded invocation live in optional `axiomasoft/corex-operations`.

## Registry introspection

`RegistryIntrospection` exposes a read-only map of the eight compiled extenders, entity/field
ownership and capability traits, plus a bounded `why()` explanation for module activation.
Diagnostics redact handler classes, connection topology and other confidential internals.
The local operator CLI is:

```bash
php artisan corex:modules:why example/demo
php artisan corex:modules:why example/demo --account=<trusted-account-id> --json
```

Without `--account`, output is declaration-only and does not claim a tenant activation verdict.
Tested installed compatibility passports remain a consumer/P9 obligation.

## Compatibility evidence

The factual compatibility passport, its local proof class, and explicit limits
are in [PACKAGE.md](PACKAGE.md). It does not extend Composer constraints or
constitute a publication promise.

## Failure semantics

Invalid or conflicting module declarations fail compilation before publishing a registry. A disabled module is not an implicit grant. Lifecycle and purge require the supported tenant PostgreSQL connection and package-owned schema; connection, dependency or ownership failures must be resolved before retry. Preserve transaction boundaries and do not replace a rejected lifecycle operation with direct table writes.

### Optional compiler contributions

Resolve `RegistryCompiler` through the container so installed contributors are included.
A package may register its Eloquent cast with `FieldCastRegistry::register($castClass, $type)`;
cast arguments such as `:USD` are ignored during lookup. The built-in scalar mappings still work
without optional packages.

Packages tag their `ManifestSectionCompiler` implementation with the interface name. Each
compiler owns one API declaration mode, its validation phase and normalized records. Operations
runs before tools, allowing exposed tools to resolve declared operations. An unsupported mode
fails compilation instead of silently omitting declarations.

WHOLE PRODUCT FILE M packages/core/src/Audit/Internal/AuditLog.php
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

namespace CoreX\Audit\Internal;

use CoreX\Audit\Enums\ActorType;
use Illuminate\Database\Eloquent\Concerns\HasUuids;
use Illuminate\Database\Eloquent\Model;
use Illuminate\Support\Carbon;

/**
 * Append-only audit entry (sys_audit_log). No updated_at:
 * DB_SCHEMA.md §Конфликт-2 (resolved 2026-07-13) — an append-only class
 * keeps updated_at only if it has ≥1 legitimate post-insert field; this
 * table has none.
 *
 * @property string $id
 * @property Carbon $occurred_at
 * @property ActorType $actor_type
 * @property string|null $actor_id
 * @property string|null $workspace_id
 * @property string|null $entity_type
 * @property string|null $entity_id
 * @property string $action
 * @property array<string, mixed>|null $changes
 * @property array<string, mixed> $context
 *
 * @internal spec: B-10 §2
 */
final class AuditLog extends Model
{
    use HasUuids;

    public const UPDATED_AT = null;

    protected $table = 'sys_audit_log';

    protected $guarded = [];

    /** @return array<string, string> */
    protected function casts(): array
    {
        return [
            'occurred_at' => 'immutable_datetime',
            'actor_type' => ActorType::class,
            'changes' => 'array',
            'context' => 'array',
        ];
    }
}


WHOLE PRODUCT FILE M packages/core/src/Audit/Internal/DatabaseSynchronousAuditWriter.php
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

namespace CoreX\Audit\Internal;

use CoreX\Audit\Contracts\SynchronousAuditWriter;
use CoreX\Audit\Data\AuditEntry;
use CoreX\Data\Laravel\JsonDocuments;
use Illuminate\Database\ConnectionResolverInterface;
use RuntimeException;

/** @internal */
final readonly class DatabaseSynchronousAuditWriter implements SynchronousAuditWriter
{
    public function __construct(private ConnectionResolverInterface $connections) {}

    public function record(AuditEntry $entry, string $connectionName): void
    {
        $connection = $this->connections->connection($connectionName);

        if ($connection->transactionLevel() !== 0 || $entry->occurredAt === null) {
            throw new RuntimeException('Synchronous audit requires a snapshot and no ambient transaction');
        }

        $connection->transaction(static function () use ($entry, $connectionName): void {
            $row = new AuditLog([
                'occurred_at' => $entry->occurredAt,
                'actor_type' => $entry->actorType,
                'actor_id' => $entry->actorId,
                'workspace_id' => $entry->workspaceId,
                'entity_type' => $entry->entityType,
                'entity_id' => $entry->entityId,
                'action' => $entry->action,
                'changes' => JsonDocuments::optionalMap($entry->changes),
                'context' => JsonDocuments::map($entry->context),
            ]);
            $row->setConnection($connectionName);

            if (! $row->saveQuietly()) {
                throw new RuntimeException('Synchronous audit insert failed');
            }
        });
    }
}

use CoreX\Settings\Internal\DatabaseSettingsRepository;
use CoreX\Settings\Internal\NullSettingDefaultsProvider;

/** Parses the host Laravel configuration and validates module bindings. */
#[Spi(kind: SpiKind::Call, since: '1.0')]
final class CoreOptionsParser
{
    /** @deprecated Resolve `CoreOptions` from the container; it is a shared lazy singleton. */
    public static function current(): CoreOptions
    {
        return app(CoreOptions::class);
    }

    /** @param  array<array-key, mixed>  $config */
    public static function fromArray(array $config): CoreOptions
    {
        $violations = new ConfigurationValidator;
        $tenant = is_array($config['tenant_context'] ?? null) ? $config['tenant_context'] : [];
        $bindings = is_array($config['bindings'] ?? null) ? $config['bindings'] : [];
        $storage = is_array($config['storage'] ?? null) ? $config['storage'] : [];
        $views = is_array($config['views'] ?? null) ? $config['views'] : [];
        $audit = is_array($config['audit'] ?? null) ? $config['audit'] : [];
        $settings = is_array($config['settings'] ?? null) ? $config['settings'] : [];
        $pubsub = is_array($config['pubsub'] ?? null) ? $config['pubsub'] : [];
        $messaging = is_array($config['messaging'] ?? null) ? $config['messaging'] : [];

        $tenantContextResolver = self::classString($violations, 'tenant_context.resolver', $tenant['resolver'] ?? SingleAccountResolver::class, TenantContextResolver::class);
        $tenancyManager = self::classString($violations, 'tenant_context.manager', $tenant['manager'] ?? NullTenancyManager::class, TenancyManager::class);
        $impersonationService = self::classString($violations, 'tenant_context.impersonation', $tenant['impersonation'] ?? NullImpersonationService::class, ImpersonationService::class);
        $settingsRepository = self::classString($violations, 'bindings.settings', $bindings['settings'] ?? DatabaseSettingsRepository::class, SettingsRepository::class);
        $auditLogger = self::classString($violations, 'bindings.audit_logger', $bindings['audit_logger'] ?? QueuedAuditLogger::class, AuditLogger::class);
        $auditRetention = self::classString($violations, 'bindings.audit_retention', $bindings['audit_retention'] ?? NullAuditRetention::class, AuditRetention::class);
        $settingDefaultsProvider = self::classString($violations, 'bindings.setting_defaults', $bindings['setting_defaults'] ?? NullSettingDefaultsProvider::class, SettingDefaultsProvider::class);
        $featureFlags = self::classString($violations, 'bindings.feature_flags', $bindings['feature_flags'] ?? DatabaseFeatureFlags::class, FeatureFlags::class);
        $departmentRepository = self::classString($violations, 'bindings.departments', $bindings['departments'] ?? DatabaseDepartmentRepository::class, DepartmentRepository::class);
        $dataScopeAuthorizer = self::classString($violations, 'bindings.data_scope_authorizer', $bindings['data_scope_authorizer'] ?? DenyDataScopeAuthorizer::class, DataScopeAuthorizer::class);

        $durable = self::nonEmpty($violations, 'storage.durable_root', $storage['durable_root'] ?? 'storage/app/corex/durable');
        $ephemeral = self::nonEmpty($violations, 'storage.ephemeral_root', $storage['ephemeral_root'] ?? 'storage/app/corex/ephemeral');
        $connection = $views['connection'] ?? null;
        $viewsConnection = is_string($connection) && $connection !== '' ? $connection : null;
        $auditEnabled = self::bool($violations, 'audit.enabled', $audit['enabled'] ?? true);
        $cacheStore = self::nonEmpty($violations, 'settings.cache_store', $settings['cache_store'] ?? 'array');
        $cacheTtl = self::nullableNonNegativeInt(
            $violations,
            'settings.cache_ttl',
            array_key_exists('cache_ttl', $settings) ? $settings['cache_ttl'] : 3600,
        );
        $cachePrefix = self::nonEmpty($violations, 'settings.cache_prefix', $settings['cache_prefix'] ?? 'corex.settings');
        $pubsubDriver = self::enum($violations, 'pubsub.driver', $pubsub['driver'] ?? PubSubDriver::Null, PubSubDriver::class);
        $poll = self::positiveInt($violations, 'pubsub.poll_interval_microseconds', $pubsub['poll_interval_microseconds'] ?? 50_000);
        $optIn = self::bool($violations, 'pubsub.octane_worker_opt_in', $pubsub['octane_worker_opt_in'] ?? false);
        $retention = self::interval($violations, 'pubsub.retention', $pubsub['retention'] ?? '30 days');
        $lease = self::positiveInt($violations, 'messaging.lease_seconds', $messaging['lease_seconds'] ?? 120);
        $batch = self::positiveInt($violations, 'messaging.batch_size', $messaging['batch_size'] ?? 100);
        $attempts = self::positiveInt($violations, 'messaging.max_attempts', $messaging['max_attempts'] ?? 8);
        $backoff = self::positiveInt($violations, 'messaging.backoff_cap_seconds', $messaging['backoff_cap_seconds'] ?? 300);
        $outboxRetention = self::interval($violations, 'messaging.outbox_retention', $messaging['outbox_retention'] ?? '14 days');
        $deadRetention = self::interval($violations, 'messaging.dead_retention', $messaging['dead_retention'] ?? '90 days');
        $inboxRetention = array_key_exists('inbox_retention', $messaging) && $messaging['inbox_retention'] !== null
            ? self::interval($violations, 'messaging.inbox_retention', $messaging['inbox_retention'])
            : null;
        $acknowledged = self::acknowledgedDangerousModes($violations, $config['acknowledged_dangerous_modes'] ?? []);

        $violations->failIfAny();

        return new CoreOptions(
            tenantContextResolver: $tenantContextResolver,
            tenancyManager: $tenancyManager,
            impersonationService: $impersonationService,
            settingsRepository: $settingsRepository,
            auditLogger: $auditLogger,
            auditRetention: $auditRetention,
            settingDefaultsProvider: $settingDefaultsProvider,
            featureFlags: $featureFlags,
            departmentRepository: $departmentRepository,
            dataScopeAuthorizer: $dataScopeAuthorizer,
            storageDurableRoot: $durable,
            storageEphemeralRoot: $ephemeral,
            viewsConnection: $viewsConnection,
            auditEnabled: $auditEnabled,
            settingsCacheStore: $cacheStore,
            settingsCacheTtl: $cacheTtl,
            settingsCachePrefix: $cachePrefix,
            pubsubDriver: $pubsubDriver,
            pubsubPollIntervalMicroseconds: $poll,
            pubsubOctaneWorkerOptIn: $optIn,
            pubsubRetention: $retention,
            messagingLeaseSeconds: $lease,
            messagingBatchSize: $batch,
            messagingMaxAttempts: $attempts,
            messagingBackoffCapSeconds: $backoff,
            messagingOutboxRetention: $outboxRetention,
            messagingDeadRetention: $deadRetention,
            messagingInboxRetention: $inboxRetention,
            acknowledgedDangerousModes: $acknowledged,
        );
    }

    /**
     * @template T of BackedEnum
     *
     * @param  class-string<T>  $enumClass
     * @return T
     */
    private static function enum(ConfigurationValidator $violations, string $path, mixed $value, string $enumClass): BackedEnum
    {
        if ($value instanceof $enumClass) {
            return $value;
        }

        if ((is_string($value) || is_int($value)) && is_subclass_of($enumClass, BackedEnum::class)) {
            $resolved = $enumClass::tryFrom($value);

            if ($resolved instanceof $enumClass) {
                return $resolved;
            }
        }

        $violations->add($path, 'must be a '.$enumClass.' value');

        return $enumClass::cases()[0];
    }

    /**
     * @param  class-string  $contract
     * @return class-string
     */
    private static function classString(ConfigurationValidator $violations, string $path, mixed $value, string $contract): string
    {
        if (is_string($value) && $value !== '' && class_exists($value) && is_a($value, $contract, true)) {
            return $value;
        }

        $violations->add($path, 'must be a class implementing '.$contract);

        return $contract;
    }

    /** @return list<string> */
    private static function acknowledgedDangerousModes(ConfigurationValidator $violations, mixed $value): array
    {
        if (! is_array($value) || ! array_is_list($value)) {
            $violations->add('acknowledged_dangerous_modes', 'must be a list of exact config paths');

            return [];
        }

        $paths = [];
        foreach ($value as $index => $path) {
            if (! is_string($path) || $path === '' || str_contains($path, '*')) {
                $violations->add('acknowledged_dangerous_modes.'.$index, 'must be an exact config path without wildcards');

                continue;
            }

            $paths[] = $path;
        }

        return $paths;
    }

    private static function bool(ConfigurationValidator $violations, string $path, mixed $value): bool
    {
        if (is_bool($value)) {
            return $value;
        }

        $violations->add($path, 'must be a boolean');

        return false;
    }

    private static function positiveInt(ConfigurationValidator $violations, string $path, mixed $value): int
    {
        if (is_int($value) && $value > 0) {
            return $value;
        }

        $violations->add($path, 'must be a positive integer');

        return 1;
    }

    private static function nullableNonNegativeInt(ConfigurationValidator $violations, string $path, mixed $value): ?int
    {
        if ($value === null) {
            return null;
        }

        if (is_int($value) && $value >= 0) {
            return $value;
        }

        $violations->add($path, 'must be null or a non-negative integer');

        return null;
    }

    private static function nonEmpty(ConfigurationValidator $violations, string $path, mixed $value): string
    {
        if (is_string($value) && $value !== '') {
            return $value;
        }

        $violations->add($path, 'must be a non-empty string');

        return 'invalid';
    }

    private static function interval(ConfigurationValidator $violations, string $path, mixed $value): string
    {
        if (is_string($value) && $value !== '' && self::parsesAsInterval($value)) {
            return $value;
        }

        $violations->add($path, 'must be a DateInterval string');

        return '30 days';
    }

    private static function parsesAsInterval(string $value): bool
    {
        try {
            return self::intervalHasDuration(new DateInterval($value));
        } catch (Throwable) {
            // Relative strings such as "30 days" are not ISO-8601 intervals.
        }

        try {
            $relative = DateInterval::createFromDateString($value);
        } catch (Throwable) {
            return false;
        }

        return self::intervalHasDuration($relative);
    }

    private static function intervalHasDuration(DateInterval $interval): bool
    {
        return $interval->y !== 0
            || $interval->m !== 0
            || $interval->d !== 0
            || $interval->h !== 0
            || $interval->i !== 0
            || $interval->s !== 0
            || $interval->f !== 0.0;
    }
}

#[Api(since: '1.0')]
enum IdStrategy: string
{
    case Ulid = 'ulid';

WHOLE PRODUCT FILE D packages/core/src/Database/Laravel/HasConfiguredId.php
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

namespace CoreX\Database\Laravel;

use CoreX\Attributes\Spi;
use CoreX\Attributes\SpiKind;
use CoreX\Config\Data\CoreOptions;
use CoreX\Database\Enums\IdStrategy;
use Illuminate\Database\Eloquent\Model;
use Illuminate\Support\Str;

/**
 * Config-driven primary key: UUIDv7 (default, per ADR-003), UUID, ULID, or
 * auto-increment int — per config('corex.ids.strategy'). A single seam so a
 * host app can line CoreX models up with its own key type without forking
 * the package (mirrors AzGuard's configurable morph key). Pair with
 * CoreX\Database\Laravel\IdColumns in migrations so the column type matches.
 */
#[Spi(kind: SpiKind::Call, since: '1.0')]
trait HasConfiguredId
{
    public static function bootHasConfiguredId(): void
    {
        static::creating(function (Model $model): void {
            if (app(CoreOptions::class)->idStrategy === IdStrategy::Int) {
                return;
            }

            foreach ($model->uniqueIds() as $column) {
                if (empty($model->getAttribute($column))) {
                    $model->setAttribute($column, $model->newUniqueId());
                }
            }
        });
    }

    /**
     * Columns that receive a generated id. Empty for the int strategy
     * (the database auto-increments).
     *
     * @return list<string>
     */
    public function uniqueIds(): array
    {
        return app(CoreOptions::class)->idStrategy === IdStrategy::Int ? [] : [$this->getKeyName()];
    }

    public function newUniqueId(): string
    {
        return match (app(CoreOptions::class)->idStrategy) {
            IdStrategy::Uuid7 => (string) Str::uuid7(),
            IdStrategy::Uuid => (string) Str::orderedUuid(),
            default => (string) Str::ulid(),
        };
    }

    public function getKeyType(): string
    {
        return app(CoreOptions::class)->idStrategy === IdStrategy::Int ? 'int' : 'string';
    }

    public function getIncrementing(): bool
    {
        return app(CoreOptions::class)->idStrategy === IdStrategy::Int;
    }
}


WHOLE PRODUCT FILE D packages/core/src/Database/Laravel/IdColumns.php
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

namespace CoreX\Database\Laravel;

use CoreX\Attributes\Spi;
use CoreX\Attributes\SpiKind;
use CoreX\Config\Data\CoreOptions;
use CoreX\Database\Enums\IdStrategy;
use Illuminate\Database\Schema\Blueprint;
use Illuminate\Database\Schema\ColumnDefinition;
use Illuminate\Database\Schema\ForeignIdColumnDefinition;

/**
 * Key columns whose type follows config('corex.ids.strategy'): UUIDv7
 * (default), UUID, ULID, or auto-increment int. Lets a host app line
 * CoreX tables up with its own key type without forking the package
 * migrations (mirrors AzGuard's MorphColumns). Pair with
 * CoreX\Database\Laravel\HasConfiguredId on the models.
 */
#[Spi(kind: SpiKind::Call, since: '1.0')]
final class IdColumns
{
    /**
     * Primary key column.
     */
    public static function primary(Blueprint $table, string $name = 'id'): void
    {
        match (app(CoreOptions::class)->idStrategy) {
            IdStrategy::Int => $table->id($name),
            IdStrategy::Uuid, IdStrategy::Uuid7 => $table->uuid($name)->primary(),
            IdStrategy::Ulid => $table->ulid($name)->primary(),
        };
    }

    /**
     * A loose reference column (no DB foreign-key constraint), e.g. tenant_id,
     * owner_id. Chain ->nullable()->index() at the call site as needed.
     */
    public static function reference(Blueprint $table, string $name): ColumnDefinition
    {
        return match (app(CoreOptions::class)->idStrategy) {
            IdStrategy::Int => $table->unsignedBigInteger($name),
            IdStrategy::Uuid, IdStrategy::Uuid7 => $table->uuid($name),
            IdStrategy::Ulid => $table->ulid($name),
        };
    }

    /**
     * A constrained foreign key. Chain ->constrained(...)->cascadeOnDelete()
     * (and ->nullable()) at the call site.
     */
    public static function foreign(Blueprint $table, string $name): ForeignIdColumnDefinition
    {
        return match (app(CoreOptions::class)->idStrategy) {
            IdStrategy::Int => $table->foreignId($name),
            IdStrategy::Uuid, IdStrategy::Uuid7 => $table->foreignUuid($name),
            IdStrategy::Ulid => $table->foreignUlid($name),
        };
    }
}

trait ResolvesCoreOptions

}


WHOLE PRODUCT FILE M packages/core/src/Departments/Internal/DatabaseDepartmentRepository.php
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

namespace CoreX\Departments\Internal;

use CoreX\Departments\Contracts\DepartmentRepository;
use CoreX\Departments\Data\Department;
use CoreX\Departments\Events\DepartmentMoved;
use CoreX\Departments\Exceptions\CrossWorkspaceMoveException;
use CoreX\Departments\Exceptions\DepartmentCycleException;
use Illuminate\Contracts\Events\Dispatcher;
use Illuminate\Database\Connection;
use Illuminate\Database\ConnectionResolverInterface;
use Illuminate\Database\Eloquent\ModelNotFoundException;
use Override;
use RuntimeException;

/**
 * `sys_departments`/`sys_department_user` on ltree.
 *
 * `$connection` mirrors DatabaseSettingsRepository's/DatabaseFeatureFlags'
 * seam (null = app default), used by the PG-only test lane — ltree/GIST
 * have no sqlite equivalent.
 *
 * @internal spec: B-10 §2/§3.1/§5.7, D12
 */
final class DatabaseDepartmentRepository implements DepartmentRepository
{
    public function __construct(
        private readonly ConnectionResolverInterface $connections,
        private readonly Dispatcher $events,
        private readonly ?string $connection = null,
    ) {}

    #[Override]
    public function find(string $id): ?Department
    {
        $row = Models\Department::on($this->connection)->where('id', $id)->first();

        return $row instanceof Models\Department ? $this->toDto($row) : null;
    }

    #[Override]
    public function subtree(string $departmentId): array
    {
        $root = Models\Department::on($this->connection)->where('id', $departmentId)->first();

        if (! $root instanceof Models\Department) {
            return [];
        }

        /** @var list<Models\Department> $rows */
        $rows = Models\Department::on($this->connection)
            ->where('workspace_id', $root->workspace_id)
            ->whereRaw('path <@ ?::ltree', [$root->path])
            ->orderBy('path')
            ->get()
            ->all();

        return array_map($this->toDto(...), $rows);
    }

    #[Override]
    public function primaryOf(string $userId): ?Department
    {
        $departmentId = $this->db()->table('sys_department_user')
            ->where('user_id', $userId)
            ->where('is_primary', true)
            ->value('department_id');

        return is_string($departmentId) ? $this->find($departmentId) : null;
    }

    #[Override]
    public function move(string $departmentId, ?string $newParentId): void
    {
        $this->db()->transaction(function () use ($departmentId, $newParentId): void {
            // (1) Minimal read for the LOCK KEY only. workspace_id is immutable,
            //     so it can be read before the lock; the raw (soft-delete-blind)
            //     query still returns it for a row mid-deletion, so we always
            //     serialize on the right workspace. Absent row → explicit throw,
            //     never a silent no-op (A5 If-then).
            $lockWorkspaceId = $this->db()->table('sys_departments')
                ->where('id', $departmentId)
                ->value('workspace_id');

            if (! is_string($lockWorkspaceId)) {
                throw (new ModelNotFoundException)->setModel(Models\Department::class, [$departmentId]);
            }

            // (2) Advisory lock BEFORE reading the mutable path (A5). Xact-scoped,
            //     released on commit — no session-level lock.
            $this->db()->statement('select pg_advisory_xact_lock(hashtext(?))', ['sys_departments:'.$lockWorkspaceId]);

            // (3) RE-READ the moving row UNDER the lock — fresh path/parent_id.
            //     Eloquent (SoftDeletes) excludes a concurrently soft-deleted row,
            //     so firstOrFail() throws instead of running a no-op UPDATE that
            //     matches nothing (A5/A24 If-then). All downstream maths use THIS
            //     row, never the pre-lock read.
            /** @var Models\Department $moving */
            $moving = Models\Department::on($this->connection)->where('id', $departmentId)->firstOrFail();

            // The lock key was read before the lock; workspace_id is immutable,
            // but if it changed under us a concurrent writer is doing something
            // the contract forbids — fail loudly rather than lock the wrong key.
            if ($moving->workspace_id !== $lockWorkspaceId) {
                throw new RuntimeException("Department {$departmentId} changed workspace concurrently during move().");
            }

            $newParentPath = '';

            if ($newParentId !== null) {
                /** @var Models\Department $newParent */
                $newParent = Models\Department::on($this->connection)->where('id', $newParentId)->firstOrFail();

                // Parent-side workspace guard, symmetric to the descendant-side
                // filter on the UPDATE below: the new parent
                // is read UNDER the already-held advisory lock and must share the
                // moving row's workspace. ltree paths are globally unique, so a
                // cross-workspace parent slips past the cycle check and the FK
                // layer — reparenting into another tenant's subtree would be a
                // silent cross-tenant integrity breach, not a legal move.
                if ($newParent->workspace_id !== $lockWorkspaceId) {
                    throw new CrossWorkspaceMoveException($departmentId, $newParentId);
                }

                if ($newParent->path === $moving->path || str_starts_with($newParent->path, $moving->path.'.')) {
                    throw new DepartmentCycleException($departmentId, $newParentId);
                }

                $newParentPath = $newParent->path;
            }

            $oldPath = $moving->path;
            $ownLabel = str_contains($oldPath, '.') ? substr($oldPath, strrpos($oldPath, '.') + 1) : $oldPath;
            $newPath = $newParentPath !== '' ? $newParentPath.'.'.$ownLabel : $ownLabel;

            // ONE UPDATE over the whole subtree (`path <@`) — no recursive
            // per-row traversal: strip the old-parent prefix
            // (nlevel(old moving path) - 1 labels) off every row under the
            // moving path and re-prepend the new parent's path.
            //
            // Scoped to the moving row's workspace: ltree paths are globally
            // unique today, but the contract is per-workspace and
            // subtree()/primaryOf() already filter
            // on workspace_id — a same-shaped path in ANOTHER workspace under
            // `path <@` must never be rewritten by this workspace's move().
            $this->db()->statement(
                'update sys_departments set
                    path = (?::ltree || subpath(path, nlevel(?::ltree) - 1)),
                    parent_id = case when id = ? then ? else parent_id end,
                    updated_at = now()
                where path <@ ?::ltree and workspace_id = ?',
                [$newParentPath, $oldPath, $departmentId, $newParentId, $oldPath, $lockWorkspaceId],
            );

            // A6: dispatch AFTER the transaction commits, not inside it — the
            // dept_tree cache invalidator (entity-scoped RBAC) must not
            // read uncommitted paths, and a rollback must not leak the event.
            $this->db()->afterCommit(fn () => $this->events->dispatch(
                new DepartmentMoved(departmentId: $departmentId, oldPath: $oldPath, newPath: $newPath),
            ));
        });
    }

    private function toDto(Models\Department $row): Department
    {
        return new Department(
            id: $row->id,
            workspaceId: $row->workspace_id,
            parentId: $row->parent_id,
            path: $row->path,
            name: $row->name,
            headUserId: $row->head_user_id,
            sortOrder: $row->sort_order,
        );
    }

    private function db(): Connection
    {
        /** @var Connection */
        $connection = $this->connections->connection($this->connection);

        return $connection;
    }
}


WHOLE PRODUCT FILE M packages/core/src/Departments/Internal/Models/Department.php
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

namespace CoreX\Departments\Internal\Models;

use Illuminate\Database\Eloquent\Concerns\HasUuids;
use Illuminate\Database\Eloquent\Model;
use Illuminate\Database\Eloquent\SoftDeletes;

/**
 * Department hierarchy row (`sys_departments`). `path` is ltree —
 * read/written as a plain string, PG casts on the wire.
 *
 * SoftDeletes (A24): a removed department must drop out of the scope that
 * feeds entity-scoped RBAC — the `deleted_at` column and the partial
 * index `WHERE deleted_at IS NULL` are in the migration for exactly this.
 * find()/subtree()/primaryOf() go through Eloquent and inherit the
 * SoftDeletingScope, so soft-deleted rows are excluded automatically.
 *
 * @property string $id
 * @property string $workspace_id
 * @property string|null $parent_id
 * @property string $path
 * @property string $name
 * @property string|null $head_user_id
 * @property int $sort_order
 *
 * @internal spec: B-10 §2, P2.9
 */
final class Department extends Model
{
    use HasUuids;
    use SoftDeletes;

    protected $table = 'sys_departments';

    protected $guarded = [];

    /**
     * @return array<string, string>
     */
    protected function casts(): array
    {
        // PG returns integers as strings over PDO; the DTO's `int $sortOrder`
        // is strict-typed, so cast here rather than at every read site.
        return ['sort_order' => 'integer'];
    }
}


WHOLE PRODUCT FILE M packages/core/src/Features/Internal/Models/FeatureFlag.php

WHOLE PRODUCT FILE M packages/core/src/Messaging/Internal/Commands/PruneOutboxMessages.php
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

namespace CoreX\Messaging\Internal\Commands;

use CoreX\Config\Data\CoreOptions;
use DateInterval;
use Illuminate\Console\Command;
use Illuminate\Database\ConnectionResolverInterface;
use Psr\Clock\ClockInterface;

/**
 * Удаляет терминальные строки sys_outbox_messages старше порога.
 *
 * Два окна, а не одно: опубликованные записи нужны сверке, мёртвые — расследованию,
 * и второе обычно длиннее. Строки в pending и publishing не удаляются никогда — это
 * недоставленная работа, а не история.
 *
 * sys_inbox_messages эта команда НЕ трогает: удаление отметки дедупликации заново
 * открывает окно дублей (research/P5-design.md §7.3, D29).
 *
 * @internal spec: P5.15 — console command is not a supported API
 */
final class PruneOutboxMessages extends Command
{
    protected $signature = 'corex:messaging:prune
        {--published-older-than= : DateInterval-parseable age for published rows (default: corex.messaging.outbox_retention)}
        {--dead-older-than= : DateInterval-parseable age for dead rows (default: corex.messaging.dead_retention)}
        {--database= : Connection to prune (default: the app default)}';

    protected $description = 'Delete terminal sys_outbox_messages rows older than their retention window.';

    public function handle(ClockInterface $clock, CoreOptions $options, ConnectionResolverInterface $connections): int
    {
        /** @var string|null $publishedOption */
        $publishedOption = $this->option('published-older-than');
        /** @var string|null $deadOption */
        $deadOption = $this->option('dead-older-than');
        /** @var string|null $connection */
        $connection = $this->option('database');

        $published = $publishedOption ?? $options->messagingOutboxRetention;
        $dead = $deadOption ?? $options->messagingDeadRetention;
        $now = $clock->now();
        $table = 'sys_outbox_messages';

        $prunedPublished = $connections->connection($connection)->table($table)
            ->where('status', 'published')
            ->where('published_at', '<', $now->sub(DateInterval::createFromDateString($published)))
            ->delete();

        $prunedDead = $connections->connection($connection)->table($table)
            ->where('status', 'dead')
            ->where('dead_at', '<', $now->sub(DateInterval::createFromDateString($dead)))
            ->delete();

        $this->info(sprintf(
            'Outbox retention: pruned %d published (older than %s) and %d dead (older than %s).',
            $prunedPublished,
            $published,
            $prunedDead,
            $dead,
        ));

        return self::SUCCESS;
    }
}


WHOLE PRODUCT FILE M packages/core/src/Messaging/Internal/DatabasePollPubSub.php
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

namespace CoreX\Messaging\Internal;

use Closure;
use CoreX\Config\Data\CoreOptions;
use CoreX\Messaging\Contracts\PubSub;
use CoreX\Messaging\Contracts\WorkerRuntime;
use Illuminate\Database\ConnectionInterface;
use Illuminate\Database\ConnectionResolverInterface;
use Override;
use Psr\Clock\ClockInterface;

/**
 * Boxed fallback driver for installs without Redis — polls
 * `sys_pubsub_messages` by an auto-increment cursor instead of `LISTEN`/
 * `NOTIFY` (forbidden under PgBouncer transaction pooling, R-15). `id` is a
 * plain bigint (not UUIDv7/ADR-003) — this table is an internal delivery
 * cursor, not a domain entity.
 *
 * DURABLE cursor: the poll position for a `($channel,
 * $subscriber)` pair is persisted in `sys_pubsub_cursors`, not restarted at 0
 * or at `max(id)` on every `subscribe()` call. A rolling-restart of the
 * subscriber process resumes exactly where the previous process left off —
 * this driver backs the RBAC/module-activity invalidation channel, and a
 * "start from max(id) on every subscribe()" design would silently DROP
 * every message published during the restart window (stale cache, not a
 * storm). The corresponding row in `sys_pubsub_cursors` is created only the
 * FIRST time a given `$subscriber` name is seen for a channel: at that point
 * there is no prior position to resume from, so it starts at the channel's
 * current `max(id)` (skips pre-existing backlog) unless `$replayBacklog` is
 * passed explicitly — never a default.
 *
 * `$connection` mirrors the other PG-only drivers' seam (null = app
 * default). `$maxPolls` is test-only: `null` polls forever (real usage), a
 * finite count lets tests observe delivery without blocking — it does NOT
 * bypass the worker-context guard (SubscribeGuard runs regardless).
 *
 * @internal spec: B-10 §6.1, D44, P1.24, Implementation Rules, pre-mortem C7
 */
final class DatabasePollPubSub implements PubSub
{
    public function __construct(
        private readonly ConnectionResolverInterface $connections,
        private readonly CoreOptions $options,
        private readonly ClockInterface $clock,
        private readonly WorkerRuntime $runtime,
        private readonly ?string $connection = null,
        private readonly ?int $pollIntervalMicroseconds = null,
    ) {}

    #[Override]
    public function publish(string $channel, array $payload): void
    {
        $connection = $this->connections->connection($this->connection);

        $connection->transaction(function () use ($channel, $payload, $connection): void {
            $connection->select(
                query: 'SELECT pg_advisory_xact_lock(hashtext(?))',
                bindings: ['corex-pubsub:'.$channel],
            );

            $connection->table('sys_pubsub_messages')->insert([
                'channel' => $channel,
                'payload' => json_encode($payload, JSON_THROW_ON_ERROR),
                'created_at' => $this->clock->now(),
            ]);
        });
    }

    /**
     * @param  Closure(array<string, mixed>): void  $handler
     * @param  int|null  $maxPolls  test-only: stop after N poll iterations instead of polling forever.
     * @param  string  $subscriber  durable-cursor identity — a distinct name per logical
     *                              subscriber lets several independent consumers each track their own
     *                              position on the same channel.
     * @param  bool  $replayBacklog  only meaningful the first time this `$subscriber` subscribes to
     *                               `$channel` (no persisted cursor yet): `true` starts at the beginning
     *                               of the channel's history instead of the current `max(id)`. Never
     *                               defaults to `true`.
     *
     * @internal spec: D44, Implementation Rules, P1.24
     */
    public function subscribe(
        string $channel,
        Closure $handler,
        ?int $maxPolls = null,
        string $subscriber = 'default',
        bool $replayBacklog = false,
    ): void {
        SubscribeGuard::assert($this->runtime, $this->options->pubsubOctaneWorkerOptIn, self::class);

        $connection = $this->connections->connection($this->connection);
        $messagesTable = 'sys_pubsub_messages';
        $cursorsTable = 'sys_pubsub_cursors';

        $cursor = $this->loadOrBootstrapCursor($connection, $cursorsTable, $messagesTable, $channel, $subscriber, $replayBacklog);
        $polls = 0;

        while ($maxPolls === null || $polls < $maxPolls) {
            $rows = $connection->table($messagesTable)
                ->where('channel', $channel)
                ->where('id', '>', $cursor)
                ->orderBy('id')
                ->get();

            foreach ($rows as $row) {
                $cursor = (int) $row->id;
                /** @var array<string, mixed> $payload */
                $payload = json_decode((string) $row->payload, true, flags: JSON_THROW_ON_ERROR);
                $handler($payload);

                $this->persistCursor($connection, $cursorsTable, $channel, $subscriber, $cursor);
            }

            $polls++;

            if ($maxPolls === null || $polls < $maxPolls) {
                usleep($this->pollIntervalMicroseconds ?? $this->options->pubsubPollIntervalMicroseconds);
            }
        }
    }

    private function loadOrBootstrapCursor(
        ConnectionInterface $connection,
        string $cursorsTable,
        string $messagesTable,
        string $channel,
        string $subscriber,
        bool $replayBacklog,
    ): int {
        $existing = $connection->table($cursorsTable)
            ->where('channel', $channel)
            ->where('subscriber', $subscriber)
            ->first();

        if ($existing !== null) {
            return (int) $existing->position;
        }

        $start = $replayBacklog
            ? 0
            : (int) ($connection->table($messagesTable)->where('channel', $channel)->max('id') ?? 0);

        $this->persistCursor($connection, $cursorsTable, $channel, $subscriber, $start);

        return $start;
    }

    private function persistCursor(
        ConnectionInterface $connection,
        string $cursorsTable,
        string $channel,
        string $subscriber,
        int $position,
    ): void {
        $connection->table($cursorsTable)->updateOrInsert(
            ['channel' => $channel, 'subscriber' => $subscriber],
            ['position' => $position, 'updated_at' => $this->clock->now()],
        );
    }
}


WHOLE PRODUCT FILE M packages/core/src/Messaging/Internal/PrunePubSubMessages.php
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

namespace CoreX\Messaging\Internal;

use CoreX\Config\Data\CoreOptions;
use DateInterval;
use Illuminate\Console\Command;
use Illuminate\Database\ConnectionResolverInterface;
use Psr\Clock\ClockInterface;

/**
 * Prunes `sys_pubsub_messages` rows older than a threshold — the
 * database-poll driver's message table has no built-in retention, so it
 * grows without bound unless something deletes old rows. Wire this into
 * the host's scheduler (e.g. `php artisan schedule` → daily) with
 * `--older-than` set LONGER than the worst-case downtime of any subscriber
 * reading this channel: a durable subscriber cursor (`sys_pubsub_cursors`)
 * can only resume correctly if the messages it hasn't consumed yet are
 * still in the table — pruning ahead of a stalled/down subscriber is a
 * silent, permanent invalidation loss, not just noise.
 *
 * Example scheduler wiring (host's `routes/console.php` or `AppServiceProvider`):
 * `Schedule::command('corex:messaging:prune-pubsub')->daily();`
 *
 * @internal spec: D44, P1.24, pre-mortem P1R C7
 */
final class PrunePubSubMessages extends Command
{
    protected $signature = 'corex:messaging:prune-pubsub
        {--older-than= : DateInterval-parseable age threshold, e.g. "30 days" (default: corex.pubsub.retention)}
        {--database= : Connection to prune (default: the app default)}';

    protected $description = 'Delete sys_pubsub_messages rows older than the retention threshold.';

    public function handle(ClockInterface $clock, CoreOptions $options, ConnectionResolverInterface $connections): int
    {
        /** @var string|null $olderThanOption */
        $olderThanOption = $this->option('older-than');
        $olderThan = $olderThanOption ?? $options->pubsubRetention;

        $cutoff = $clock->now()->sub(DateInterval::createFromDateString($olderThan));

        /** @var string|null $connection */
        $connection = $this->option('database');

        $deleted = $connections->connection($connection)->table('sys_pubsub_messages')
            ->where('created_at', '<', $cutoff)
            ->delete();

        $this->info(sprintf(
            'PubSub retention: pruned %d row(s) older than %s (threshold: %s).',
            $deleted,
            $cutoff->format(DATE_ATOM),
            $olderThan,
        ));

        return self::SUCCESS;
    }
}


WHOLE PRODUCT FILE M packages/core/src/Messaging/Laravel/MessagingRegistrar.php
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

namespace CoreX\Messaging\Laravel;

use CoreX\Attributes\Spi;
use CoreX\Attributes\SpiKind;
use CoreX\Config\Data\CoreOptions;
use CoreX\Config\Internal\ApplicationLocator;
use CoreX\Messaging\Contracts\CloudEventCodec;
use CoreX\Messaging\Contracts\Inbox;
use CoreX\Messaging\Contracts\MessageContractRegistry;
use CoreX\Messaging\Contracts\Outbox;
use CoreX\Messaging\Contracts\OutboxRelay;
use CoreX\Messaging\Contracts\PubSub;
use CoreX\Messaging\Contracts\TraceContextBridge;
use CoreX\Messaging\Contracts\WorkerRuntime;
use CoreX\Messaging\Enums\PubSubDriver;
use CoreX\Messaging\Internal\ArrayMessageContractRegistry;
use CoreX\Messaging\Internal\DatabasePollPubSub;
use CoreX\Messaging\Internal\DefaultWorkerRuntime;
use CoreX\Messaging\Internal\JsonCloudEventCodec;
use CoreX\Messaging\Internal\NullPubSub;
use CoreX\Messaging\Internal\NullTraceContextBridge;
use CoreX\Messaging\Internal\RedisPubSub;
use CoreX\Messaging\Internal\SqlDeliveryHealth;
use CoreX\Messaging\Internal\SqlInbox;
use CoreX\Messaging\Internal\SqlOutbox;
use CoreX\Messaging\Internal\SqlOutboxRelay;
use CoreX\Modules\Contracts\PackageRegistrar;
use Illuminate\Contracts\Foundation\Application;
use Illuminate\Database\Connection;
use Illuminate\Database\ConnectionInterface;
use Override;
use Psr\Clock\ClockInterface;

/** Composition of the kernel module; the public provider delegates here. */
#[Spi(kind: SpiKind::Call, since: '1.0')]
final class MessagingRegistrar implements PackageRegistrar
{
    #[Override]
    public function register(Application $app): void
    {
        $app->singleton(
            MessageContractRegistry::class,
            static fn (): ArrayMessageContractRegistry => new ArrayMessageContractRegistry(contracts: []),
        );
        $app->scoped(CloudEventCodec::class, static fn (Application $app): JsonCloudEventCodec => new JsonCloudEventCodec(
            contracts: $app->make(MessageContractRegistry::class),
        ));
        $app->instance(TraceContextBridge::class, new NullTraceContextBridge);
        $app->scoped(
            Outbox::class,
            static fn (Application $app): SqlOutbox => new SqlOutbox(
                connection: $app->make(ConnectionInterface::class),
                contracts: $app->make(MessageContractRegistry::class),
                clock: $app->make(ClockInterface::class),
                traces: $app->make(TraceContextBridge::class),
                table: 'sys_outbox_messages',
            ),
        );
        $app->scoped(
            OutboxRelay::class,
            static fn (Application $app): SqlOutboxRelay => new SqlOutboxRelay(
                connection: $app->make(Connection::class),
                clock: $app->make(ClockInterface::class),
                leaseSeconds: $app->make(CoreOptions::class)->messagingLeaseSeconds,
                maxAttempts: $app->make(CoreOptions::class)->messagingMaxAttempts,
                backoffCapSeconds: $app->make(CoreOptions::class)->messagingBackoffCapSeconds,
                table: 'sys_outbox_messages',
            ),
        );
        $app->scoped(
            Inbox::class,
            static fn (Application $app): SqlInbox => new SqlInbox(
                connection: $app->make(ConnectionInterface::class),
                clock: $app->make(ClockInterface::class),
                table: 'sys_inbox_messages',
            ),
        );
        $app->scoped(
            SqlDeliveryHealth::class,
            static fn (Application $app): SqlDeliveryHealth => new SqlDeliveryHealth(
                connection: $app->make(ConnectionInterface::class),
                clock: $app->make(ClockInterface::class),
                table: 'sys_outbox_messages',
            ),
        );
        $app->singleton(PubSub::class, static fn (Application $app): PubSub => $app->make(match ($app->make(CoreOptions::class)->pubsubDriver) {
            PubSubDriver::Redis => RedisPubSub::class,
            PubSubDriver::Database => DatabasePollPubSub::class,
            PubSubDriver::Null => NullPubSub::class,
        }));
        $app->singleton(WorkerRuntime::class, static fn (): DefaultWorkerRuntime => new DefaultWorkerRuntime(ApplicationLocator::current(...)));
    }

    #[Override]
    public function boot(Application $app): void {}
}
