WHOLE PRODUCT FILE A packages/core/tests/Feature/BaseModelIdentityTest.php
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

use CoreX\Database\Laravel\BaseModel;
use Illuminate\Database\Schema\Blueprint;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Schema;
use Illuminate\Support\Str;

it('persists generated UUIDv7 keys and preserves an explicit UUID key', function (): void {
    Schema::create('identity_probes', function (Blueprint $table): void {
        $table->uuid('id')->primary();
        $table->timestamps();
        $table->softDeletes();
    });

    $model = new class extends BaseModel
    {
        protected $table = 'identity_probes';

        protected $guarded = [];
    };
    $model->save();

    expect(Str::isUuid($model->getKey()))->toBeTrue()
        ->and($model->getKey()[14])->toBe('7')
        ->and($model->getKeyType())->toBe('string')
        ->and($model->getIncrementing())->toBeFalse()
        ->and($model->fresh()->getKey())->toBe($model->getKey());

    $explicitId = '01932f5a-0000-7000-8000-000000000001';
    $explicit = $model->newInstance(['id' => $explicitId]);
    $explicit->save();

    expect($explicit->fresh()->getKey())->toBe($explicitId);
});


WHOLE PRODUCT FILE M packages/core/tests/Feature/InboxClaimTest.php
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

use CoreX\Messaging\Contracts\Inbox;
use CoreX\Messaging\Exceptions\InboxRefused;
use CoreX\Messaging\Laravel\InboxRetention;
use CoreX\Time\Testing\FrozenClock;
use Illuminate\Database\Schema\Blueprint;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\DB;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Schema;
use Psr\Clock\ClockInterface;

function inboxClaimInstallSchema(): void
{
    Schema::create('sys_inbox_messages', function (Blueprint $table): void {
        $table->string('consumer');
        $table->string('message_id');
        $table->string('name');
        $table->dateTimeTz('processed_at');
        $table->primary(['consumer', 'message_id']);
    });
}

beforeEach(function (): void {
    inboxClaimInstallSchema();
});

it('forgets only explicit message ids for the selected consumer', function (): void {
    $inbox = app(Inbox::class);
    DB::transaction(function () use ($inbox): void {
        foreach (['webhooks', 'billing'] as $consumer) {
            foreach (['old', 'retained'] as $messageId) {
                $inbox->claim(consumer: $consumer, messageId: $messageId, name: 'example');
            }
        }
    });
    $retention = new InboxRetention(connection: DB::connection(), table: 'sys_inbox_messages');

    expect($retention->forget(consumer: 'webhooks', messageIds: []))->toBe(0)
        ->and($retention->forget(consumer: 'webhooks', messageIds: ['old']))->toBe(1)
        ->and($retention->forget(consumer: 'webhooks', messageIds: ['old']))->toBe(0)
        ->and(DB::table('sys_inbox_messages')->where('consumer', 'billing')->count())->toBe(2)
        ->and(DB::table('sys_inbox_messages')->where('consumer', 'webhooks')->value('message_id'))->toBe('retained');
});

it('refuses claim outside a transaction with MSG-006', function (): void {
    $inbox = app(Inbox::class);

    expect(fn (): bool => $inbox->claim(
        consumer: 'search.projection.v1',
        messageId: '11111111-1111-7111-8111-111111111111',
        name: 'membership.expired',
    ))->toThrow(InboxRefused::class, 'MSG-006');
});

it('returns true on first claim and false on duplicate consumer and message id', function (): void {
    $inbox = app(Inbox::class);
    $messageId = '22222222-2222-7222-8222-222222222222';

    $first = null;
    $second = null;

    DB::transaction(function () use ($inbox, $messageId, &$first, &$second): void {
        $first = $inbox->claim(
            consumer: 'search.projection.v1',
            messageId: $messageId,
            name: 'membership.expired',
        );
        $second = $inbox->claim(
            consumer: 'search.projection.v1',
            messageId: $messageId,
            name: 'membership.expired',
        );
    });

    expect($first)->toBeTrue()
        ->and($second)->toBeFalse();
});

it('treats consumers independently for the same message id', function (): void {
    $inbox = app(Inbox::class);
    $messageId = '33333333-3333-7333-8333-333333333333';

    $firstConsumer = null;
    $secondConsumer = null;

    DB::transaction(function () use ($inbox, $messageId, &$firstConsumer, &$secondConsumer): void {
        $firstConsumer = $inbox->claim(
            consumer: 'search.projection.v1',
            messageId: $messageId,
            name: 'membership.expired',
        );
        $secondConsumer = $inbox->claim(
            consumer: 'billing.projection.v1',
            messageId: $messageId,
            name: 'membership.expired',
        );
    });

    expect($firstConsumer)->toBeTrue()
        ->and($secondConsumer)->toBeTrue();
});

it('stores processed_at from FrozenClock', function (): void {
    $frozen = new DateTimeImmutable('2026-06-15T12:00:00+00:00');
    app()->instance(ClockInterface::class, new FrozenClock($frozen));
    app()->forgetScopedInstances();

    $inbox = app(Inbox::class);
    $messageId = '44444444-4444-7444-8444-444444444444';

    DB::transaction(function () use ($inbox, $messageId): void {
        $inbox->claim(
            consumer: 'search.projection.v1',
            messageId: $messageId,
            name: 'membership.expired',
        );
    });

    $row = DB::table('sys_inbox_messages')
        ->where('consumer', 'search.projection.v1')
        ->where('message_id', $messageId)
        ->first();

    expect($row)->not->toBeNull()
        ->and((new DateTimeImmutable((string) $row->processed_at))->getTimestamp())->toBe($frozen->getTimestamp());
});


WHOLE PRODUCT FILE M packages/core/tests/Feature/MessagePublisherTest.php
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

use CoreX\Messaging\Contracts\DurableMessagePublisher;
use CoreX\Messaging\Contracts\MessagePublisher;
use CoreX\Messaging\Data\MessageEnvelope;
use CoreX\Time\Testing\FrozenClock;
use Illuminate\Contracts\Container\BindingResolutionException;
use Illuminate\Database\Schema\Blueprint;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\DB;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Schema;
use Psr\Clock\ClockInterface;

function messagePublisherInstallSchema(): void
{
    Schema::create('sys_outbox_messages', function (Blueprint $table): void {
        $table->string('id')->primary();
        $table->string('name');
        $table->unsignedSmallInteger('contract_version');
        $table->unsignedSmallInteger('envelope_version');
        $table->string('source');
        $table->string('subject')->nullable();
        $table->string('correlation_id')->nullable();
        $table->string('causation_id')->nullable();
        $table->string('pii_class');
        $table->text('scope');
        $table->text('payload');
        $table->text('metadata');
        $table->dateTimeTz('occurred_at');
        $table->dateTimeTz('available_at');
        $table->string('status');
        $table->string('lease_owner')->nullable();
        $table->dateTimeTz('lease_until')->nullable();
        $table->unsignedSmallInteger('attempts')->default(0);
        $table->string('failure_class')->nullable();
        $table->text('last_error')->nullable();
        $table->dateTimeTz('published_at')->nullable();
        $table->dateTimeTz('dead_at')->nullable();
        $table->timestampsTz();
    });
}

function messagePublisherBindDurable(DurableMessagePublisher $publisher): void
{
    app()->instance(DurableMessagePublisher::class, $publisher);
    app()->instance(MessagePublisher::class, $publisher);
}

beforeEach(function (): void {
    messagePublisherInstallSchema();
    $this->app->instance(ClockInterface::class, new FrozenClock(new DateTimeImmutable('2026-06-15T12:00:00+00:00')));
});

it('admits a durable publisher binding and marks a leased row published', function (): void {
    $publisher = new class implements DurableMessagePublisher
    {
        /** @var list<string> */
        public array $publishedIds = [];

        public function publish(MessageEnvelope $message): void
        {
            $this->publishedIds[] = $message->messageId;
        }
    };
    messagePublisherBindDurable($publisher);

    $envelope = messagingSampleEnvelope(['messageId' => '01932f5a-0000-7000-8000-000000000010']);
    $now = app(ClockInterface::class)->now();
    DB::table('sys_outbox_messages')->insert([
        'id' => $envelope->messageId,
        'name' => $envelope->name,
        'contract_version' => $envelope->contractVersion,
        'envelope_version' => $envelope->envelopeVersion,
        'source' => $envelope->source,
        'subject' => $envelope->subject,
        'correlation_id' => $envelope->correlationId,
        'causation_id' => $envelope->causationId,
        'pii_class' => $envelope->piiClass->value,
        'scope' => json_encode($envelope->scope, JSON_THROW_ON_ERROR),
        'payload' => $envelope->payload->encode(),
        'metadata' => json_encode($envelope->metadata, JSON_THROW_ON_ERROR),
        'occurred_at' => $envelope->occurredAt,
        'available_at' => $now,
        'status' => 'pending',
        'attempts' => 0,
        'created_at' => $now,
        'updated_at' => $now,
    ]);

    $this->artisan('corex:messaging:relay', ['--once' => true])->assertSuccessful();

    expect($publisher->publishedIds)->toBe([$envelope->messageId])
        ->and(DB::table('sys_outbox_messages')->value('status'))->toBe('published');
});

it('refuses relay when no durable publisher is bound', function (): void {
    expect(fn () => $this->artisan('corex:messaging:relay', ['--once' => true]))
        ->toThrow(BindingResolutionException::class);
});

WHOLE PRODUCT FILE M packages/core/tests/Feature/OutboxAppendTest.php
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

use CoreX\Messaging\Contracts\MessageContractRegistry;
use CoreX\Messaging\Contracts\Outbox;
use CoreX\Messaging\Contracts\TraceContextBridge;
use CoreX\Messaging\Data\MessageContract;
use CoreX\Messaging\Data\MessagePayload;
use CoreX\Messaging\Enums\MessageClass;
use CoreX\Messaging\Enums\PiiClass;
use CoreX\Messaging\Exceptions\OutboxRefused;
use CoreX\Messaging\Exceptions\UnknownMessageContract;
use CoreX\Messaging\Internal\ArrayMessageContractRegistry;
use CoreX\Observability\Data\OperationContext;
use CoreX\Time\Testing\FrozenClock;
use Illuminate\Database\Schema\Blueprint;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\DB;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Schema;
use Psr\Clock\ClockInterface;

function outboxAppendDeclaredContract(): MessageContract
{
    return new MessageContract(
        name: 'membership.expired',
        version: 1,
        class: MessageClass::IntegrationEvent,
        owner: 'acme/core',
        maxPiiClass: PiiClass::Pseudonymous,
        channel: 'membership',
    );
}

function outboxAppendInstallSchema(): void
{
    Schema::create('sys_outbox_messages', function (Blueprint $table): void {
        $table->string('id')->primary();
        $table->string('name');
        $table->unsignedSmallInteger('contract_version');
        $table->unsignedSmallInteger('envelope_version');
        $table->string('source');
        $table->string('subject')->nullable();
        $table->string('correlation_id')->nullable();
        $table->string('causation_id')->nullable();
        $table->string('pii_class');
        $table->text('scope');
        $table->text('payload');
        $table->text('metadata');
        $table->dateTimeTz('occurred_at');
        $table->dateTimeTz('available_at');
        $table->string('status');
        $table->unsignedInteger('attempts');
        $table->dateTimeTz('created_at');
        $table->dateTimeTz('updated_at');
    });
}

function outboxAppendBindRegistry(MessageContract ...$contracts): void
{
    app()->instance(
        MessageContractRegistry::class,
        new ArrayMessageContractRegistry(contracts: $contracts),
    );
    app()->forgetScopedInstances();
}

beforeEach(function (): void {
    outboxAppendInstallSchema();
    outboxAppendBindRegistry(outboxAppendDeclaredContract());
});

it('refuses append outside a transaction with MSG-008', function (): void {
    $outbox = app(Outbox::class);
    $envelope = messagingSampleEnvelope();

    expect(fn (): mixed => $outbox->append($envelope))
        ->toThrow(OutboxRefused::class, 'MSG-008');
});

it('refuses undeclared contracts inside a transaction with MSG-002', function (): void {
    outboxAppendBindRegistry();
    $outbox = app(Outbox::class);
    $envelope = messagingSampleEnvelope();

    DB::transaction(function () use ($outbox, $envelope): void {
        expect(fn (): mixed => $outbox->append($envelope))
            ->toThrow(UnknownMessageContract::class, 'MSG-002');
    });
});

it('refuses payload personal data above the contract maximum with MSG-007', function (): void {
    $outbox = app(Outbox::class);
    $envelope = messagingSampleEnvelope(['piiClass' => PiiClass::Personal]);

    DB::transaction(function () use ($outbox, $envelope): void {
        expect(fn (): mixed => $outbox->append($envelope))
            ->toThrow(OutboxRefused::class, 'MSG-007');
    });
});

it('persists a pending row with encoded payload and clock-backed timestamps', function (): void {
    $frozen = new DateTimeImmutable('2026-06-15T12:00:00+00:00');
    app()->instance(ClockInterface::class, new FrozenClock($frozen));
    app()->forgetScopedInstances();

    $payload = new MessagePayload(['kind' => 'expired', 'count' => 2]);
    $envelope = messagingSampleEnvelope(['payload' => $payload]);
    $outbox = app(Outbox::class);

    DB::transaction(function () use ($outbox, $envelope): void {
        $outbox->append($envelope);
    });

    $row = DB::table('sys_outbox_messages')->where('id', $envelope->messageId)->first();

    expect($row)->not->toBeNull()
        ->and($row->status)->toBe('pending')
        ->and($row->attempts)->toBe(0)
        ->and($row->payload)->toBe($payload->encode())
        ->and((new DateTimeImmutable((string) $row->available_at))->getTimestamp())->toBe($frozen->getTimestamp())
        ->and((new DateTimeImmutable((string) $row->created_at))->getTimestamp())->toBe($frozen->getTimestamp())
        ->and((new DateTimeImmutable((string) $row->updated_at))->getTimestamp())->toBe($frozen->getTimestamp());
});

it('injects trace carrier into persisted metadata without changing identity', function (): void {
    app()->instance(TraceContextBridge::class, new class implements TraceContextBridge
    {
        public function inject(array $metadata): array
        {
            $metadata['traceparent'] = '00-'.str_repeat('ab', 16).'-'.str_repeat('cd', 8).'-01';

            return $metadata;
        }

        public function linkedConsumer(array $metadata, OperationContext $operation, Closure $work): mixed
        {
            return $work();
        }
    });
    app()->forgetScopedInstances();

    $envelope = messagingSampleEnvelope([
        'messageId' => 'msg-trace-1',
        'metadata' => ['trace' => 'keep'],
        'correlationId' => 'corr-keep',
    ]);
    $outbox = app(Outbox::class);

    DB::transaction(function () use ($outbox, $envelope): void {
        $outbox->append($envelope);
    });

    $row = DB::table('sys_outbox_messages')->where('id', 'msg-trace-1')->first();
    $metadata = json_decode((string) $row->metadata, true, flags: JSON_THROW_ON_ERROR);

    expect($row->correlation_id)->toBe('corr-keep')
        ->and($row->payload)->toBe($envelope->payload->encode())
        ->and($envelope->metadata)->toBe(['trace' => 'keep'])
        ->and($metadata)->toBe([
            'trace' => 'keep',
            'traceparent' => '00-'.str_repeat('ab', 16).'-'.str_repeat('cd', 8).'-01',
        ]);
});

it('uses one clock instant for available_at created_at and updated_at', function (): void {
    $frozen = new DateTimeImmutable('2024-01-02T03:04:05+00:00');
    app()->instance(ClockInterface::class, new FrozenClock($frozen));
    app()->forgetScopedInstances();

    $outbox = app(Outbox::class);

    DB::transaction(function () use ($outbox): void {
        $outbox->append(messagingSampleEnvelope(['messageId' => 'msg-clock-1']));
    });

    $row = DB::table('sys_outbox_messages')->where('id', 'msg-clock-1')->first();

    expect($row->available_at)->toBe($row->created_at)
        ->and($row->created_at)->toBe($row->updated_at);
});


WHOLE PRODUCT FILE M packages/core/tests/Feature/PruneOutboxTest.php
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

use CoreX\Time\Testing\FrozenClock;
use Illuminate\Database\Schema\Blueprint;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Artisan;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\DB;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Schema;
use Psr\Clock\ClockInterface;

function pruneOutboxInstallSchema(): void
{
    Schema::create('sys_outbox_messages', function (Blueprint $table): void {
        $table->string('id')->primary();
        $table->string('name');
        $table->unsignedSmallInteger('contract_version');
        $table->unsignedSmallInteger('envelope_version');
        $table->string('source');
        $table->string('subject')->nullable();
        $table->string('correlation_id')->nullable();
        $table->string('causation_id')->nullable();
        $table->string('pii_class');
        $table->text('scope');
        $table->text('payload');
        $table->text('metadata');
        $table->dateTimeTz('occurred_at');
        $table->dateTimeTz('available_at');
        $table->string('status');
        $table->string('lease_owner')->nullable();
        $table->dateTimeTz('lease_until')->nullable();
        $table->unsignedInteger('attempts');
        $table->string('failure_class')->nullable();
        $table->text('last_error')->nullable();
        $table->dateTimeTz('published_at')->nullable();
        $table->dateTimeTz('dead_at')->nullable();
        $table->dateTimeTz('created_at');
        $table->dateTimeTz('updated_at');
    });

    Schema::create('sys_inbox_messages', function (Blueprint $table): void {
        $table->string('consumer');
        $table->string('message_id');
        $table->string('name');
        $table->dateTimeTz('processed_at');
        $table->primary(['consumer', 'message_id']);
    });
}

function pruneOutboxInsertRow(array $overrides = []): void
{
    $now = app(ClockInterface::class)->now();

    DB::table('sys_outbox_messages')->insert([
        'id' => $overrides['id'] ?? 'msg-prune-1',
        'name' => 'membership.expired',
        'contract_version' => 1,
        'envelope_version' => 1,
        'source' => 'axiomasoft/corex',
        'subject' => null,
        'correlation_id' => null,
        'causation_id' => null,
        'pii_class' => 'none',
        'scope' => '{}',
        'payload' => '{}',
        'metadata' => '{}',
        'occurred_at' => $overrides['occurred_at'] ?? $now,
        'available_at' => $overrides['available_at'] ?? $now,
        'status' => $overrides['status'] ?? 'pending',
        'lease_owner' => null,
        'lease_until' => null,
        'attempts' => $overrides['attempts'] ?? 0,
        'failure_class' => null,
        'last_error' => null,
        'published_at' => $overrides['published_at'] ?? null,
        'dead_at' => $overrides['dead_at'] ?? null,
        'created_at' => $now,
        'updated_at' => $now,
    ]);
}

beforeEach(function (): void {
    pruneOutboxInstallSchema();
    app()->instance(ClockInterface::class, new FrozenClock(new DateTimeImmutable('2026-06-15T12:00:00+00:00')));
});

it('prunes published rows older than the published window and keeps newer ones', function (): void {
    $now = app(ClockInterface::class)->now();
    pruneOutboxInsertRow([
        'id' => 'pub-old',
        'status' => 'published',
        'published_at' => $now->sub(new DateInterval('P20D')),
    ]);
    pruneOutboxInsertRow([
        'id' => 'pub-new',
        'status' => 'published',
        'published_at' => $now->sub(new DateInterval('P5D')),
    ]);

    Artisan::call('corex:messaging:prune', ['--published-older-than' => '14 days']);

    expect(DB::table('sys_outbox_messages')->pluck('id')->all())
        ->toBe(['pub-new']);
});

it('prunes dead rows by the dead window not the published window', function (): void {
    $now = app(ClockInterface::class)->now();
    pruneOutboxInsertRow([
        'id' => 'dead-old',
        'status' => 'dead',
        'dead_at' => $now->sub(new DateInterval('P100D')),
    ]);
    pruneOutboxInsertRow([
        'id' => 'dead-recent',
        'status' => 'dead',
        'dead_at' => $now->sub(new DateInterval('P30D')),
    ]);

    Artisan::call('corex:messaging:prune', [
        '--published-older-than' => '1 day',
        '--dead-older-than' => '90 days',
    ]);

    expect(DB::table('sys_outbox_messages')->pluck('id')->all())
        ->toBe(['dead-recent']);
});

it('never prunes pending or publishing rows', function (): void {
    $now = app(ClockInterface::class)->now();
    pruneOutboxInsertRow([
        'id' => 'pending-1',
        'status' => 'pending',
        'occurred_at' => $now->sub(new DateInterval('P365D')),
    ]);
    pruneOutboxInsertRow([
        'id' => 'publishing-1',
        'status' => 'publishing',
        'occurred_at' => $now->sub(new DateInterval('P365D')),
    ]);

    Artisan::call('corex:messaging:prune', [
        '--published-older-than' => '0 seconds',
        '--dead-older-than' => '0 seconds',
    ]);

    expect(DB::table('sys_outbox_messages')->count())->toBe(2);
});

it('does not touch sys_inbox_messages', function (): void {
    $now = app(ClockInterface::class)->now();
    pruneOutboxInsertRow([
        'id' => 'pub-stale',
        'status' => 'published',
        'published_at' => $now->sub(new DateInterval('P30D')),
    ]);

    DB::table('sys_inbox_messages')->insert([
        'consumer' => 'worker-a',
        'message_id' => 'inbox-1',
        'name' => 'membership.expired',
        'processed_at' => $now,
    ]);

    Artisan::call('corex:messaging:prune', ['--published-older-than' => '1 day']);

    expect(DB::table('sys_inbox_messages')->count())->toBe(1);
});

it('uses FrozenClock for retention thresholds', function (): void {
    $anchor = new DateTimeImmutable('2026-01-01T00:00:00+00:00');
    app()->instance(ClockInterface::class, new FrozenClock($anchor));

    pruneOutboxInsertRow([
        'id' => 'on-boundary',
        'status' => 'published',
        'published_at' => $anchor->sub(new DateInterval('P14D')),
    ]);
    pruneOutboxInsertRow([
        'id' => 'past-boundary',
        'status' => 'published',
        'published_at' => $anchor->sub(new DateInterval('P14DT1S')),
    ]);

    Artisan::call('corex:messaging:prune', ['--published-older-than' => '14 days']);

    expect(DB::table('sys_outbox_messages')->pluck('id')->all())
        ->toBe(['on-boundary']);
});

<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

use CoreX\Audit\Contracts\SynchronousAuditWriter;
use CoreX\Audit\Data\AuditEntry;
use CoreX\Audit\Enums\ActorType;
use CoreX\Data\Data\JsonDocument;
use Illuminate\Database\Schema\Blueprint;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\DB;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Schema;
use Illuminate\Support\Str;

beforeEach(function (): void {
    Schema::connection('testbench')->create('sys_audit_log', function (Blueprint $table): void {
        $table->string('id')->primary();
        $table->dateTime('occurred_at');
        $table->string('actor_type');
        $table->string('actor_id')->nullable();
        $table->string('workspace_id')->nullable();
        $table->string('entity_type')->nullable();
        $table->string('entity_id')->nullable();
        $table->string('action');
        $table->text('changes')->nullable();
        $table->text('context');
        $table->dateTime('created_at')->nullable();
    });
});

it('commits a mandatory audit row with the event snapshot despite optional audit being disabled', function (): void {
    config(['corex.audit.enabled' => false, 'queue.default' => 'database']);
    $entry = new AuditEntry(
        action: 'impersonation.started', actorType: ActorType::Support, actorId: 'staff',
        context: new JsonDocument('{"grant":"g1"}'),
        occurredAt: new DateTimeImmutable('2026-10-05T04:00:00+00:00'),
    );

    app(SynchronousAuditWriter::class)->record($entry, 'testbench');

    expect(DB::connection('testbench')->table('sys_audit_log')->count())->toBe(1)
        ->and(DB::connection('testbench')->transactionLevel())->toBe(0);
    $id = DB::connection('testbench')->table('sys_audit_log')->value('id');
    expect(is_string($id) && Str::isUuid($id))->toBeTrue()
        ->and($id[14])->toBe('7');
});

it('rejects an ambient transaction before writing the audit row', function (): void {
    $connection = DB::connection('testbench');
    $connection->beginTransaction();

    try {
        expect(fn () => app(SynchronousAuditWriter::class)->record(new AuditEntry(
            action: 'impersonation.started', actorType: ActorType::Support,
            occurredAt: new DateTimeImmutable('2026-10-05T04:00:00+00:00'),
        ), 'testbench'))->toThrow(RuntimeException::class);
        expect($connection->table('sys_audit_log')->count())->toBe(0);
    } finally {
        $connection->rollBack();
    }
});


WHOLE PRODUCT FILE M packages/core/tests/Pg/DeliveryGuaranteesTest.php
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

use CoreX\Messaging\Contracts\DurableMessagePublisher;
use CoreX\Messaging\Contracts\Inbox;
use CoreX\Messaging\Contracts\MessagePublisher;
use CoreX\Messaging\Contracts\Outbox;
use CoreX\Messaging\Data\MessageEnvelope;
use CoreX\Messaging\Internal\SqlOutboxRelay;
use CoreX\Testing\Fixtures\RecordingPublisher;
use Illuminate\Contracts\Container\BindingResolutionException;
use Illuminate\Database\Schema\Blueprint;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Artisan;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\DB;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Schema;
use Psr\Clock\ClockInterface;

const DG_RELAY_OWNER_A = 'cccccccc-cccc-4ccc-8ccc-cccccccccccc';

const DG_RELAY_OWNER_B = 'dddddddd-dddd-4ddd-8ddd-dddddddddddd';

function deliveryGuaranteesBindPublisher(DurableMessagePublisher $publisher): void
{
    app()->instance(DurableMessagePublisher::class, $publisher);
    app()->instance(MessagePublisher::class, $publisher);
}

function deliveryGuaranteesInstallBusinessTable(): void
{
    Schema::connection('pgsql')->create('dg_business_effects', function (Blueprint $table): void {
        $table->uuid('message_id')->primary();
        $table->string('consumer', 120);
        $table->timestampsTz();
    });
}

function deliveryGuaranteesInstallDurableStore(): void
{
    Schema::connection('pgsql')->create('dg_durable_store', function (Blueprint $table): void {
        $table->uuid('message_id')->primary();
        $table->string('name', 160);
        $table->text('payload');
        $table->timestampTz('stored_at');
    });
}

/**
 * @return list<bool>
 */
function deliveryGuaranteesConsume(MessageEnvelope $envelope, string $consumer = 'delivery.guarantees.v1'): array
{
    $inbox = app(Inbox::class);
    $claims = [];

    DB::connection('pgsql')->transaction(function () use ($inbox, $envelope, $consumer, &$claims): void {
        $claims[] = $inbox->claim(
            consumer: $consumer,
            messageId: $envelope->messageId,
            name: $envelope->name,
        );

        if ($claims[array_key_last($claims)] === false) {
            return;
        }

        DB::connection('pgsql')->table('dg_business_effects')->insert([
            'message_id' => $envelope->messageId,
            'consumer' => $consumer,
            'created_at' => app(ClockInterface::class)->now(),
            'updated_at' => app(ClockInterface::class)->now(),
        ]);
    });

    return $claims;
}

function deliveryGuaranteesRelayOnConnection(string $connectionName): SqlOutboxRelay
{
    return new SqlOutboxRelay(
        connection: DB::connection($connectionName),
        clock: app(ClockInterface::class),
        leaseSeconds: 120,
        maxAttempts: 8,
        backoffCapSeconds: 300,
    );
}

beforeEach(function (): void {
    outboxRelayMigrate();
    outboxRelayClock(new DateTimeImmutable('2026-06-15T12:00:00+00:00'));
    outboxAppendBindRegistry(outboxAppendDeclaredContract());
    deliveryGuaranteesInstallBusinessTable();
    deliveryGuaranteesInstallDurableStore();

    config([
        'database.connections.dg_relay_a' => array_merge(config('database.connections.pgsql'), ['name' => 'dg_relay_a']),
        'database.connections.dg_relay_b' => array_merge(config('database.connections.pgsql'), ['name' => 'dg_relay_b']),
    ]);
});

it('proves at-least-once when the relay dies after durable publish but before markPublished', function (): void {
    $messageId = outboxRelayInsertRow(['id' => '01932f5a-0000-7000-8000-000000000101']);
    $relay = outboxRelayService(leaseSeconds: 60);
    $leased = $relay->lease(leaseOwner: OUTBOX_RELAY_OWNER, batchSize: 1);
    expect($leased)->toHaveCount(1);

    $publisher = new RecordingPublisher(mode: RecordingPublisher::MODE_THROW_AFTER_RECORD);
    deliveryGuaranteesBindPublisher($publisher);

    try {
        $publisher->publish($leased[0]->envelope);
    } catch (RuntimeException) {
        // Relay process died before markPublished; no releaseWithBackoff — crash, not defer.
    }

    expect(DB::table('sys_outbox_messages')->where('id', $messageId)->value('status'))->toBe('publishing')
        ->and($publisher->recordedMessageIds)->toBe([$messageId]);

    outboxRelayClock(new DateTimeImmutable('2026-06-15T13:05:00+00:00'));

    $rowBeforeReclaim = DB::table('sys_outbox_messages')->where('id', $messageId)->first();
    expect($rowBeforeReclaim->status)->toBe('publishing');

    $reclaimRelay = outboxRelayService(leaseSeconds: 60);
    $secondLease = $reclaimRelay->lease(leaseOwner: OUTBOX_RELAY_OTHER_OWNER, batchSize: 1);
    expect($secondLease)->toHaveCount(1)
        ->and($secondLease[0]->envelope->messageId)->toBe($messageId);

    $publisher->mode = RecordingPublisher::MODE_SUCCEED;
    $publisher->publish($secondLease[0]->envelope);
    $reclaimRelay->markPublished($secondLease[0]);

    expect($publisher->recordedMessageIds)->toBe([$messageId, $messageId]);
})->group('pg');

it('does not repeat the business effect when the same messageId is consumed twice', function (): void {
    $envelope = messagingSampleEnvelope(['messageId' => '01932f5a-0000-7000-8000-000000000102']);

    $firstClaim = deliveryGuaranteesConsume($envelope)[0];
    $secondClaim = deliveryGuaranteesConsume($envelope)[0];

    expect($firstClaim)->toBeTrue()
        ->and($secondClaim)->toBeFalse()
        ->and(DB::table('dg_business_effects')->count())->toBe(1);
})->group('pg');

it('partitions concurrent relay leases without overlap or blocking', function (): void {
    foreach (range(1, 4) as $index) {
        outboxRelayInsertRow(['id' => sprintf('01932f5a-0000-7000-8000-0000000001%02d', $index)]);
    }

    $relayA = deliveryGuaranteesRelayOnConnection('dg_relay_a');
    $relayB = deliveryGuaranteesRelayOnConnection('dg_relay_b');

    $leasedA = $relayA->lease(leaseOwner: DG_RELAY_OWNER_A, batchSize: 2);
    $leasedB = $relayB->lease(leaseOwner: DG_RELAY_OWNER_B, batchSize: 2);

    $idsA = array_map(static fn ($message) => $message->envelope->messageId, $leasedA);
    $idsB = array_map(static fn ($message) => $message->envelope->messageId, $leasedB);

    expect($idsA)->toHaveCount(2)
        ->and($idsB)->toHaveCount(2)
        ->and(array_intersect($idsA, $idsB))->toBe([]);

    $heldId = outboxRelayInsertRow(['id' => '01932f5a-0000-7000-8000-000000000120']);
    $table = 'sys_outbox_messages';

    DB::connection('dg_relay_a')->beginTransaction();
    DB::connection('dg_relay_a')->table($table)->where('id', $heldId)->lockForUpdate()->first();

    $skippedWhileHeld = $relayB->lease(leaseOwner: DG_RELAY_OWNER_B, batchSize: 10);
    $skippedIds = array_map(static fn ($message) => $message->envelope->messageId, $skippedWhileHeld);

    expect($skippedIds)->not->toContain($heldId);

    DB::connection('dg_relay_a')->rollBack();
})->group('pg');

it('rolls back outbox rows with the business transaction and commits both on success', function (): void {
    $outbox = app(Outbox::class);
    $messageId = '01932f5a-0000-7000-8000-000000000103';

    try {
        DB::connection('pgsql')->transaction(function () use ($outbox, $messageId): void {
            DB::connection('pgsql')->table('dg_business_effects')->insert([
                'message_id' => $messageId,
                'consumer' => 'rollback.probe',
                'created_at' => app(ClockInterface::class)->now(),
                'updated_at' => app(ClockInterface::class)->now(),
            ]);
            $outbox->append(messagingSampleEnvelope(['messageId' => $messageId]));

            throw new RuntimeException('force-rollback');
        });
    } catch (RuntimeException $exception) {
        expect($exception->getMessage())->toBe('force-rollback');
    }

    expect(DB::table('dg_business_effects')->count())->toBe(0)
        ->and(DB::table('sys_outbox_messages')->count())->toBe(0);

    DB::connection('pgsql')->transaction(function () use ($outbox, $messageId): void {
        DB::connection('pgsql')->table('dg_business_effects')->insert([
            'message_id' => $messageId,
            'consumer' => 'rollback.probe',
            'created_at' => app(ClockInterface::class)->now(),
            'updated_at' => app(ClockInterface::class)->now(),
        ]);
        $outbox->append(messagingSampleEnvelope(['messageId' => $messageId]));
    });

    expect(DB::table('dg_business_effects')->count())->toBe(1)
        ->and(DB::table('sys_outbox_messages')->count())->toBe(1);
})->group('pg');

it('hands off durably to a restarted consumer and rejects lossy publishers before markPublished', function (): void {
    $durablePublisher = new class implements DurableMessagePublisher
    {
        public function publish(MessageEnvelope $message): void
        {
            DB::connection('pgsql')->table('dg_durable_store')->insert([
                'message_id' => $message->messageId,
                'name' => $message->name,
                'payload' => $message->payload->encode(),
                'stored_at' => app(ClockInterface::class)->now(),
            ]);
        }
    };
    deliveryGuaranteesBindPublisher($durablePublisher);

    $messageId = outboxRelayInsertRow(['id' => '01932f5a-0000-7000-8000-000000000104']);
    Artisan::call('corex:messaging:relay', ['--once' => true]);

    expect(DB::table('sys_outbox_messages')->where('id', $messageId)->value('status'))->toBe('published')
        ->and(DB::table('dg_durable_store')->count())->toBe(1);

    $stored = DB::table('dg_durable_store')->where('message_id', $messageId)->first();
    $envelope = messagingSampleEnvelope(['messageId' => $messageId]);

    deliveryGuaranteesConsume($envelope);
    deliveryGuaranteesConsume($envelope);

    expect(DB::table('dg_business_effects')->count())->toBe(1);

    outboxRelayInsertRow(['id' => '01932f5a-0000-7000-8000-000000000105']);
    app()->forgetInstance(DurableMessagePublisher::class);
    app()->forgetInstance(MessagePublisher::class);
    app()->scoped(MessagePublisher::class, static fn (): MessagePublisher => new class implements MessagePublisher
    {
        public function publish(MessageEnvelope $message): void {}
    });

    expect(fn () => Artisan::call('corex:messaging:relay', ['--once' => true]))
        ->toThrow(BindingResolutionException::class);

    expect(DB::table('sys_outbox_messages')->where('id', '01932f5a-0000-7000-8000-000000000105')->value('status'))
        ->toBe('pending');
})->group('pg');

it('terminates expired publishing rows at maxAttempts without another publish and fences corrupt envelopes', function (): void {
    $now = app(ClockInterface::class)->now();
    $exhaustedId = outboxRelayInsertRow([
        'id' => '01932f5a-0000-7000-8000-000000000106',
        'status' => 'publishing',
        'lease_owner' => OUTBOX_RELAY_OTHER_OWNER,
        'lease_until' => $now->modify('-1 minute'),
        'attempts' => 8,
    ]);
    $publisher = new RecordingPublisher;
    deliveryGuaranteesBindPublisher($publisher);

    $relay = outboxRelayService(maxAttempts: 8);
    expect($relay->lease(leaseOwner: OUTBOX_RELAY_OWNER, batchSize: 1))->toBe([]);

    $exhaustedRow = DB::table('sys_outbox_messages')->where('id', $exhaustedId)->first();
    expect($exhaustedRow->status)->toBe('dead')
        ->and($exhaustedRow->failure_class)->toBe('lease-expiry-attempts-exhausted')
        ->and($publisher->recordedMessageIds)->toBe([]);

    $corruptId = outboxRelayInsertRow([
        'id' => '01932f5a-0000-7000-8000-000000000107',
        'scope_raw' => '[]',
    ]);
    $corruptRelay = outboxRelayService(maxAttempts: 1);
    expect($corruptRelay->lease(leaseOwner: OUTBOX_RELAY_OWNER, batchSize: 1))->toBe([]);

    $corruptRow = DB::table('sys_outbox_messages')->where('id', $corruptId)->first();
    expect($corruptRow->status)->toBe('dead')
        ->and($corruptRow->failure_class)->toBe('attempts-exhausted')
        ->and($publisher->recordedMessageIds)->toBe([]);
})->group('pg');


WHOLE PRODUCT FILE M packages/core/tests/Pg/DeliveryHealthTest.php
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

use CoreX\Messaging\Internal\SqlDeliveryHealth;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Artisan;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\DB;
use Psr\Clock\ClockInterface;

function deliveryHealthInsertRow(array $overrides = []): void
{
    $now = app(ClockInterface::class)->now();

    DB::connection('pgsql')->table('sys_outbox_messages')->insert([
        'id' => $overrides['id'] ?? '01932f5a-0000-7000-8000-000000000099',
        'name' => $overrides['name'] ?? 'membership.expired',
        'contract_version' => $overrides['contract_version'] ?? 1,
        'envelope_version' => 1,
        'source' => 'axiomasoft/corex',
        'subject' => 'subject-1',
        'correlation_id' => 'corr-1',
        'causation_id' => 'cause-1',
        'pii_class' => 'none',
        'scope' => json_encode(['accountId' => 'account-1'], JSON_THROW_ON_ERROR),
        'payload' => '{}',
        'metadata' => '{}',
        'occurred_at' => $overrides['occurred_at'] ?? $now,
        'available_at' => $overrides['available_at'] ?? $now,
        'status' => $overrides['status'] ?? 'pending',
        'lease_owner' => null,
        'lease_until' => null,
        'attempts' => $overrides['attempts'] ?? 0,
        'failure_class' => null,
        'last_error' => null,
        'published_at' => $overrides['published_at'] ?? null,
        'dead_at' => $overrides['dead_at'] ?? null,
        'created_at' => $now,
        'updated_at' => $now,
    ]);
}

beforeEach(function (): void {
    outboxRelayMigrate();
});

it('returns null oldest age and zero counters on an empty table', function (): void {
    outboxRelayClock(new DateTimeImmutable('2026-06-15T12:00:00+00:00'));

    $health = app(SqlDeliveryHealth::class)->read();

    expect($health->oldestPendingSeconds)->toBeNull()
        ->and($health->oldestPendingSecondsByContract)->toBe([])
        ->and($health->pendingCount)->toBe(0)
        ->and($health->publishingCount)->toBe(0)
        ->and($health->deadCount)->toBe(0)
        ->and($health->maxAttempts)->toBe(0);
})->group('pg');

it('reports per-contract ages and the maximum as oldest pending', function (): void {
    $now = new DateTimeImmutable('2026-06-15T12:00:00+00:00');
    outboxRelayClock($now);

    deliveryHealthInsertRow([
        'id' => '01932f5a-0000-7000-8000-000000000031',
        'occurred_at' => $now->sub(new DateInterval('PT2H')),
    ]);
    deliveryHealthInsertRow([
        'id' => '01932f5a-0000-7000-8000-000000000032',
        'name' => 'billing.invoice',
        'contract_version' => 2,
        'occurred_at' => $now->sub(new DateInterval('PT30M')),
    ]);

    $health = app(SqlDeliveryHealth::class)->read();

    expect($health->oldestPendingSeconds)->toBe(7200)
        ->and($health->oldestPendingSecondsByContract)->toBe([
            'billing.invoice.v2' => 1800,
            'membership.expired.v1' => 7200,
        ])
        ->and($health->pendingCount)->toBe(2);
})->group('pg');

it('excludes pending rows with future available_at from age', function (): void {
    $now = new DateTimeImmutable('2026-06-15T12:00:00+00:00');
    outboxRelayClock($now);

    deliveryHealthInsertRow([
        'id' => '01932f5a-0000-7000-8000-000000000041',
        'occurred_at' => $now->sub(new DateInterval('PT1H')),
        'available_at' => $now->add(new DateInterval('PT1H')),
    ]);

    $health = app(SqlDeliveryHealth::class)->read();

    expect($health->oldestPendingSeconds)->toBeNull()
        ->and($health->pendingCount)->toBe(1);
})->group('pg');

it('counts published rows only in status totals not in pending age', function (): void {
    $now = new DateTimeImmutable('2026-06-15T12:00:00+00:00');
    outboxRelayClock($now);

    deliveryHealthInsertRow([
        'id' => '01932f5a-0000-7000-8000-000000000051',
        'status' => 'published',
        'published_at' => $now,
        'occurred_at' => $now->sub(new DateInterval('P1D')),
    ]);

    $health = app(SqlDeliveryHealth::class)->read();

    expect($health->oldestPendingSeconds)->toBeNull()
        ->and($health->pendingCount)->toBe(0)
        ->and($health->publishingCount)->toBe(0)
        ->and($health->deadCount)->toBe(0);
})->group('pg');

it('computes maxAttempts only from pending and publishing rows', function (): void {
    $now = new DateTimeImmutable('2026-06-15T12:00:00+00:00');
    outboxRelayClock($now);

    deliveryHealthInsertRow([
        'id' => '01932f5a-0000-7000-8000-000000000061',
        'status' => 'pending',
        'attempts' => 3,
    ]);
    deliveryHealthInsertRow([
        'id' => '01932f5a-0000-7000-8000-000000000062',
        'status' => 'publishing',
        'attempts' => 5,
    ]);
    deliveryHealthInsertRow([
        'id' => '01932f5a-0000-7000-8000-000000000063',
        'status' => 'dead',
        'attempts' => 99,
        'dead_at' => $now,
    ]);

    $health = app(SqlDeliveryHealth::class)->read();

    expect($health->maxAttempts)->toBe(5)
        ->and($health->deadCount)->toBe(1);
})->group('pg');

it('fails the health probe when oldest pending exceeds max-age', function (): void {
    $now = new DateTimeImmutable('2026-06-15T12:00:00+00:00');
    outboxRelayClock($now);

    deliveryHealthInsertRow([
        'id' => '01932f5a-0000-7000-8000-000000000071',
        'occurred_at' => $now->sub(new DateInterval('PT5M')),
    ]);

    expect(Artisan::call('corex:messaging:health', ['--max-age' => '0']))->toBe(1)
        ->and(Artisan::call('corex:messaging:health', ['--max-age' => '600']))->toBe(0);
})->group('pg');

it('returns success for max-age probe on an empty table', function (): void {
    outboxRelayClock(new DateTimeImmutable('2026-06-15T12:00:00+00:00'));

    expect(Artisan::call('corex:messaging:health', ['--max-age' => '0']))->toBe(0);
})->group('pg');


WHOLE PRODUCT FILE M packages/core/tests/Pg/InboxConcurrencyTest.php
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

use Illuminate\Support\Facades\DB;

beforeEach(function (): void {
    config(['database.default' => 'pgsql']);
    DB::statement('DROP SCHEMA IF EXISTS public CASCADE');
    DB::statement('CREATE SCHEMA public');
    test()->migrateSchemaSets(['axiomasoft/corex:tenant'], 'pgsql');
});

it('inserts exactly one inbox row when two connections race on the same key', function (): void {

    $table = 'sys_inbox_messages';
    $consumer = 'inbox.concurrency.v1';
    $messageId = '55555555-5555-7555-8555-555555555555';
    $name = 'membership.expired';
    $processedAt = '2026-06-15T12:00:00+00:00';

    $connection = config('database.connections.pgsql');
    $dsn = "pgsql:host={$connection['host']};port={$connection['port']};dbname={$connection['database']}";
    $options = [PDO::ATTR_ERRMODE => PDO::ERRMODE_EXCEPTION];

    $first = new PDO($dsn, $connection['username'], $connection['password'], $options);
    $second = new PDO($dsn, $connection['username'], $connection['password'], $options);

    $sql = <<<SQL
        INSERT INTO {$table} (consumer, message_id, name, processed_at)
        VALUES (:consumer, :message_id::uuid, :name, :processed_at::timestamptz)
        ON CONFLICT DO NOTHING
        SQL;

    // Autocommit on two independent connections: no open txn on A while B inserts
    // (holding A's row uncommitted would block B forever in this single-threaded test).
    $stmtA = $first->prepare($sql);
    $stmtA->execute([
        'consumer' => $consumer,
        'message_id' => $messageId,
        'name' => $name,
        'processed_at' => $processedAt,
    ]);
    $insertedA = $stmtA->rowCount();

    $stmtB = $second->prepare($sql);
    $stmtB->execute([
        'consumer' => $consumer,
        'message_id' => $messageId,
        'name' => $name,
        'processed_at' => $processedAt,
    ]);
    $insertedB = $stmtB->rowCount();

    expect([$insertedA, $insertedB])->toContain(1)->toContain(0)
        ->and(DB::connection('pgsql')->table($table)->count())->toBe(1);
})->group('pg');


WHOLE PRODUCT FILE M packages/core/tests/Pg/MessagingSchemaTest.php
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

use Illuminate\Database\QueryException;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\DB;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Schema;

beforeEach(function (): void {
    config(['database.default' => 'pgsql']);
    DB::statement('DROP SCHEMA IF EXISTS public CASCADE');
    DB::statement('CREATE SCHEMA public');
    $this->migrateSchemaSets(['axiomasoft/corex:tenant'], 'pgsql');
});

it('creates messaging tables with expected columns', function (): void {
    expect(Schema::hasTable('sys_outbox_messages'))->toBeTrue()
        ->and(Schema::hasTable('sys_inbox_messages'))->toBeTrue();

    $outboxColumns = collect(Schema::getColumnListing('sys_outbox_messages'))->sort()->values()->all();
    $expectedOutbox = [
        'attempts',
        'available_at',
        'causation_id',
        'contract_version',
        'correlation_id',
        'created_at',
        'dead_at',
        'envelope_version',
        'failure_class',
        'id',
        'last_error',
        'lease_owner',
        'lease_until',
        'metadata',
        'name',
        'occurred_at',
        'payload',
        'pii_class',
        'published_at',
        'scope',
        'source',
        'status',
        'subject',
        'updated_at',
    ];
    sort($expectedOutbox);
    expect($outboxColumns)->toBe($expectedOutbox);

    $inboxColumns = collect(Schema::getColumnListing('sys_inbox_messages'))->sort()->values()->all();
    expect($inboxColumns)->toBe(['consumer', 'message_id', 'name', 'processed_at']);
})->group('pg');

it('defines a composite primary key on sys_inbox_messages', function (): void {
    $columns = DB::select(
        <<<'SQL'
        select kcu.column_name, kcu.ordinal_position
        from information_schema.table_constraints tc
        join information_schema.key_column_usage kcu
            on tc.constraint_name = kcu.constraint_name
            and tc.table_schema = kcu.table_schema
            and tc.table_name = kcu.table_name
        where tc.table_name = 'sys_inbox_messages'
          and tc.constraint_type = 'PRIMARY KEY'
        order by kcu.ordinal_position
        SQL,
    );

    expect(collect($columns)->pluck('column_name')->all())->toBe(['consumer', 'message_id']);
})->group('pg');

it('creates partial indexes on sys_outbox_messages hot paths', function (): void {
    $indexNames = [
        'sys_outbox_messages_claim_idx',
        'sys_outbox_messages_reclaim_idx',
        'sys_outbox_messages_published_idx',
        'sys_outbox_messages_dead_idx',
    ];

    foreach ($indexNames as $indexName) {
        $row = DB::selectOne(
            'select indexdef from pg_indexes where tablename = ? and indexname = ?',
            ['sys_outbox_messages', $indexName],
        );

        expect($row)->not->toBeNull()
            ->and(str_contains((string) $row->indexdef, 'WHERE'))->toBeTrue();
    }
})->group('pg');

it('indexes the shipped inbox table for retention', function (): void {
    expect(DB::table('pg_indexes')
        ->where('tablename', 'sys_inbox_messages')
        ->where('indexname', 'sys_inbox_messages_processed_idx')
        ->exists())->toBeTrue();
})->group('pg');

it('rejects duplicate inbox consumer and message_id pairs', function (): void {
    $now = now()->toIso8601String();

    DB::table('sys_inbox_messages')->insert([
        'consumer' => 'test-consumer',
        'message_id' => '01932f5a-0000-7000-8000-000000000001',
        'name' => 'example.event.v1',
        'processed_at' => $now,
    ]);

    DB::table('sys_inbox_messages')->insert([

        'message_id' => '01932f5a-0000-7000-8000-000000000001',
        'name' => 'example.event.v1',
        'processed_at' => $now,
    ]);
})->throws(QueryException::class)->group('pg');

WHOLE PRODUCT FILE M packages/core/tests/Pg/OutboxRelayCommandTest.php
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

use CoreX\Messaging\Contracts\DurableMessagePublisher;
use CoreX\Messaging\Contracts\MessagePublisher;
use CoreX\Messaging\Data\MessageEnvelope;
use CoreX\Messaging\Exceptions\OutboxLeaseLost;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Artisan;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\DB;
use Psr\Clock\ClockInterface;

function outboxRelayCommandBindPublisher(DurableMessagePublisher $publisher): void
{
    app()->instance(DurableMessagePublisher::class, $publisher);
    app()->instance(MessagePublisher::class, $publisher);
}

beforeEach(function (): void {
    outboxRelayMigrate();
    outboxRelayClock(new DateTimeImmutable('2026-06-15T12:00:00+00:00'));
    outboxAppendBindRegistry(outboxAppendDeclaredContract());
});

it('publishes two pending messages in one pass', function (): void {
    $publisher = new class implements DurableMessagePublisher
    {
        public function publish(MessageEnvelope $message): void {}
    };
    outboxRelayCommandBindPublisher($publisher);

    outboxRelayInsertRow(['id' => '01932f5a-0000-7000-8000-000000000021']);
    outboxRelayInsertRow(['id' => '01932f5a-0000-7000-8000-000000000022']);

    Artisan::call('corex:messaging:relay', ['--once' => true]);

    expect(Artisan::output())->toContain('published 2')
        ->and(DB::connection('pgsql')->table('sys_outbox_messages')->where('status', 'published')->count())->toBe(2);
})->group('pg');

it('defers the second message when the publisher throws', function (): void {
    $calls = 0;
    $publisher = new class($calls) implements DurableMessagePublisher
    {
        public function __construct(private int &$calls) {}

        public function publish(MessageEnvelope $message): void
        {
            $this->calls++;

            if ($this->calls === 2) {
                throw new RuntimeException('publish-failed');
            }
        }
    };
    outboxRelayCommandBindPublisher($publisher);

    outboxRelayInsertRow(['id' => '01932f5a-0000-7000-8000-000000000031']);
    outboxRelayInsertRow(['id' => '01932f5a-0000-7000-8000-000000000032']);

    $this->artisan('corex:messaging:relay', ['--once' => true])
        ->expectsOutputToContain('deferred 1')
        ->assertSuccessful();

    $now = app(ClockInterface::class)->now();
    $second = DB::connection('pgsql')->table('sys_outbox_messages')
        ->where('id', '01932f5a-0000-7000-8000-000000000032')
        ->first();

    $availableAt = new DateTimeImmutable((string) $second->available_at);

    expect(DB::connection('pgsql')->table('sys_outbox_messages')->where('status', 'published')->count())->toBe(1)
        ->and($second->status)->toBe('pending')
        ->and($availableAt->getTimestamp())->toBe($now->modify('+2 seconds')->getTimestamp());
})->group('pg');

it('warns on OutboxLeaseLost without deferring', function (): void {
    $calls = 0;
    $publisher = new class($calls) implements DurableMessagePublisher
    {
        public function __construct(private int &$calls) {}

        public function publish(MessageEnvelope $message): void
        {
            $this->calls++;

            if ($this->calls === 2) {
                throw OutboxLeaseLost::superseded($message->messageId);
            }
        }
    };
    outboxRelayCommandBindPublisher($publisher);

    outboxRelayInsertRow(['id' => '01932f5a-0000-7000-8000-000000000041']);
    outboxRelayInsertRow(['id' => '01932f5a-0000-7000-8000-000000000042']);

    $this->artisan('corex:messaging:relay', ['--once' => true])
        ->expectsOutputToContain('deferred 0')
        ->expectsOutputToContain('superseded')
        ->assertSuccessful();

    $second = DB::connection('pgsql')->table('sys_outbox_messages')
        ->where('id', '01932f5a-0000-7000-8000-000000000042')
        ->first();

    expect($second->status)->toBe('publishing');
})->group('pg');

it('runs relay once in the test environment', function (): void {
    $publisher = new class implements DurableMessagePublisher
    {
        public function publish(MessageEnvelope $message): void {}
    };
    outboxRelayCommandBindPublisher($publisher);

    $this->artisan('corex:messaging:relay', ['--once' => true])->assertSuccessful();
})->group('pg');


WHOLE PRODUCT FILE M packages/core/tests/Pg/OutboxRelayTest.php
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

use CoreX\Messaging\Data\LeasedMessage;
use CoreX\Messaging\Data\MessageEnvelope;
use CoreX\Messaging\Data\MessagePayload;
use CoreX\Messaging\Enums\PiiClass;
use CoreX\Messaging\Exceptions\OutboxLeaseLost;
use CoreX\Messaging\Internal\SqlOutboxRelay;
use CoreX\Time\Testing\FrozenClock;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\DB;
use Psr\Clock\ClockInterface;

const OUTBOX_RELAY_OWNER = 'aaaaaaaa-aaaa-4aaa-8aaa-aaaaaaaaaaaa';

const OUTBOX_RELAY_OTHER_OWNER = 'bbbbbbbb-bbbb-4bbb-8bbb-bbbbbbbbbbbb';

function outboxRelayMigrate(): void
{
    config(['database.default' => 'pgsql']);
    DB::statement('DROP SCHEMA IF EXISTS public CASCADE');
    DB::statement('CREATE SCHEMA public');
    test()->migrateSchemaSets(['axiomasoft/corex:tenant'], 'pgsql');
}

function outboxRelayClock(DateTimeImmutable $frozen): void
{
    app()->instance(ClockInterface::class, new FrozenClock($frozen));
}

function outboxRelayService(
    int $leaseSeconds = 120,
    int $maxAttempts = 8,
    int $backoffCapSeconds = 300,
): SqlOutboxRelay {
    return new SqlOutboxRelay(
        connection: DB::connection('pgsql'),
        clock: app(ClockInterface::class),
        leaseSeconds: $leaseSeconds,
        maxAttempts: $maxAttempts,
        backoffCapSeconds: $backoffCapSeconds,
    );
}

function outboxRelayInsertRow(array $overrides = []): string
{
    $payload = $overrides['payload'] ?? MessagePayload::empty();
    $storedEnvelopeVersion = $overrides['envelope_version'] ?? MessageEnvelope::ENVELOPE_VERSION;
    $envelope = new MessageEnvelope(
        messageId: $overrides['id'] ?? '01932f5a-0000-7000-8000-000000000001',
        source: 'axiomasoft/corex',
        name: 'membership.expired',
        contractVersion: 1,
        occurredAt: new DateTimeImmutable('2026-01-01T00:00:00Z'),
        scope: ['accountId' => 'account-1'],
        payload: $payload,
        piiClass: PiiClass::None,
        subject: 'subject-1',
        correlationId: 'corr-1',
        causationId: 'cause-1',
        metadata: ['trace' => 't1'],
        envelopeVersion: MessageEnvelope::ENVELOPE_VERSION,
    );
    $now = app(ClockInterface::class)->now();
    $id = $envelope->messageId;

    DB::table('sys_outbox_messages')->insert([
        'id' => $id,
        'name' => $envelope->name,
        'contract_version' => $envelope->contractVersion,
        'envelope_version' => $storedEnvelopeVersion,
        'source' => $envelope->source,
        'subject' => $envelope->subject,
        'correlation_id' => $envelope->correlationId,
        'causation_id' => $envelope->causationId,
        'pii_class' => $envelope->piiClass->value,
        'scope' => $overrides['scope_raw'] ?? json_encode($envelope->scope, JSON_THROW_ON_ERROR),
        'payload' => $overrides['payload_raw'] ?? $envelope->payload->encode(),
        'metadata' => json_encode($envelope->metadata, JSON_THROW_ON_ERROR),
        'occurred_at' => $envelope->occurredAt,
        'available_at' => $overrides['available_at'] ?? $now,
        'status' => $overrides['status'] ?? 'pending',
        'lease_owner' => $overrides['lease_owner'] ?? null,
        'lease_until' => $overrides['lease_until'] ?? null,
        'attempts' => $overrides['attempts'] ?? 0,
        'failure_class' => $overrides['failure_class'] ?? null,
        'last_error' => $overrides['last_error'] ?? null,
        'published_at' => $overrides['published_at'] ?? null,
        'dead_at' => $overrides['dead_at'] ?? null,
        'created_at' => $now,
        'updated_at' => $now,
    ]);

    return $id;
}

beforeEach(function (): void {
    outboxRelayMigrate();
    outboxRelayClock(new DateTimeImmutable('2026-06-15T12:00:00+00:00'));
});

it('leases a pending row into publishing with owner, lease window, and attempt count', function (): void {
    $id = outboxRelayInsertRow();
    $relay = outboxRelayService();
    $now = app(ClockInterface::class)->now();

    $leased = $relay->lease(leaseOwner: OUTBOX_RELAY_OWNER, batchSize: 10);

    expect($leased)->toHaveCount(1)
        ->and($leased[0])->toBeInstanceOf(LeasedMessage::class)
        ->and($leased[0]->leaseOwner)->toBe(OUTBOX_RELAY_OWNER)
        ->and($leased[0]->attempt)->toBe(1)
        ->and($leased[0]->leaseUntil->getTimestamp())->toBe($now->modify('+120 seconds')->getTimestamp())
        ->and($leased[0]->envelope->messageId)->toBe($id);

    $row = DB::table('sys_outbox_messages')->where('id', $id)->first();

    expect($row->status)->toBe('publishing')
        ->and($row->lease_owner)->toBe(OUTBOX_RELAY_OWNER)
        ->and($row->attempts)->toBe(1);
})->group('pg');

it('does not lease rows with a future available_at', function (): void {
    $now = app(ClockInterface::class)->now();
    outboxRelayInsertRow(['available_at' => $now->modify('+1 hour')]);

    $leased = outboxRelayService()->lease(leaseOwner: OUTBOX_RELAY_OWNER, batchSize: 10);

    expect($leased)->toBe([])
        ->and(DB::table('sys_outbox_messages')->value('status'))->toBe('pending');
})->group('pg');

it('reclaims expired publishing rows, marks exhausted leases dead, and fences corrupt envelopes', function (): void {
    $now = app(ClockInterface::class)->now();
    $reclaimId = outboxRelayInsertRow([
        'id' => '01932f5a-0000-7000-8000-000000000002',
        'status' => 'publishing',
        'lease_owner' => OUTBOX_RELAY_OTHER_OWNER,
        'lease_until' => $now->modify('-1 minute'),
        'attempts' => 2,
    ]);
    $deadId = outboxRelayInsertRow([
        'id' => '01932f5a-0000-7000-8000-000000000003',
        'status' => 'publishing',
        'lease_owner' => OUTBOX_RELAY_OTHER_OWNER,
        'lease_until' => $now->modify('-1 minute'),
        'attempts' => 8,
    ]);
    $relay = outboxRelayService(maxAttempts: 8);
    $leased = $relay->lease(leaseOwner: OUTBOX_RELAY_OWNER, batchSize: 1);

    expect($leased)->toHaveCount(1)
        ->and($leased[0]->envelope->messageId)->toBe($reclaimId)
        ->and($leased[0]->attempt)->toBe(3);

    $relay->markPublished($leased[0]);

    $deadRow = DB::table('sys_outbox_messages')->where('id', $deadId)->first();
    expect($deadRow->status)->toBe('dead')
        ->and($deadRow->failure_class)->toBe('lease-expiry-attempts-exhausted');
})->group('pg');

it('fences corrupt envelope reconstruction through the same backoff budget', function (): void {
    $corruptId = outboxRelayInsertRow([
        'id' => '01932f5a-0000-7000-8000-000000000004',
        'scope_raw' => '[]',
    ]);
    $relay = outboxRelayService(maxAttempts: 1);

    expect($relay->lease(leaseOwner: OUTBOX_RELAY_OWNER, batchSize: 1))->toBe([]);

    $row = DB::table('sys_outbox_messages')->where('id', $corruptId)->first();
    expect($row->status)->toBe('dead')
        ->and($row->attempts)->toBe(1)
        ->and($row->failure_class)->toBe('attempts-exhausted');
})->group('pg');

it('refuses markPublished when the lease owner no longer matches', function (): void {
    $id = outboxRelayInsertRow();
    $relay = outboxRelayService();
    $leased = $relay->lease(leaseOwner: OUTBOX_RELAY_OWNER, batchSize: 1)[0];

    $wrongOwner = new LeasedMessage(
        envelope: $leased->envelope,
        leaseOwner: OUTBOX_RELAY_OTHER_OWNER,
        attempt: $leased->attempt,
        leaseUntil: $leased->leaseUntil,
    );

    expect(fn (): mixed => $relay->markPublished($wrongOwner))
        ->toThrow(OutboxLeaseLost::class, 'may already have reached its destination');

    expect(DB::table('sys_outbox_messages')->where('id', $id)->value('status'))->toBe('publishing');
})->group('pg');

function outboxRelayLeasedMessageFromRow(string $id, int $attempt): LeasedMessage
{
    $row = DB::table('sys_outbox_messages')->where('id', $id)->first();
    $envelope = new MessageEnvelope(
        messageId: (string) $row->id,
        source: (string) $row->source,
        name: (string) $row->name,
        contractVersion: (int) $row->contract_version,
        occurredAt: new DateTimeImmutable((string) $row->occurred_at),
        scope: json_decode((string) $row->scope, true, flags: JSON_THROW_ON_ERROR),
        payload: MessagePayload::empty(),
        piiClass: PiiClass::from((string) $row->pii_class),
        subject: $row->subject === null ? null : (string) $row->subject,
        correlationId: $row->correlation_id === null ? null : (string) $row->correlation_id,
        causationId: $row->causation_id === null ? null : (string) $row->causation_id,
        metadata: json_decode((string) $row->metadata, true, flags: JSON_THROW_ON_ERROR),
        envelopeVersion: (int) $row->envelope_version,
    );

    return new LeasedMessage(
        envelope: $envelope,
        leaseOwner: (string) $row->lease_owner,
        attempt: $attempt,
        leaseUntil: new DateTimeImmutable((string) $row->lease_until),
    );
}

function outboxRelaySeedPublishing(string $id, int $attempt): void
{
    $now = app(ClockInterface::class)->now();
    outboxRelayInsertRow(['id' => $id, 'attempts' => $attempt]);
    $updated = DB::table('sys_outbox_messages')->where('id', $id)->update([
        'status' => 'publishing',
        'lease_owner' => OUTBOX_RELAY_OWNER,
        'lease_until' => $now->modify('+120 seconds'),
        'updated_at' => $now,
    ]);

    expect($updated)->toBe(1);
}

it('applies deterministic exponential backoff on the first four publish failures', function (): void {
    $relay = outboxRelayService(maxAttempts: 8);
    $baseline = new DateTimeImmutable('2026-06-15T12:00:00+00:00');
    outboxRelayClock($baseline);
    $expectedDelays = [2, 4, 8, 16];

    $messageIds = [
        '01932f5a-0000-7000-8000-000000000011',
        '01932f5a-0000-7000-8000-000000000012',
        '01932f5a-0000-7000-8000-000000000013',
        '01932f5a-0000-7000-8000-000000000014',
    ];

    foreach ($expectedDelays as $attempt => $delay) {
        $messageId = $messageIds[$attempt];
        outboxRelaySeedPublishing(id: $messageId, attempt: $attempt + 1);

        $relay->releaseWithBackoff(
            outboxRelayLeasedMessageFromRow(id: $messageId, attempt: $attempt + 1),
            new RuntimeException('publish-failed'),
        );

        $availableAt = new DateTimeImmutable((string) DB::table('sys_outbox_messages')->where('id', $messageId)->value('available_at'));
        expect($availableAt->getTimestamp())->toBe($baseline->modify("+{$delay} seconds")->getTimestamp());
    }
})->group('pg');

it('marks a row dead after the eighth failed attempt', function (): void {
    $now = app(ClockInterface::class)->now();
    $id = outboxRelayInsertRow([
        'id' => '01932f5a-0000-7000-8000-000000000006',
        'attempts' => 7,
        'available_at' => $now,
    ]);
    $relay = outboxRelayService(maxAttempts: 8);

    $leased = $relay->lease(leaseOwner: OUTBOX_RELAY_OWNER, batchSize: 1);
    expect($leased)->toHaveCount(1)
        ->and($leased[0]->attempt)->toBe(8);

    $relay->releaseWithBackoff($leased[0], new RuntimeException('publish-failed'));

    $row = DB::table('sys_outbox_messages')->where('id', $id)->first();
    expect($row->status)->toBe('dead')
        ->and($row->dead_at)->not->toBeNull()
        ->and($row->failure_class)->toBe('attempts-exhausted');
})->group('pg');

it('truncates last_error to two thousand characters', function (): void {
    outboxRelayInsertRow(['id' => '01932f5a-0000-7000-8000-000000000008']);
    $relay = outboxRelayService();
    $leased = $relay->lease(leaseOwner: OUTBOX_RELAY_OWNER, batchSize: 1)[0];

    $relay->releaseWithBackoff($leased, new RuntimeException(str_repeat('x', 2_500)));

    $error = (string) DB::table('sys_outbox_messages')->value('last_error');
    expect(mb_strlen($error))->toBe(2_000);
})->group('pg');

it('skips locked pending rows with SKIP LOCKED and blocks without it', function (): void {
    $id = outboxRelayInsertRow(['id' => '01932f5a-0000-7000-8000-000000000007']);
    $table = 'sys_outbox_messages';
    $connection = config('database.connections.pgsql');
    $dsn = "pgsql:host={$connection['host']};port={$connection['port']};dbname={$connection['database']}";
    $second = new PDO($dsn, $connection['username'], $connection['password'], [PDO::ATTR_ERRMODE => PDO::ERRMODE_EXCEPTION]);

    DB::beginTransaction();
    DB::table($table)->where('id', $id)->lock('for update skip locked')->first();

    $skipped = $second->query(
        "SELECT id FROM {$table} WHERE status = 'pending' FOR UPDATE SKIP LOCKED",
    )->fetchAll();

    expect($skipped)->toBe([]);

    $second->exec("SET lock_timeout = '100ms'");

    try {
        expect(fn (): mixed => $second->query("SELECT id FROM {$table} WHERE id = '{$id}' FOR UPDATE"))
            ->toThrow(PDOException::class);
    } finally {
        DB::rollBack();
    }
})->group('pg');


WHOLE PRODUCT FILE M packages/custom-fields/CHANGELOG.md
# Changelog

## Unreleased — deterministic schema (P08.2)

- Remove the tables configuration and internal Options table map; storage tables use package-owned literals while AttributeTables remains available for explicit composition.

## Unreleased — thin providers and registrars (P05.2)

- Schema delivery now uses tagged `SchemaSetProvider` declarations and `corex:schema:migrate`; migration publishing is removed.

- `AttributesServiceProvider` delegates to messaging, composition and bindings registrars; multi-statement closures moved to `AttributeStoreFactory`, `SchemaOperationsFactory` and `FieldCatalogueFactory`. With the kernel provider the module registry is always bound, so `SchemaOperations` resolves to the queued DDL implementation.

## Unreleased — typed Options and runtime configuration checks (P05.1)

- Added internal `CustomFieldsOptions`, a lazy singleton resolved in `boot()` that carries the validated composition and table map; `AttributeTables` and the composition are built from it.

## Unreleased — kernel package and API migration

- Breaking (PLAN3.P02–P04, pre-1.0): AttributeStore, FieldCatalogue and SchemaOperations expose AttributeId, AttributeDefinition, FieldSelection and write-result DTOs; physical attribute storage and catalogue implementation are Internal.
- Package installation uses the `axiomasoft` Composer vendor and PHP 8.4–8.5 / Laravel 12–13 constraints; `resources/contracts/php-api.json` records the explicitly attributed API and SPI. Old names have no compatibility aliases.

## Unreleased — PHP surface strict cutover

- Breaking (PLAN3.P04, pre-1.0): supported PHP symbols are declared individually with Api/Spi attributes. Concrete implementation types moved to Internal and typed ports/DTOs and error contracts replace predecessor surfaces as recorded in PLAN3.D63/D64; namespace metadata no longer grants compatibility.
- Generated `resources/contracts/php-api.json` records supported layers and signatures. Strict source/graph/BC checks enforce the declared surface; Internal has no external BC promise.

## Unreleased — typed error contract (P04.3)

- Added package owned error enums, definitions and safe coded exception descriptors.
- Error constructors require enum identities; existing error wire values are scoped.
- Contributions are validated and frozen at boot; persisted identities use validated lookup.

## Unreleased — package identifier alignment

### Changed

- PLAN3.D57: implementation classes moved to `<Module>\Internal\…`, public types to module categories
  (`Contracts|Data|Enums|Events|Exceptions`); see `docs/architecture/constitution.md` STR-003. Drain queues before
  upgrading; stored `*_type` values holding an old model FQCN no longer resolve (no aliases, PLAN3.D01).

- Breaking before 1.0: Rename the config file and key to `corex-custom-fields`.

## Unreleased

- Declare Symfony Process as a development dependency used by package tests.

- PHP API manifests now record direct inheritance, interfaces and traits separately and include only members declared by this package; external dependency members are omitted without changing the `php-api/1` schema.

- Added owned neutral JSONB/EAV attribute storage, active EntityRegistry field binding, and typed value validation.
- Added durable schema-operation requests and a restore-safe asynchronous PostgreSQL concurrent-index worker.


WHOLE PRODUCT FILE M packages/custom-fields/README.md
# axiomasoft/corex-custom-fields

Typed PostgreSQL attribute-store, filter, and schema-operation ports for CoreX.

`axiomasoft/corex-custom-fields` is a neutral owned engine. It reads active scalar declarations from the existing
`EntityRegistry`/`ModuleRegistry`; it never creates a second field registry or product dependency.
Its only tables are additive `sys_attribute_*` tables.

The application selects either `jsonb` or `eav` storage. Both drivers enforce a trusted `DataScope`,
including the physical `pgsql:<database>` identity, authorization callback, record version and write
token. Reads and writes always validate the active field snapshot and all fifteen supported scalar
types when `axiomasoft/corex` is installed and both registries are bound.

Without `axiomasoft/corex`, `FieldCatalogue`, `AttributeStore`, and `SchemaOperations` resolve to
`UnavailableAttributes`. Dynamic attribute fields are unavailable in that configuration; this is an
observable fail-closed state, not a silent degradation.

Schema requests persist an idempotent operation only. They do not execute SQL in the request path.
A trusted asynchronous worker invokes `SchemaOperationWorker` with a fresh `DataScope`; it claims
the operation transactionally, executes PostgreSQL concurrent index DDL outside that transaction,
checks the catalog, preserves a 30-day retirement grace period before a managed-index drop, and
denies stale restore epochs. The application is responsible for dispatching that worker from its
queue or console boundary; it must never be called from HTTP handling.

## Installation

Install with `composer require axiomasoft/corex-custom-fields` through the configured CoreX channel. Provide a trusted PostgreSQL tenant connection and apply the package-owned `sys_attribute_*` migrations before enabling writes. Bind the module/entity registries when dynamic attribute definitions are required; without them the package reports its unavailable state.

## Configuration

`corex-custom-fields.storage_driver` defaults to `jsonb`; select `eav` when needed. Use
`corex-custom-fields.storage_by_entity` to map individual entities to either driver. The application
controls these settings and the `drivers` enablement map. `sources` selects definition sources
(`static` by default), while `definitions` maps entities to lists of field definitions.

Package-owned `sys_attribute_*` table names are literal in the migrations and composed into an
immutable `AttributeTables` object at provider registration. Host configuration does not rename
these tables; explicit storage composition can still use the validated `AttributeTables` DTO.

## Extension contracts

Use the exported `FieldCatalogue`, `AttributeStoreResolver`, `AttributeStore`, `AttributeFilter` and `SchemaOperations` ports. The host supplies its trusted scope and authorization callback. Replace the definition source through the catalogue boundary; storage implementations and worker internals are not extension points.

## Typed errors

Package error identities use backed enums and a boot compiled kernel registry.
Error constructors require `ErrorCode`; descriptors keep scoped string wire codes.
See the monorepo [error contract](../../docs/reference/errors.md) for contributions,
validated replay and safe mapping.

## Schema installation

Register the package provider, then run `php artisan corex:schema:migrate --scope=tenant --database=<tenant>`. Module sets are installed by `ModuleLifecycle::enable()`; template preparation includes them with `--modules`. Inspect the journal with `corex:schema:status`.
