WHOLE PRODUCT FILE M packages/tenancy/database/migrations/tenant/2026_07_26_190004_add_cross_package_fks_table.php
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

use Illuminate\Database\Migrations\Migration;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\DB;

// Final integration migration of the tenant-DB chain (C4/D14, audit F17):
// cross-package FKs the owning packages' own DDL deliberately omit (inline
// FK would fix a publication order between axiomasoft/corex, axiomasoft/corex-tenancy and
// axiomasoft/corex-access that isn't guaranteed — DB_SCHEMA.md §Конфликты C4, resolved
// variant "б"). All four land here, on whichever package's migration runs
// last in the golden-template chain:
//   1) users.department_id            → sys_departments   (nullable, SET NULL)
//   2) sys_departments.workspace_id    → wsp_workspaces     (NOT NULL, RESTRICT)
//   3) sys_departments.head_user_id    → users              (nullable, SET NULL)
//   4) sys_department_user.user_id     → users              (PK column, CASCADE)
return new class extends Migration
{
    public function up(): void
    {
        $departments = 'sys_departments';
        $departmentUser = 'sys_department_user';

        DB::statement("ALTER TABLE users ADD CONSTRAINT users_department_fk FOREIGN KEY (department_id) REFERENCES {$departments} (id) ON DELETE SET NULL");
        DB::statement("ALTER TABLE {$departments} ADD CONSTRAINT sys_dept_workspace_fk FOREIGN KEY (workspace_id) REFERENCES wsp_workspaces (id) ON DELETE RESTRICT");
        DB::statement("ALTER TABLE {$departments} ADD CONSTRAINT sys_dept_head_user_fk FOREIGN KEY (head_user_id) REFERENCES users (id) ON DELETE SET NULL");
        DB::statement("ALTER TABLE {$departmentUser} ADD CONSTRAINT sys_du_user_fk FOREIGN KEY (user_id) REFERENCES users (id) ON DELETE CASCADE");
    }

    public function down(): void
    {
        $departments = 'sys_departments';
        $departmentUser = 'sys_department_user';

        DB::statement("ALTER TABLE {$departmentUser} DROP CONSTRAINT sys_du_user_fk");
        DB::statement("ALTER TABLE {$departments} DROP CONSTRAINT sys_dept_head_user_fk");
        DB::statement("ALTER TABLE {$departments} DROP CONSTRAINT sys_dept_workspace_fk");
        DB::statement('ALTER TABLE users DROP CONSTRAINT users_department_fk');
    }
};


WHOLE PRODUCT FILE M packages/tenancy/src/Identity/Internal/Models/User.php
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

namespace CoreX\Tenancy\Identity\Internal\Models;

use Carbon\CarbonImmutable;
use Illuminate\Database\Eloquent\Model;
use Illuminate\Database\Eloquent\SoftDeletes;
use Illuminate\Support\Carbon;

/**
 * Tenant-DB projection of `root_identities` (B-11 §2.2, full denorm D4/C6).
 * Cannot extend `CoreX\Database\Laravel\BaseModel` (its own docblock carves out
 * exactly this case): `id` is external (= root_identities.id, no generated
 * default) — automatic UUID generation would fight that.
 *
 * @property string $id
 * @property string $email
 * @property string $name
 * @property ?string $phone
 * @property string $locale
 * @property ?string $password_hash
 * @property ?string $department_id
 * @property bool $is_active
 * @property array<string, mixed> $settings
 * @property ?Carbon $last_login_at
 * @property ?Carbon $synced_at
 * @property ?CarbonImmutable $identity_source_updated_at
 */
final class User extends Model
{
    use SoftDeletes;

    public $incrementing = false;

    protected $keyType = 'string';

    protected $dateFormat = 'Y-m-d H:i:s.u';

    protected $hidden = [
        'password_hash',
    ];

    protected $fillable = [
        'id',
        'email',
        'name',
        'phone',
        'locale',
        'department_id',
        'is_active',
        'settings',
        'last_login_at',
        'synced_at',
        'identity_source_updated_at',
    ];

    protected $casts = [
        'is_active' => 'boolean',
        'settings' => 'array',
        'last_login_at' => 'datetime',
        'synced_at' => 'datetime',
        'identity_source_updated_at' => 'immutable_datetime',
    ];
}


WHOLE PRODUCT FILE M packages/tenancy/src/Workspace/Internal/Models/Invitation.php
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

namespace CoreX\Tenancy\Workspace\Internal\Models;

use Carbon\CarbonImmutable;
use CoreX\Tenancy\Identity\Laravel\UserRelations;
use CoreX\Tenancy\Workspace\Exceptions\InvitationNotAcceptableException;
use Illuminate\Database\Eloquent\Concerns\HasUuids;
use Illuminate\Database\Eloquent\Model;
use Illuminate\Database\Eloquent\Relations\BelongsTo;

/**
 * `wsp_invitations` — invitation into a workspace (or the account, when
 * `workspace_id` is null). `role_id` is a soft-ref with no FK (D21): the
 * role is only resolved/applied on acceptance, application-side. No soft
 * deletes — the table carries no `deleted_at` (B-11 §2.2).
 *
 * @property string $id
 * @property string $email
 * @property ?string $workspace_id
 * @property ?string $role_id
 * @property string $invited_by
 * @property string $token_hash
 * @property string $status
 * @property CarbonImmutable $expires_at
 * @property ?CarbonImmutable $accepted_at
 * @property CarbonImmutable $created_at
 * @property CarbonImmutable $updated_at
 */
final class Invitation extends Model
{
    use HasUuids;

    protected $table = 'wsp_invitations';

    /**
     * Mirrors the DB defaults (`status` DEFAULT 'pending'): a
     * non-incrementing model's create() does not re-fetch the inserted row,
     * so without this the in-memory attribute would stay unset even though
     * the DB column already holds 'pending'.
     *
     * @var array<string, mixed>
     */
    protected $attributes = ['status' => 'pending'];

    /** @var list<string> */
    protected $fillable = ['email', 'workspace_id', 'role_id', 'invited_by', 'token_hash', 'status', 'expires_at', 'accepted_at'];

    /** @var list<string> */
    protected $hidden = ['token_hash'];

    /** @return BelongsTo<Workspace, $this> */
    public function workspace(): BelongsTo
    {
        return $this->belongsTo(Workspace::class);
    }

    /** @return BelongsTo<covariant \Illuminate\Database\Eloquent\Model, $this> */
    public function invitedBy(): BelongsTo
    {
        return UserRelations::belongsTo($this, 'invited_by', null, 'invitedBy');
    }

    /** @return array<string, string> */
    protected function casts(): array
    {
        return ['expires_at' => 'datetime', 'accepted_at' => 'datetime'];
    }

    /**
     * Accept the invitation as `$userId` (AC-19): revoked/already-accepted/
     * (persisted-or-lazily) expired → not acceptable; `$usersMax` (from the
     * account's `limits`) is checked against the workspace's active member
     * count before creating the membership. Role assignment lands with
     * axiomasoft/corex-access's `aut_role_assignments` (P2.8, Scope Excluded here) —
     * `role_id` is only carried on the invitation row.
     */
    public function accept(string $userId, int $usersMax): ?WorkspaceMember
    {
        return $this->getConnection()->transaction(function () use ($userId, $usersMax): ?WorkspaceMember {
            $invitation = $this->newQuery()->whereKey($this->getKey())->lockForUpdate()->firstOrFail();
            $this->setRawAttributes($invitation->getAttributes(), true);

            if ($this->status !== 'pending' || $this->expires_at->isPast()) {
                throw new InvitationNotAcceptableException("Invitation [{$this->id}] is not acceptable (status={$this->status}).");
            }

            if ($this->workspace_id === null) {
                $this->update(['status' => 'accepted', 'accepted_at' => $this->freshTimestamp()]);

                return null;
            }
            // All invitations for this workspace compete for the same quota resource.
            Workspace::on($this->getConnectionName())->whereKey($this->workspace_id)->lockForUpdate()->firstOrFail();
            $currentMembers = WorkspaceMember::on($this->getConnectionName())->where('workspace_id', $this->workspace_id)->where('status', 'active')->count();

            if ($currentMembers >= $usersMax) {
                throw new InvitationNotAcceptableException("Workspace [{$this->workspace_id}] has reached its users_max limit ({$usersMax}).");
            }
            $member = WorkspaceMember::on($this->getConnectionName())->create(['workspace_id' => $this->workspace_id, 'user_id' => $userId, 'status' => 'active']);
            $this->update(['status' => 'accepted', 'accepted_at' => $this->freshTimestamp()]);

            return $member;
        });
    }
}


WHOLE PRODUCT FILE M packages/tenancy/src/Workspace/Internal/Models/WorkspaceMember.php
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

namespace CoreX\Tenancy\Workspace\Internal\Models;

use Carbon\CarbonImmutable;
use CoreX\Tenancy\Identity\Laravel\UserRelations;
use Illuminate\Database\Eloquent\Concerns\HasUuids;
use Illuminate\Database\Eloquent\Model;
use Illuminate\Database\Eloquent\Relations\BelongsTo;

/**
 * `wsp_workspace_members` — fast admission flag for the resolver (403
 * without an active row); roles are NOT stored here (axiomasoft/corex-access owns
 * aut_role_assignments, P2.8). No soft deletes — the table carries no
 * `deleted_at` (B-11 §2.2).
 *
 * @property string $id
 * @property string $workspace_id
 * @property string $user_id
 * @property string $status
 * @property bool $is_default
 * @property CarbonImmutable $joined_at
 * @property CarbonImmutable $created_at
 * @property CarbonImmutable $updated_at
 */
final class WorkspaceMember extends Model
{
    use HasUuids;

    protected $table = 'wsp_workspace_members';

    /** @var list<string> */
    protected $fillable = ['workspace_id', 'user_id', 'status', 'is_default', 'joined_at'];

    /** @return BelongsTo<Workspace, $this> */
    public function workspace(): BelongsTo
    {
        return $this->belongsTo(Workspace::class);
    }

    /** @return BelongsTo<covariant \Illuminate\Database\Eloquent\Model, $this> */
    public function user(): BelongsTo
    {
        return UserRelations::belongsTo($this, 'user_id', null, 'user');
    }

    /** @return array<string, string> */
    protected function casts(): array
    {
        return ['is_default' => 'boolean', 'joined_at' => 'datetime'];
    }
}


WHOLE PRODUCT FILE M packages/tenancy/tests/Arch/NoCrossDbFkTest.php
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);
use CoreX\Tenancy\Internal\TenancySchemaSets;

// B-11 §7.3 п.2 / AC-29 — тенантские таблицы не ссылаются на root и наоборот.
//
// Cross-DB FK физически невозможен (root-БД и БД аккаунта — разные базы, D14),
// поэтому объявленный FK через границу не падает на код-ревью, а падает на
// провижининге аккаунта или, хуже, молча ломает клон golden-шаблона. Ловим
// статически.
//
// Плюс arch-тест «migration-path partition» (pre-mortem A4, Scope Included
// P2.13): файл миграции классифицируется root|tenant ПО ДИРЕКТОРИИ, множества
// путей central- и tenant-раннеров дизъюнктны, `corex:tenancy:migrate` грузит только
// tenant-пути — так root_-таблица в тенант-БД (и наоборот) невозможна по
// конструкции, а не по внимательности автора миграции.
//
// Разрешение имён — по строковым литералам и известным таблицам моделей.
// Любая динамическая цель требует ручного разбора и краснит гейт, если её нет
// в явном allowlist ниже. После D112 конфиг-driven целей в миграциях нет.

$packageRoot = \dirname(__DIR__, 2);
$repoRoot = \dirname($packageRoot, 2);

$rootLayerDir = $packageRoot.'/database/migrations/root';
$tenantLayerDir = $packageRoot.'/database/migrations/tenant';

/** Единственная директория миграций центральной (root) БД во всём репозитории. */
$isRootLayer = static fn (string $file): bool => str_contains(
    str_replace('\\', '/', $file),
    '/database/migrations/root/',
);

/**
 * `*.php` под $dir (рекурсивно), отсортированные.
 *
 * @return list<string>
 */
$migrationFiles = static function (string $dir): array {
    if (! is_dir($dir)) {
        return [];
    }

    $files = [];

    /** @var SplFileInfo $file */
    foreach (new RecursiveIteratorIterator(new RecursiveDirectoryIterator($dir, FilesystemIterator::SKIP_DOTS)) as $file) {
        if ($file->getExtension() === 'php') {
            $files[] = $file->getPathname();
        }
    }

    sort($files);

    return $files;
};

/**
 * Цели FK, объявленные в исходнике: `->references('id')->on('X')` и
 * `->constrained('X')` (Laravel-формы). Возвращает пару списков: строковые
 * литералы и НЕразрешимые статически выражения.
 *
 * @return array{literal: list<string>, dynamic: list<string>}
 */
// Resolve only explicit imports with a plain literal model table in owning
// production PSR-4 source. No eval, model construction, query, or wildcard
// exception: unresolved/dynamic expressions still fail the inventory gate.
$literalModelTables = static function (string $source) use ($repoRoot): array {
    $prefixes = [];
    foreach (glob($repoRoot.'/packages/*/composer.json') ?: [] as $manifest) {
        $composer = json_decode((string) file_get_contents($manifest), true, flags: JSON_THROW_ON_ERROR);
        foreach ($composer['autoload']['psr-4'] ?? [] as $prefix => $paths) {
            foreach ((array) $paths as $path) {
                $prefixes[$prefix][] = dirname($manifest).'/'.$path;
            }
        }
    }
    preg_match_all('/^use ([a-zA-Z_\\\\][a-zA-Z0-9_\\\\]*)(?: as ([a-zA-Z_][a-zA-Z0-9_]*))?;/m', $source, $imports, PREG_SET_ORDER);
    $tables = [];
    foreach ($imports as $import) {
        $class = $import[1];
        $alias = $import[2] ?? substr($class, (int) strrpos($class, '\\') + 1);
        foreach ($prefixes as $prefix => $directories) {
            if (! str_starts_with($class, $prefix)) {
                continue;
            }
            foreach ($directories as $directory) {
                $file = $directory.'/'.str_replace('\\', '/', substr($class, strlen($prefix))).'.php';

                if (! is_file($file)) {
                    continue;
                }
                $model = (string) file_get_contents($file);

                if (! preg_match('/function\s+(?:getTable|__construct)\s*\(/', $model)
                    && preg_match('/protected\s+\$table\s*=\s*([\'"])([a-zA-Z_][a-zA-Z0-9_]*)\1\s*;/', $model, $table)) {
                    $tables[$alias] = $table[2];
                }
            }
        }
    }

    return $tables;
};

$foreignKeyTargets = static function (string $source) use ($literalModelTables): array {
    $literal = [];
    $dynamic = [];
    $modelTables = $literalModelTables($source);

    // Аргумент допускает ОДИН уровень вложенных скобок — этого хватает на
    // произвольное выражение с вызовом функции и не даёт регэксу оборваться на её `)`.
    $argument = '(?:[^(),]|\([^()]*\))*';

    preg_match_all(
        '/->on\(\s*('.$argument.')\s*[,)]|->constrained\(\s*('.$argument.')\s*[,)]/',
        $source,
        $matches,
        PREG_SET_ORDER,
    );

    foreach ($matches as $match) {
        $target = $match[1] !== '' ? $match[1] : ($match[2] ?? '');

        if ($target === '') {
            // `->constrained()` без аргумента — цель выводится Laravel'ом из
            // имени колонки; кросс-слойной она быть не может (в этом дереве
            // такой формы нет, но пропускать её молча нельзя).
            continue;
        }

        if (preg_match('/^([\'"])(.+)\1$/', $target, $quoted) === 1) {
            $literal[] = $quoted[2];

            continue;
        }

        if (preg_match('/^\(new ([a-zA-Z_][a-zA-Z0-9_]*)\)->getTable\(\)$/D', trim($target), $model) === 1 && isset($modelTables[$model[1]])) {
            $literal[] = $modelTables[$model[1]];

            continue;
        }

        $dynamic[] = $target;
    }

    return ['literal' => $literal, 'dynamic' => $dynamic];
};

/**
 * Таблицы, создаваемые миграцией: `Schema::create('X'` (только литералы —
 * конфиг-driven имена в axiomasoft/corex проверяются директорией, не именем).
 *
 * @return list<string>
 */
$createdTables = static function (string $source): array {
    preg_match_all('/Schema::create\(\s*([\'"])(.+?)\1/', $source, $matches);

    return $matches[2];
};

// D112: shipped migrations use literal targets; no dynamic exceptions remain.
$allowedDynamicTargets = [];

/**
 * Все миграции репозитория (в split-зеркале — только собственные).
 *
 * @return list<string>
 */
$allMigrations = static function () use ($repoRoot, $packageRoot, $migrationFiles): array {
    $roots = is_dir($repoRoot.'/packages/core/database')
        ? (array) glob($repoRoot.'/packages/*/database/migrations', GLOB_ONLYDIR)
        : [$packageRoot.'/database/migrations'];

    $files = [];

    foreach ($roots as $dir) {
        $files = [...$files, ...$migrationFiles((string) $dir)];
    }

    sort($files);

    return $files;
};

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Анти-вакуум: детекторы обязаны детектировать.
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

test('the FK-target detector reads both Laravel forms and flags dynamic targets', function () use ($foreignKeyTargets): void {
    $sample = <<<'PHP'
    $table->foreign('account_id')->references('id')->on('root_accounts')->cascadeOnDelete();
    $table->foreignUuid('flag_id')->constrained($configuredTable)->cascadeOnDelete();
    $table->foreignUuid('stage_id')->constrained('crm_stages')->restrictOnDelete();
    PHP;

    expect($foreignKeyTargets($sample))->toBe([
        'literal' => ['root_accounts', 'crm_stages'],
        'dynamic' => ['$configuredTable'],
    ]);
});

test('literal model table resolution retains root detection and rejects unknown expressions', function () use ($foreignKeyTargets): void {
    $sample = <<<'PHP'
    use CoreX\Tenancy\Account\Internal\Models\Account;
    use CoreX\Tenancy\Workspace\Internal\Models\Workspace as Scope;
    $table->foreign('account_id')->references('id')->on((new Account)->getTable());
    $table->foreign('workspace_id')->constrained((new Scope)->getTable());
    $table->foreign('unknown_id')->constrained((new Unknown)->getTable());
    PHP;
    expect($foreignKeyTargets($sample))->toBe(['literal' => ['root_accounts', 'wsp_workspaces'], 'dynamic' => ['(new Unknown)->getTable()']]);
});

test('the migration inventory is not empty in either layer', function () use ($migrationFiles, $rootLayerDir, $tenantLayerDir, $allMigrations): void {
    // Пустой инвентарь дал бы зелёные гейты ниже, ничего не проверив.
    expect(\count($migrationFiles($rootLayerDir)))->toBeGreaterThan(10)
        ->and(\count($migrationFiles($tenantLayerDir)))->toBeGreaterThan(3)
        ->and(\count($allMigrations()))->toBeGreaterThan(\count($migrationFiles($rootLayerDir)));
});

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// AC-29 (п.2) — нет FK через границу БД.
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

test('root-layer migrations declare foreign keys only to root_ tables', function () use ($migrationFiles, $rootLayerDir, $foreignKeyTargets): void {
    $offenders = [];
    $seen = 0;

    foreach ($migrationFiles($rootLayerDir) as $file) {
        $targets = $foreignKeyTargets((string) file_get_contents($file));

        foreach ($targets['literal'] as $target) {
            $seen++;

            if (! str_starts_with($target, 'root_')) {
                $offenders[] = basename($file).' → '.$target;
            }
        }

        foreach ($targets['dynamic'] as $target) {
            // В центральной БД конфиг-driven цель недопустима в принципе:
            // разрешить её означало бы разрешить FK на что угодно.
            $offenders[] = basename($file).' → (dynamic) '.$target;
        }
    }

    expect($seen)->toBeGreaterThan(10)
        ->and($offenders)->toBe([]);
});

test('tenant-layer migrations declare no foreign key to a root_ table', function () use ($allMigrations, $isRootLayer, $foreignKeyTargets, $allowedDynamicTargets): void {
    $offenders = [];
    $unknownDynamic = [];
    $seen = 0;

    foreach ($allMigrations() as $file) {
        if ($isRootLayer($file)) {
            continue;
        }

        $targets = $foreignKeyTargets((string) file_get_contents($file));

        foreach ($targets['literal'] as $target) {
            $seen++;

            if (str_starts_with($target, 'root_')) {
                $offenders[] = basename($file).' → '.$target;
            }
        }

        foreach ($targets['dynamic'] as $target) {
            if (! \in_array($target, $allowedDynamicTargets, strict: true)) {
                $unknownDynamic[] = basename($file).' → '.$target;
            }
        }
    }

    expect($seen)->toBeGreaterThan(5)
        ->and($offenders)->toBe([])
        ->and($unknownDynamic)->toBe([]);
});

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Pre-mortem A4 — migration-path partition.
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

test('every axiomasoft/corex-tenancy migration is classified root or tenant by its directory', function () use ($packageRoot, $migrationFiles, $rootLayerDir, $tenantLayerDir): void {
    $all = $migrationFiles($packageRoot.'/database/migrations');
    $root = $migrationFiles($rootLayerDir);
    $tenant = $migrationFiles($tenantLayerDir);

    // Тотальность классификации: файла вне двух директорий (например прямо в
    // `database/migrations/`) быть не может — такой поехал бы обоими раннерами.
    expect(array_values(array_diff($all, $root, $tenant)))->toBe([])
        ->and(array_values(array_intersect($root, $tenant)))->toBe([])
        ->and(\count($all))->toBe(\count($root) + \count($tenant));
});

test('root-directory migrations create only root_ tables and tenant-directory migrations create none', function () use ($migrationFiles, $rootLayerDir, $tenantLayerDir, $createdTables): void {
    $misplaced = [];
    $seen = 0;

    foreach ([$rootLayerDir => true, $tenantLayerDir => false] as $dir => $expectsRootPrefix) {
        foreach ($migrationFiles((string) $dir) as $file) {
            foreach ($createdTables((string) file_get_contents($file)) as $table) {
                $seen++;

                if (str_starts_with($table, 'root_') !== $expectsRootPrefix) {
                    $misplaced[] = basename($file).' → '.$table;
                }
            }
        }
    }

    expect($seen)->toBeGreaterThan(15)
        ->and($misplaced)->toBe([]);
});

test('the central and tenant runners use disjoint registered schema scopes', function () use ($packageRoot): void {
    $provider = (string) file_get_contents($packageRoot.'/src/TenancyServiceProvider.php');
    $sets = new TenancySchemaSets;
    $byName = [];
    foreach ($sets->sets() as $set) {
        $byName[$set->name] = $set->scope->value;
    }
    expect($byName)->toBe(['root' => 'central', 'tenant' => 'tenant'])
        ->and(preg_match('/\$this->loadMigrationsFrom\s*\(/', $provider))->toBe(0);
    $orchestrator = (string) file_get_contents($packageRoot.'/src/Account/Laravel/MigrationWaveOrchestrator.php');
    expect($orchestrator)->toContain("\$this->schema->migrate(SchemaScope::Tenant, 'tenant')")
        ->not->toContain('database/migrations/root');
});


WHOLE PRODUCT FILE M packages/tenancy/tests/Pg/ResidentTenantSwitchPgTest.php
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

use CoreX\Context\Contracts\TenancyManager;
use CoreX\Context\Data\AccountRef;
use CoreX\Messaging\Contracts\MessageContractRegistry;
use CoreX\Messaging\Contracts\Outbox;
use CoreX\Messaging\Data\MessageContract;
use CoreX\Messaging\Data\MessageEnvelope;
use CoreX\Messaging\Data\MessagePayload;
use CoreX\Messaging\Enums\MessageClass;
use CoreX\Messaging\Enums\PiiClass;
use CoreX\Messaging\Internal\ArrayMessageContractRegistry;
use CoreX\Tenancy\Account\Internal\Models\Account;
use CoreX\Tenancy\Tests\TestCase;
use Illuminate\Database\Connection;
use Illuminate\Database\ConnectionInterface;
use Illuminate\Database\Connectors\ConnectionFactory;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\DB;
use Illuminate\Support\Str;

uses(TestCase::class)->group('pg');

/**
 * Resident worker: one application serves tenant A, the lifecycle boundary
 * (Application::forgetScopedInstances()) runs, then the same application serves tenant B.
 * The scoped Outbox must be rebuilt against the connection of the tenant that is current
 * *now*; a binding that captured A's connection would write B's message into A's database.
 * The assertions read the physical databases through their own connection names, bypassing
 * every service under test.
 */
function residentSwitchMessage(string $marker): MessageEnvelope
{
    return new MessageEnvelope(
        messageId: (string) Str::uuid7(),
        source: 'axiomasoft/corex',
        name: 'resident.marker',
        contractVersion: 1,
        occurredAt: new DateTimeImmutable('2026-01-01T00:00:00Z'),
        scope: ['accountId' => 'resident-'.$marker],
        payload: new MessagePayload(['marker' => $marker]),
        piiClass: PiiClass::None,
    );
}

/** @return list<string> markers stored in the outbox table of the database behind $connection */
function residentSwitchMarkers(ConnectionInterface $connection): array
{
    $markers = [];

    foreach ($connection->table('sys_outbox_messages')->orderBy('id')->pluck('payload') as $payload) {
        $markers[] = json_decode((string) $payload, true, flags: JSON_THROW_ON_ERROR)['marker'];
    }

    return $markers;
}

function residentSwitchTenantConnection(string $database): Connection
{
    $configuration = DB::connection('pgsql')->getConfig();
    $configuration['database'] = $database;

    return (new ConnectionFactory(app()))->make($configuration, $database);
}

beforeEach(function (): void {
    expect(app()->environment())->toBe('testing')
        ->and(str_ends_with(DB::connection('pgsql')->getDatabaseName(), '_test'))->toBeTrue();
    DB::statement('DROP SCHEMA IF EXISTS public CASCADE');
    DB::statement('CREATE SCHEMA public');
    DB::purge('pgsql');
    $this->migrateSchemaSets(['axiomasoft/corex-tenancy:root'], 'pgsql');
    // The central database carries the kernel tables too: a leaked write lands there and is visible.
    $this->migrateSchemaSets(['axiomasoft/corex:tenant'], 'pgsql');

    $this->databases = [];
    $this->accounts = [];

    foreach (['alpha', 'beta'] as $name) {
        $database = 'corex_p05_resident_'.bin2hex(random_bytes(6)).'_test';
        $this->databases[$name] = $database;
        DB::statement('CREATE DATABASE '.$database);

        $cluster = (string) Str::uuid();
        $identity = (string) Str::uuid();
        DB::table('root_clusters')->insert(['id' => $cluster, 'name' => $name, 'pg_host_writer' => 'writer.test', 'pgbouncer_host' => 'pool.test', 'app_db_user' => 'corex']);
        DB::table('root_identities')->insert(['id' => $identity, 'email' => $identity.'@example.test']);
        $account = Account::on('pgsql')->create(['cluster_id' => $cluster, 'db_name' => $database, 'slug' => $name, 'template_version' => 1, 'embedding_model' => 'bge-m3', 'embedding_dim' => 1024, 'owner_identity_id' => $identity, 'status' => 'active']);
        $this->accounts[$name] = new AccountRef(id: $account->id, slug: $name, status: 'active', features: [], limits: []);
    }

    // Kernel tables inside each tenant database: run the same migration files in that tenant's context.
    foreach ($this->accounts as $account) {
        app(TenancyManager::class)->runFor($account, null, function (): void {
            $this->migrateSchemaSets(['axiomasoft/corex:tenant'], 'tenant');
        });
    }

    app()->instance(MessageContractRegistry::class, new ArrayMessageContractRegistry(contracts: [new MessageContract(
        name: 'resident.marker',
        version: 1,
        class: MessageClass::IntegrationEvent,
        owner: 'axiomasoft/corex',
        maxPiiClass: PiiClass::None,
        channel: 'resident',
    )]));
    app()->forgetScopedInstances();
});

afterEach(function (): void {
    app(TenancyManager::class)->end();
    DB::purge('tenant');

    foreach ($this->databases as $database) {
        DB::connection('pgsql')->statement('DROP DATABASE IF EXISTS '.$database);
    }
});

it('writes after a lifecycle boundary into the database of the tenant that is current then', function (): void {
    $manager = app(TenancyManager::class);

    $manager->initialize($this->accounts['alpha']);
    $outboxForAlpha = app(Outbox::class);
    DB::transaction(static fn () => $outboxForAlpha->append(residentSwitchMessage('A')));
    $manager->end();

    // What an Octane or queue worker runs between two requests.
    app()->forgetScopedInstances();

    $manager->initialize($this->accounts['beta']);
    $outboxForBeta = app(Outbox::class);
    DB::transaction(static fn () => $outboxForBeta->append(residentSwitchMessage('B')));
    $manager->end();

    $alpha = residentSwitchTenantConnection($this->databases['alpha']);
    $beta = residentSwitchTenantConnection($this->databases['beta']);

    try {
        expect($outboxForBeta)->not->toBe($outboxForAlpha)
            ->and(residentSwitchMarkers($alpha))->toBe(['A'])
            ->and(residentSwitchMarkers($beta))->toBe(['B'])
            ->and(residentSwitchMarkers(DB::connection('pgsql')))->toBe([]);
    } finally {
        $alpha->disconnect();
        $beta->disconnect();
    }
});


WHOLE PRODUCT FILE M packages/tools/tests/Feature/ToolAuthorizationLayersTest.php
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

namespace CoreX\Tools\Tests\Feature;

use Closure;
use CoreX\Context\Contracts\TenancyManager;
use CoreX\Context\Contracts\TenantContextResolver;
use CoreX\Context\Data\AccountRef;
use CoreX\CoreServiceProvider;
use CoreX\Modules\Contracts\ModuleRegistry;
use CoreX\Modules\Data\Api;
use CoreX\Modules\Data\ModuleManifest;
use CoreX\Modules\Internal\Compiler\RegistryCompiler;
use CoreX\Modules\ModulesServiceProvider;
use CoreX\Operations\ApiServiceProvider;
use CoreX\Operations\Authorization\Contracts\ClientScopeAuthorizer;
use CoreX\Operations\Authorization\Contracts\OperationAuthorizer;
use CoreX\Operations\Authorization\Contracts\OperationFieldAuthorizer;
use CoreX\Operations\Authorization\Contracts\PrincipalResolver;
use CoreX\Operations\Definition\Contracts\OperationHandler;
use CoreX\Operations\Definition\Data\Operation;
use CoreX\Operations\Definition\Data\ResourceId;
use CoreX\Operations\Definition\Enums\ResourceIdFormat;
use CoreX\Operations\Errors\Enums\OperationsErrorCode;
use CoreX\Operations\Errors\Exceptions\ApiError;
use CoreX\Operations\Invocation\Contracts\RequestContext;
use CoreX\Operations\Invocation\Data\InvocationMetadata;
use CoreX\Operations\Invocation\Internal\OperationDispatcher;
use CoreX\Operations\Invocation\Laravel\ResolvedContexts;
use CoreX\Operations\Testing\ContextFixture;
use CoreX\Operations\Testing\IdentityFixture;
use CoreX\Operations\Testing\ModuleFixture;
use CoreX\Operations\Validation\Data\JsonObject;
use CoreX\Operations\Validation\Laravel\JsonCanonical;
use CoreX\Operations\Validation\Laravel\JsonObjectFactory;
use CoreX\Tools\Contracts\ToolExecutionGuard;
use CoreX\Tools\Contracts\ToolInvoker;
use CoreX\Tools\Contracts\ToolPolicy;
use CoreX\Tools\Data\ToolCall;
use CoreX\Tools\Data\ToolDescriptor;
use CoreX\Tools\Data\ToolOutcome;
use CoreX\Tools\Data\ToolResult;
use CoreX\Tools\Internal\ToolDescriptorFactory;
use CoreX\Tools\ToolServiceProvider;
use Illuminate\Http\JsonResponse;
use Illuminate\Http\Request;
use Orchestra\Testbench\TestCase;

final class ToolAuthorizationLayersTest extends TestCase
{
    protected function defineEnvironment($app): void
    {
        $app['config']->set('database.default', 'sqlite');
        $app['config']->set('database.connections.sqlite.database', ':memory:');
        $app['config']->set('cache.default', 'array');
        $app['config']->set('session.driver', 'array');
        $app['config']->set('queue.default', 'sync');
    }

    public const LocalResource = 'note:uuid:01890f3e-7b2a-7cc0-98c4-dc0c0c07398f';

    public const ForeignTenantResource = 'note:uuid:01890f3e-7b2a-7cc0-98c4-dc0c0c073990';

    private ModuleFixture $modules;

    private ContextFixture $tenants;

    private IdentityFixture $identity;

    private ToolAuthorizationLayersClientScopeFixture $clientScopes;

    private ToolAuthorizationLayersActorAuthorizerFixture $actors;

    private ToolAuthorizationLayersFieldAuthorizerFixture $fields;

    protected function getPackageProviders($app): array
    {
        return [CoreServiceProvider::class, ModulesServiceProvider::class, ApiServiceProvider::class, ToolServiceProvider::class];
    }

    protected function setUp(): void
    {
        parent::setUp();
        $input = $this->objectSchema([
            'title' => ['type' => 'string'],
            'owner' => ['type' => 'string'],
            'resource_id' => ['type' => 'string'],
            'claimed_account' => ['type' => 'string'],
            'secret' => ['type' => 'string'],
        ]);
        $output = $this->objectSchema([
            'id' => ['type' => 'string'],
            'title' => ['type' => 'string'],
            'owner' => ['type' => 'string'],
            'account' => ['type' => 'string'],
            'secret' => ['type' => 'string'],
            'items' => [
                'type' => 'array',
                'items' => $this->objectSchema([
                    'title' => ['type' => 'string'],
                    'secret' => ['type' => 'string'],
                ]),
            ],
        ]);
        $operation = Api::operation(
            'notes.update',
            'POST',
            '/notes',
            ToolAuthorizationLayersAuthorizationHandler::class,
            'notes.write',
            $input,
            $output,
            [
                'title' => ['required', 'string'],
                'owner' => ['required', 'string'],
                'resource_id' => ['required', 'string'],
                'claimed_account' => ['sometimes', 'string'],
                'secret' => ['sometimes', 'string'],
            ],
        )->asCommand()
            ->idempotency('optional')
            ->effect('tenant_transactional')
            ->requiresScopes('notes.write')
            ->fields(
                read: ['/id', '/title', '/owner', '/account', '/items'],
                write: ['/title', '/owner', '/resource_id', '/claimed_account'],
            );
        $tool = Api::exposeTool(
            'notes.update',
            'v1',
            'notes.update',
            'v1',
            'write',
            'domain-key',
            $input,
            $output,
            [
                'title' => 'title',
                'owner' => 'owner',
                'resource_id' => 'resource_id',
                'claimed_account' => 'claimed_account',
                'secret' => 'secret',
            ],
        );
        $registry = app(RegistryCompiler::class)->compile([
            ModuleManifest::make('example/notes')->title('Notes')->tablePrefix('nts')->extends([$operation, $tool]),
        ], installedPackages: []);

        $this->modules = new ModuleFixture($registry);
        $this->tenants = new ContextFixture;
        $this->tenants->initialize(new AccountRef('account-a', 'account-a', 'active', [], []));
        $this->identity = new IdentityFixture;
        $this->clientScopes = new ToolAuthorizationLayersClientScopeFixture;
        $this->actors = new ToolAuthorizationLayersActorAuthorizerFixture;
        $this->fields = new ToolAuthorizationLayersFieldAuthorizerFixture;

        $this->app->instance(ModuleRegistry::class, $this->modules);
        $this->app->instance(TenantContextResolver::class, $this->tenants);
        $this->app->instance(TenancyManager::class, $this->tenants);
        $this->app->instance(ClientScopeAuthorizer::class, $this->clientScopes);
        $this->app->instance(OperationAuthorizer::class, $this->actors);
        $this->app->instance(OperationFieldAuthorizer::class, $this->fields);
        $this->app->instance(ToolPolicy::class, new class implements ToolPolicy
        {
            public function discover(ToolDescriptor $tool, RequestContext $context): bool
            {
                return true;
            }

            public function invoke(ToolDescriptor $tool, RequestContext $context, JsonObject $arguments): bool
            {
                return true;
            }
        });
        $this->app->instance(ToolExecutionGuard::class, new class implements ToolExecutionGuard
        {
            public function execute(ToolDescriptor $tool, RequestContext $context, ToolCall $call, Closure $effect): ToolResult
            {
                return $effect();
            }
        });
        $this->app->instance(PrincipalResolver::class, $this->identity);
        ToolAuthorizationLayersAuthorizationHandler::$calls = 0;
    }

    public function test_client_actor_record_field_and_tenant_layers_deny_independently(): void
    {
        $this->clientScopes->allow = false;
        $this->assertHttpError('operations.scope_required', $this->validInput());
        self::assertSame(0, ToolAuthorizationLayersAuthorizationHandler::$calls);

        $this->clientScopes->allow = true;
        $this->actors->allow = false;
        $this->assertHttpError('operations.access_denied', $this->validInput());
        self::assertSame(0, ToolAuthorizationLayersAuthorizationHandler::$calls);

        $this->actors->allow = true;
        $forbiddenInput = [...$this->validInput(), 'secret' => 'must-not-reach-handler'];
        $this->assertHttpError('operations.access_denied', $forbiddenInput);
        $toolDenied = $this->app->make(ToolInvoker::class)->invoke(
            $this->context(),
            $this->toolCall($forbiddenInput),
        );
        self::assertSame('operations.access_denied', $toolDenied->errorCode);
        self::assertSame(0, ToolAuthorizationLayersAuthorizationHandler::$calls);

        $this->assertHttpError('operations.resource_not_found', $this->validInput(owner: 'foreign-user'));
        self::assertSame(1, ToolAuthorizationLayersAuthorizationHandler::$calls);

        $this->assertHttpError('operations.resource_not_found', $this->validInput(resourceId: self::ForeignTenantResource));
        self::assertSame(2, ToolAuthorizationLayersAuthorizationHandler::$calls);
    }

    public function test_http_and_tool_apply_the_same_recursive_read_policy_without_trusting_payload_scope(): void
    {
        $this->fields->deniedRead = ['/owner', '/items/0/secret', '/items/1/secret'];
        $input = $this->validInput(claimedAccount: 'forged-account');

        $http = $this->dispatch($input);
        $tool = $this->app->make(ToolInvoker::class)->invoke($this->context(), $this->toolCall($input));
        $httpData = $http->getData(true)['data'];
        $toolData = JsonCanonical::decodeObject((string) $tool->structuredData?->canonicalJson);

        self::assertSame(200, $http->getStatusCode());
        self::assertSame(ToolOutcome::Committed, $tool->outcome);
        self::assertSame('account-a', $httpData['account']);
        self::assertSame('account-a', $toolData['account']);
        self::assertArrayNotHasKey('owner', $httpData);
        self::assertArrayNotHasKey('owner', $toolData);
        self::assertArrayNotHasKey('secret', $httpData);
        self::assertArrayNotHasKey('secret', $toolData);
        self::assertSame([0, 1], array_keys($httpData['items']));
        self::assertSame([0, 1], array_keys($toolData['items']));
        self::assertArrayNotHasKey('secret', $httpData['items'][0]);
        self::assertArrayNotHasKey('secret', $httpData['items'][1]);
        self::assertArrayNotHasKey('secret', $toolData['items'][0]);
        self::assertArrayNotHasKey('secret', $toolData['items'][1]);
        self::assertSame(2, ToolAuthorizationLayersAuthorizationHandler::$calls);
    }

    /** @param array<string, mixed> $input */
    private function dispatch(array $input, ?string $idempotencyKey = null): JsonResponse
    {
        return $this->app->make(OperationDispatcher::class)->dispatch(Request::create(
            '/api/v1/notes',
            'POST',
            server: array_filter([
                'CONTENT_TYPE' => 'application/json',
                'HTTP_IDEMPOTENCY_KEY' => $idempotencyKey,
            ]),
            content: JsonCanonical::encode($input),
        ), 'v1', 'notes');
    }

    /** @param array<string, mixed> $input */
    private function assertHttpError(string $code, array $input): void
    {
        self::assertSame($code, $this->dispatch($input)->getData(true)['code']);
    }

    /** @return array<string, string> */
    private function validInput(
        string $owner = 'trusted-user',
        string $resourceId = self::LocalResource,
        string $claimedAccount = 'account-a',
    ): array {
        return [
            'title' => 'Hello',
            'owner' => $owner,
            'resource_id' => $resourceId,
            'claimed_account' => $claimedAccount,
        ];
    }

    /** @param array<string, mixed> $input */
    private function toolCall(array $input): ToolCall
    {
        $declaration = collect($this->modules->registry->api)->first(
            static fn (Api $api): bool => $api->mode === 'tool' && $api->toolCode === 'notes.update',
        );

        return new ToolCall(
            code: 'notes.update',
            toolVersion: 'v1',
            expectedContractHash: ToolDescriptorFactory::fromDeclaration($declaration)->contractHash,
            arguments: JsonObjectFactory::fromArray($input),
            requestId: 'tool-request',
        );
    }

    private function context(): RequestContext
    {
        return ResolvedContexts::make(
            $this->tenants->current(),
            $this->identity,
            new InvocationMetadata('request-1', 'v1', 'notes.update', 'POST'),
        );
    }

    /** @param array<string, mixed> $properties */
    private function objectSchema(array $properties): array
    {
        return [
            '$schema' => 'https://json-schema.org/draft/2020-12/schema',
            'type' => 'object',
            'additionalProperties' => false,
            'properties' => $properties,
        ];
    }
}

final class ToolAuthorizationLayersClientScopeFixture implements ClientScopeAuthorizer
{
    public bool $allow = true;

    public function allows(Operation $operation, RequestContext $context): bool
    {
        return $this->allow;
    }
}

final class ToolAuthorizationLayersActorAuthorizerFixture implements OperationAuthorizer
{
    public bool $allow = true;

    public function allows(Operation $operation, RequestContext $context): bool
    {
        return $this->allow;
    }
}

final class ToolAuthorizationLayersFieldAuthorizerFixture implements OperationFieldAuthorizer
{
    /** @var list<string> */
    public array $deniedRead = [];

    /** @var list<string> */
    public array $deniedWrite = [];

    public function allowsRead(Operation $operation, RequestContext $context, string $path): bool
    {
        return ! in_array($path, $this->deniedRead, true);
    }

    public function allowsWrite(Operation $operation, RequestContext $context, string $path): bool
    {
        return ! in_array($path, $this->deniedWrite, true);
    }
}

final class ToolAuthorizationLayersAuthorizationHandler implements OperationHandler
{
    public static int $calls = 0;

    public function handle(RequestContext $context, array $input, array $parameters): array
    {
        self::$calls++;
        $resource = ResourceId::parse((string) $input['resource_id'], ResourceIdFormat::Uuid7, 'note');

        if ($resource->toString() === ToolAuthorizationLayersTest::ForeignTenantResource
            || $input['owner'] !== $context->principal()->id()) {
            throw new ApiError(OperationsErrorCode::ResourceNotFound, 404, 'Resource not found.');
        }

        return [
            'id' => $resource->toString(),
            'title' => $input['title'],
            'owner' => $input['owner'],
            'account' => $context->tenant()->account->id,
            'secret' => 'top-secret',
            'items' => [
                ['title' => 'first', 'secret' => 'first-secret'],
                ['title' => 'second', 'secret' => 'second-secret'],
            ],
        ];
    }
}


WHOLE PRODUCT FILE M packages/webhooks/tests/Feature/WebhookDeliveryFixture.php
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

namespace CoreX\Webhooks\Tests\Feature;

use CoreX\Config\Data\SecretRef;
use CoreX\Context\Data\AccountRef;
use CoreX\Data\Contracts\DataScopeCodec;
use CoreX\Data\Data\DataScope;
use CoreX\Database\Contracts\SchemaMigrator;
use CoreX\Database\Contracts\SchemaSets;
use CoreX\Messaging\Data\MessageContract;
use CoreX\Messaging\Data\MessageEnvelope;
use CoreX\Messaging\Enums\MessageClass;
use CoreX\Messaging\Enums\PiiClass;
use CoreX\Messaging\Testing\MessagingFixtures;
use CoreX\Operations\Laravel\ApiSchemaRegistrar;
use CoreX\Operations\Testing\ContextFixture;
use CoreX\Operations\Validation\Laravel\JsonCanonical;
use CoreX\Time\Testing\FrozenClock;
use CoreX\Webhooks\Contracts\WebhookPayloadProjector;
use CoreX\Webhooks\Data\WebhookTopic;
use CoreX\Webhooks\Internal\HmacWebhookSigner;
use CoreX\Webhooks\Internal\WebhookKeyRing;
use CoreX\Webhooks\Internal\WebhookTopicCatalogue;
use CoreX\Webhooks\Internal\WebhookUrlPolicy;
use DateInterval;
use DateTimeImmutable;
use Illuminate\Database\Connection;
use Illuminate\Database\Schema\Blueprint;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\DB;

trait WebhookDeliveryFixture
{
    private const string Secret = 'whsec_AAECAwQFBgcICQoLDA0ODxAREhMUFRYXGBkaGxwdHh8=';

    private Connection $connection;

    private FrozenClock $clock;

    private DataScope $scope;

    private ContextFixture $contexts;

    protected function setUp(): void
    {
        parent::setUp();
        $this->configure('api_budget_stub');
        $this->configure('api_tenant');
        $this->selectSchema();
        $this->connection = DB::connection('api_tenant');
        $migration = require app(SchemaSets::class)->get('axiomasoft/corex-webhooks:tenant')->path.'/create_sys_api_webhook_tables.php';
        app(SchemaMigrator::class)->migrateSets(['axiomasoft/corex-webhooks:tenant'], app(ApiSchemaRegistrar::class)->tenant()->getName());
        $this->connection->getSchemaBuilder()->create('sys_inbox_messages', function (Blueprint $table): void {
            $table->string('consumer', 120);
            $table->string('message_id', 64);
            $table->string('name', 160);
            $table->timestampTz('processed_at', precision: 6);
            $table->primary(['consumer', 'message_id']);
        });
        $this->clock = new FrozenClock(new DateTimeImmutable('2026-09-26T12:00:00.000000+00:00'));
        $this->contexts = new ContextFixture;
        $this->contexts->initialize(new AccountRef('account-1', 'account', 'active', [], []));
        $this->scope = new DataScope(
            accountId: 'account-1',
            workspaceId: null,
            connectionIdentity: 'sqlite::memory:',
            actorId: 'actor-1',
            restoreEpoch: hash('sha256', 'webhook-delivery-restore'),
        );
    }

    private function signer(): HmacWebhookSigner
    {
        $keys = new WebhookKeyRing;
        $keys->provision('current', new SecretRef(self::Secret), 0);

        return new HmacWebhookSigner($keys);
    }

    private function urlPolicy(): WebhookUrlPolicy
    {
        return new WebhookUrlPolicy(static fn (): array => ['93.184.216.34']);
    }

    private function topics(): WebhookTopicCatalogue
    {
        $schema = ['id' => 'string'];
        $projector = new FixtureDeliveryProjector;

        return new WebhookTopicCatalogue(
            MessagingFixtures::contracts([
                new MessageContract('orders.placed', 1, MessageClass::IntegrationEvent, 'orders/api', PiiClass::Personal, 'webhooks'),
            ]),
            [new WebhookTopic(
                'orders.placed',
                1,
                'v1',
                $schema,
                hash('sha256', JsonCanonical::encode($schema)),
                'personal',
                'orders.placed',
            )],
            ['orders.placed' => $projector],
        );
    }

    private function seedDelivery(
        string $delivery,
        string $subscription,
        int $attempt = 0,
        ?DateTimeImmutable $createdAt = null,
    ): void {
        $now = $this->clock->now();
        $createdAt ??= $now;
        $this->connection->table('sys_api_webhook_subscriptions')->insert([
            'id' => $subscription,
            'revision' => 0,
            'major' => 'v1',
            'topic' => 'orders.placed',
            'topic_version' => 1,
            'endpoint_url' => 'https://hooks.example/receiver',
            'status' => 'active',
            'max_pii_class' => 'personal',
            'key_refs' => '["current"]',
            'created_at' => $createdAt,
            'updated_at' => $createdAt,
        ]);
        $body = JsonCanonical::encode(['event_id' => $delivery, 'data' => ['id' => 'order-1']]);
        $this->connection->table('sys_api_webhook_deliveries')->insert([
            'id' => $delivery,
            'subscription_id' => $subscription,
            'subscription_revision' => 0,
            'event_id' => 'event-'.$delivery,
            'topic' => 'orders.placed',
            'topic_version' => 1,
            'major' => 'v1',
            'endpoint_snapshot' => 'https://hooks.example/receiver',
            'scope' => JsonCanonical::encode(app(DataScopeCodec::class)->encode($this->scope)),
            'body' => $body,
            'body_hash' => hash('sha256', $body),
            'status' => 'pending',
            'attempt' => $attempt,
            'replay_count' => 0,
            'lease_owner' => null,
            'lease_until' => null,
            'next_attempt_at' => $now,
            'created_at' => $createdAt,
            'finished_at' => null,
            'expires_at' => $createdAt->add(new DateInterval('P7D')),
            'last_error' => null,
        ]);
    }

    private function configure(string $name): void
    {
        $this->app['config']->set('database.connections.'.$name, [
            'driver' => 'sqlite',
            'database' => ':memory:',
            'prefix' => '',
            'foreign_key_constraints' => true,
        ]);
        DB::purge($name);
    }

    private function selectSchema(): void
    {
        $this->app->forgetInstance(ApiSchemaRegistrar::class);
        $this->app->forgetInstance(SchemaSets::class);
        $this->app->forgetInstance(SchemaMigrator::class);
        $this->app['config']->set('corex-operations.schema', [
            'budget' => ['connection' => 'api_budget_stub', 'driver' => 'sqlite', 'database' => ':memory:'],
            'tenant' => ['connection' => 'api_tenant', 'driver' => 'sqlite', 'database' => ':memory:'],
        ]);
    }
}

WHOLE PRODUCT FILE M packages/webhooks/tests/Feature/WebhookFanoutTest.php
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

namespace CoreX\Webhooks\Tests\Feature;

use CoreX\Config\Data\SecretRef;
use CoreX\Context\Data\AccountRef;
use CoreX\CoreServiceProvider;
use CoreX\Database\Contracts\SchemaMigrator;
use CoreX\Database\Contracts\SchemaSets;
use CoreX\Messaging\Contracts\DurableMessagePublisher;
use CoreX\Messaging\Contracts\MessagePublisher;
use CoreX\Messaging\Data\MessageContract;
use CoreX\Messaging\Data\MessageEnvelope;
use CoreX\Messaging\Data\MessagePayload;
use CoreX\Messaging\Enums\MessageClass;
use CoreX\Messaging\Enums\PiiClass;
use CoreX\Messaging\Internal\JsonCloudEventCodec;
use CoreX\Messaging\Testing\MessagingFixtures;
use CoreX\Operations\Laravel\ApiSchemaRegistrar;
use CoreX\Operations\Testing\ContextFixture;
use CoreX\Operations\Validation\Laravel\JsonCanonical;
use CoreX\Time\Testing\FrozenClock;
use CoreX\Webhooks\Contracts\WebhookPayloadProjector;
use CoreX\Webhooks\Data\WebhookSubscription;
use CoreX\Webhooks\Data\WebhookTopic;
use CoreX\Webhooks\Internal\HmacWebhookSigner;
use CoreX\Webhooks\Internal\WebhookFanout;
use CoreX\Webhooks\Internal\WebhookKeyRing;
use CoreX\Webhooks\Internal\WebhookMessagePublisher;
use CoreX\Webhooks\Internal\WebhookTopicCatalogue;
use CoreX\Webhooks\Tests\TestCase;
use DateTimeImmutable;
use Illuminate\Database\Schema\Blueprint;
use Illuminate\Foundation\Application;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\DB;
use PHPUnit\Framework\Attributes\Group;
use RuntimeException;
use TypeError;

final class WebhookFanoutTest extends TestCase
{
    private FrozenClock $clock;

    protected function setUp(): void
    {
        parent::setUp();
        $this->clock = new FrozenClock(new DateTimeImmutable('2026-09-25T12:00:00.000000+00:00'));
        $this->configure('api_budget_stub');
        $this->configure('api_tenant');
        $this->selectSchema('api_tenant');
        $migration = require app(SchemaSets::class)->get('axiomasoft/corex-webhooks:tenant')->path.'/create_sys_api_webhook_tables.php';
        app(SchemaMigrator::class)->migrateSets(['axiomasoft/corex-webhooks:tenant'], app(ApiSchemaRegistrar::class)->tenant()->getName());
        $this->inbox();
    }

    /** @param Application $app */
    protected function getPackageProviders($app): array
    {
        return [CoreServiceProvider::class, ...parent::getPackageProviders($app)];
    }

    public function test_projection_failure_rolls_back_inbox_and_delivery_together(): void
    {
        $this->subscribe('sub-1');
        $publisher = $this->publisher(new ThrowingProjector);

        try {
            $publisher->publish($this->envelope('11111111-1111-4111-8111-111111111111'));
            self::fail('Projection failure was committed.');
        } catch (RuntimeException) {
            self::assertSame(0, DB::connection('api_tenant')->table('sys_inbox_messages')->count());
            self::assertSame(0, DB::connection('api_tenant')->table('sys_api_webhook_deliveries')->count());
        }
    }

    public function test_duplicate_event_creates_one_delivery_and_forwards_the_existing_publisher(): void
    {
        $this->subscribe('sub-1');
        $forwarded = 0;
        $inner = new class($forwarded) implements DurableMessagePublisher
        {
            public function __construct(private int &$count) {}

            public function publish(MessageEnvelope $message): void
            {
                $this->count++;
            }
        };
        $publisher = $this->publisher(new ExportProjector, $inner);
        $envelope = $this->envelope('22222222-2222-4222-8222-222222222222');
        $publisher->publish($envelope);
        $publisher->publish($envelope);
        $this->subscribe('sub-later');
        $publisher->publish($envelope);

        $rows = DB::connection('api_tenant')->table('sys_api_webhook_deliveries')->get();

        self::assertCount(1, $rows);
        self::assertSame($envelope->messageId, $rows[0]->event_id);
        self::assertNotSame($rows[0]->event_id, $rows[0]->id);
        self::assertSame(hash('sha256', (string) $rows[0]->body), $rows[0]->body_hash);
        self::assertSame('https://hooks.example/receiver', $rows[0]->endpoint_snapshot);
        self::assertSame(3, $forwarded);
        self::assertStringNotContainsString('hidden', (string) $rows[0]->body);
        $event = json_decode((string) $rows[0]->body, true, flags: JSON_THROW_ON_ERROR);
        self::assertSame(['specversion', 'id', 'source', 'type', 'time', 'datacontenttype', 'data'], array_keys($event));
        self::assertSame('1.0', $event['specversion']);
        self::assertSame('orders.placed.v1', $event['type']);
        self::assertSame(['id' => 'order-1'], $event['data']);
        self::assertArrayNotHasKey('dataschema', $event);
        $keys = new WebhookKeyRing;
        $keys->provision('current', new SecretRef('whsec_'.base64_encode(str_repeat('s', 32))), 0);
        $signer = new HmacWebhookSigner($keys);
        $signed = $signer->sign((string) $rows[0]->id, 1790337600, (string) $rows[0]->body, ['current']);
        self::assertSame(['v1,'.base64_encode(hash_hmac('sha256', $rows[0]->id.'.1790337600.'.$rows[0]->body, str_repeat('s', 32), true))], $signed->signatures);
    }

    public function test_oversize_payload_is_dead_and_a_lossy_publisher_is_rejected(): void
    {
        $this->subscribe('sub-1');
        $this->publisher(new HugeProjector)->publish($this->envelope('33333333-3333-4333-8333-333333333333'));
        $row = DB::connection('api_tenant')->table('sys_api_webhook_deliveries')->first();

        self::assertNotNull($row);
        self::assertSame('dead', $row->status);
        self::assertSame('payload_too_large', $row->last_error);
        self::assertLessThan(WebhookFanout::BodyLimit, strlen((string) $row->body));

        $this->expectException(TypeError::class);
        new WebhookMessagePublisher(new class implements MessagePublisher
        {
            public function publish(MessageEnvelope $message): void {}
        }, $this->fanout(new ExportProjector));
    }

    #[Group('pg')]
    public function test_postgresql_inbox_and_delivery_roll_back_together(): void
    {
        $database = getenv('PGSQL_DATABASE') ?: '';

        if (preg_match('/\A[a-z0-9_]+_test\z/D', $database) !== 1) {
            self::markTestSkipped('PGSQL_DATABASE must name an isolated *_test database.');
        }

        $base = [
            'driver' => 'pgsql',
            'host' => getenv('PGSQL_HOST') ?: '127.0.0.1',
            'port' => getenv('PGSQL_PORT') ?: '5434',
            'database' => $database,
            'username' => getenv('PGSQL_USERNAME') ?: 'corex',
            'password' => getenv('PGSQL_PASSWORD') ?: 'corex',
            'charset' => 'utf8',
            'prefix' => '',
        ];
        $this->app['config']->set('database.connections.api_pg_root', $base + ['search_path' => 'public']);
        $this->app['config']->set('database.connections.api_pg_tenant', $base + ['search_path' => 'api_p1412_tenant_test']);
        DB::purge('api_pg_root');
        DB::purge('api_pg_tenant');
        $root = DB::connection('api_pg_root');
        self::assertSame($database, $root->getDatabaseName());
        $root->statement('DROP SCHEMA IF EXISTS api_p1412_tenant_test CASCADE');
        $root->statement('CREATE SCHEMA api_p1412_tenant_test');

        try {
            $this->selectSchema('api_pg_tenant');
            $migration = require app(SchemaSets::class)->get('axiomasoft/corex-webhooks:tenant')->path.'/create_sys_api_webhook_tables.php';
            app(SchemaMigrator::class)->migrateSets(['axiomasoft/corex-webhooks:tenant'], app(ApiSchemaRegistrar::class)->tenant()->getName());
            $this->inbox('api_pg_tenant');
            $connection = DB::connection('api_pg_tenant');
            $this->subscribe('sub-pg', $connection->getName());
            $fanout = $this->fanout(new ThrowingProjector, 'api_pg_tenant');

            try {
                $fanout->accept($this->envelope('44444444-4444-4444-8444-444444444444'));
                self::fail('PostgreSQL projection failure was committed.');
            } catch (RuntimeException) {
                self::assertSame(0, $connection->table('sys_inbox_messages')->count());
                self::assertSame(0, $connection->table('sys_api_webhook_deliveries')->count());
            }
        } finally {
            $root->statement('DROP SCHEMA IF EXISTS api_p1412_tenant_test CASCADE');
        }
    }

    private function publisher(WebhookPayloadProjector $projector, ?DurableMessagePublisher $inner = null): WebhookMessagePublisher
    {
        $inner ??= new class implements DurableMessagePublisher
        {
            public function publish(MessageEnvelope $message): void {}
        };

        return new WebhookMessagePublisher($inner, $this->fanout($projector));
    }

    private function fanout(WebhookPayloadProjector $projector, string $connection = 'api_tenant'): WebhookFanout
    {
        $db = DB::connection($connection);
        $contexts = new ContextFixture;
        $contexts->initialize(new AccountRef('account-1', 'account', 'active', [], []));
        $schema = ['id' => 'string'];
        $topic = new WebhookTopic('orders.placed', 1, 'v1', $schema, hash('sha256', JsonCanonical::encode($schema)), 'personal', 'orders.placed');
        $contracts = MessagingFixtures::contracts([
            new MessageContract('orders.placed', 1, MessageClass::IntegrationEvent, 'orders/api', PiiClass::Personal, 'webhooks', 'https://schemas.example.test/orders/v1.json'),
        ]);
        $catalogue = new WebhookTopicCatalogue($contracts, [$topic], ['orders.placed' => $projector]);

        return new WebhookFanout($db, MessagingFixtures::inbox($db, $this->clock), $catalogue, ['orders.placed' => $projector], $this->clock, new JsonCloudEventCodec($contracts));
    }

    private function subscribe(string $id, string $connection = 'api_tenant'): void
    {
        $now = $this->clock->now();
        $subscription = new WebhookSubscription($id, 0, 'v1', 'orders.placed', 1, 'https://hooks.example/receiver', 'active', 'personal', ['current'], $now, $now);
        DB::connection($connection)->table('sys_api_webhook_subscriptions')->insert([
            'id' => $subscription->id,
            'revision' => 0,
            'major' => $subscription->major,
            'topic' => $subscription->topic,
            'topic_version' => $subscription->topicVersion,
            'endpoint_url' => $subscription->endpointUrl,
            'status' => 'active',
            'max_pii_class' => $subscription->maxPiiClass,
            'key_refs' => '["current"]',
            'created_at' => $now,
            'updated_at' => $now,
        ]);
    }

    private function envelope(string $id): MessageEnvelope
    {
        return new MessageEnvelope(
            messageId: $id,
            source: 'orders',
            name: 'orders.placed',
            contractVersion: 1,
            occurredAt: new DateTimeImmutable('2026-09-25T12:00:00+00:00'),
            scope: ['account' => 'account-1'],
            payload: new MessagePayload(['id' => 'order-1', 'secret' => 'hidden']),
            piiClass: PiiClass::Personal,
            correlationId: 'correlation-1',
        );
    }

    private function inbox(string $connection = 'api_tenant'): void
    {
        DB::connection($connection)->getSchemaBuilder()->create('sys_inbox_messages', function (Blueprint $table): void {
            $table->string('consumer', 120);
            $table->uuid('message_id');
            $table->string('name', 160);
            $table->timestampTz('processed_at', precision: 6);
            $table->primary(['consumer', 'message_id']);
        });
    }

    private function configure(string $name): void
    {
        $this->app['config']->set('database.connections.'.$name, [
            'driver' => 'sqlite',
            'database' => ':memory:',
            'prefix' => '',
            'foreign_key_constraints' => true,
        ]);
        DB::purge($name);
    }

    private function selectSchema(string $tenant): void
    {
        $this->app->forgetInstance(ApiSchemaRegistrar::class);
        $this->app->forgetInstance(SchemaSets::class);
        $this->app->forgetInstance(SchemaMigrator::class);
        $driver = (string) $this->app['config']->get('database.connections.'.$tenant.'.driver');
        $database = (string) $this->app['config']->get('database.connections.'.$tenant.'.database');
        $this->app['config']->set('corex-operations.schema', [
            'budget' => ['connection' => 'api_budget_stub', 'driver' => 'sqlite', 'database' => ':memory:'],
            'tenant' => ['connection' => $tenant, 'driver' => $driver, 'database' => $database],
        ]);
    }
}

final class ExportProjector implements WebhookPayloadProjector
{
    public function project(MessageEnvelope $message, WebhookTopic $topic): array
    {
        return ['id' => 'order-1'];
    }
}

final class ThrowingProjector implements WebhookPayloadProjector
{
    public function project(MessageEnvelope $message, WebhookTopic $topic): array
    {
        throw new RuntimeException('projection failed');
    }
}

final class HugeProjector implements WebhookPayloadProjector
{
    public function project(MessageEnvelope $message, WebhookTopic $topic): array
    {
        return ['id' => str_repeat('x', WebhookFanout::BodyLimit)];
    }
}

    #[Group('pg')]
    public function test_postgresql_fanout_delivers_once_to_the_local_receiver(): void
    {
        $database = getenv('PGSQL_DATABASE') ?: '';

        if (preg_match('/\A[a-z0-9_]+_test\z/D', $database) !== 1) {
            self::markTestSkipped('PGSQL_DATABASE must name an isolated *_test database.');
        }

        $base = [
            'driver' => 'pgsql',
            'host' => getenv('PGSQL_HOST') ?: '127.0.0.1',
            'port' => getenv('PGSQL_PORT') ?: '5434',
            'database' => $database,
            'username' => getenv('PGSQL_USERNAME') ?: 'corex',
            'password' => getenv('PGSQL_PASSWORD') ?: 'corex',
            'charset' => 'utf8',
            'prefix' => '',
        ];
        $this->configureSqlite('api_budget_stub');
        $this->app['config']->set('database.connections.api_p1420_root', $base + ['search_path' => 'public']);
        $this->app['config']->set('database.connections.api_p1420_tenant', $base + ['search_path' => 'api_p1420_tenant_test']);
        DB::purge('api_p1420_root');
        DB::purge('api_p1420_tenant');
        $root = DB::connection('api_p1420_root');
        self::assertSame($database, $root->getDatabaseName());
        $root->statement('DROP SCHEMA IF EXISTS api_p1420_tenant_test CASCADE');
        $root->statement('CREATE SCHEMA api_p1420_tenant_test');

        try {
            $this->selectSchema('api_budget_stub', 'sqlite', ':memory:', 'api_p1420_tenant', 'pgsql', $database);
            $migration = require app(SchemaSets::class)->get('axiomasoft/corex-webhooks:tenant')->path.'/create_sys_api_webhook_tables.php';
            app(SchemaMigrator::class)->migrateSets(['axiomasoft/corex-webhooks:tenant'], app(ApiSchemaRegistrar::class)->tenant()->getName());
            $connection = DB::connection('api_p1420_tenant');
            $connection->getSchemaBuilder()->create('sys_inbox_messages', function (Blueprint $table): void {
                $table->string('consumer', 120);
                $table->uuid('message_id');
                $table->string('name', 160);
                $table->timestampTz('processed_at', precision: 6);
                $table->primary(['consumer', 'message_id']);
            });
            $clock = new FrozenClock(new DateTimeImmutable('2026-09-26T12:00:00.000000+00:00'));
            $catalogue = $this->catalogue();
            $fanout = new WebhookFanout($connection, MessagingFixtures::inbox($connection, $clock), $catalogue, ['orders.placed' => new WebhookLoadContourIntegrationLoadContourProjector], $clock, new JsonCloudEventCodec(MessagingFixtures::contracts([new MessageContract('orders.placed', 1, MessageClass::IntegrationEvent, 'orders/api', PiiClass::Personal, 'webhooks')])));
            $now = $clock->now();
            $connection->table('sys_api_webhook_subscriptions')->insert([
                'id' => 'sub-local',
                'revision' => 0,
                'major' => 'v1',
                'topic' => 'orders.placed',
                'topic_version' => 1,
                'endpoint_url' => 'https://hooks.example/receiver',
                'status' => 'active',
                'max_pii_class' => 'personal',
                'key_refs' => '["current"]',
                'created_at' => $now,
                'updated_at' => $now,
            ]);
            $scope = new DataScope(
                'account-1',
                null,
                'pgsql:'.$database,
                'actor-1',
                hash('sha256', 'p1420-restore'),
            );
            $envelope = new MessageEnvelope(
                messageId: '55555555-5555-4555-8555-555555555555',
                source: 'orders',
                name: 'orders.placed',
                contractVersion: 1,
                occurredAt: $now,
                scope: app(DataScopeCodec::class)->encode($scope),
                payload: new MessagePayload(['id' => 'order-1', 'secret' => 'hidden']),
                piiClass: PiiClass::Personal,
                correlationId: 'correlation-1',
            );
            $fanout->accept($envelope);
            $fanout->accept($envelope);
            $rows = $connection->table('sys_api_webhook_deliveries')->get();
            self::assertCount(1, $rows);
            self::assertNotSame($envelope->messageId, $rows[0]->id);
            self::assertStringNotContainsString('hidden', (string) $rows[0]->body);

            $contexts = new ContextFixture;
            $contexts->initialize(new AccountRef('account-1', 'account', 'active', [], []));
            $keys = new WebhookKeyRing;
            $keys->provision('current', new SecretRef(self::Secret), 0);
            $receiver = new LocalWebhookReceiver([self::Secret], $clock->now()->getTimestamp());
            $worker = new WebhookDeliveryWorker(
                store: new SqlWebhookDeliveryStore($connection, new GuardedTenantContextRestorer($contexts), $clock, new WebhookRetryPolicy(WebhookOptionsParser::fromArray([]))),
                signer: new HmacWebhookSigner($keys),
                urlPolicy: new WebhookUrlPolicy(static fn (): array => ['93.184.216.34']),
                transport: $receiver,
                topics: $catalogue,
                clock: $clock,
                egress: new PermitPublicEgressPolicy,
                scopeCodec: app(DataScopeCodec::class),
            );
            $delivered = $worker->workOnce($scope, 'worker-local');

            self::assertNotNull($delivered);
            self::assertSame('succeeded', $delivered->status);
            self::assertSame(1, $receiver->effects);
            self::assertNull($worker->workOnce($scope, 'worker-local'));

            $signed = (new HmacWebhookSigner($keys))->sign($delivered->id, $clock->now()->getTimestamp(), $delivered->body, ['current']);
            $replay = new ValidatedWebhookRequest(
                'hooks.example',
                'https://hooks.example/receiver',
                '93.184.216.34',
                $delivered->body,
                $signed->toArray(),
            );
            $receiver->send($replay);
            $tampered = new ValidatedWebhookRequest(
                'hooks.example',
                'https://hooks.example/receiver',
                '93.184.216.34',
                $delivered->body.' ',
                $signed->toArray(),
            );

            self::assertSame(1, $receiver->effects);
            self::assertSame(401, $receiver->send($tampered)->status);
            $rejected = $receiver->send($tampered);
            self::assertSame(401, $rejected->status);
            self::assertNull($rejected->errorCode);
            self::assertSame(1, $receiver->effects);
        } finally {
            $root->statement('DROP SCHEMA IF EXISTS api_p1420_tenant_test CASCADE');
        }
    }
