    public function test_boxed_and_cloud_scopes_stay_on_the_configured_connection(): void
    {
        $this->configureSqlite('api_budget_stub');
        $this->configureSqlite('api_tenant');
        $this->selectSchema('api_budget_stub', 'sqlite', ':memory:', 'api_tenant', 'sqlite', ':memory:');
        $migration = require app(SchemaSets::class)->get('axiomasoft/corex-webhooks:tenant')->path.'/create_sys_api_webhook_tables.php';
        app(SchemaMigrator::class)->migrateSets(['axiomasoft/corex-webhooks:tenant'], app(ApiSchemaRegistrar::class)->tenant()->getName());
        $connection = DB::connection('api_tenant');
        $account = new AccountRef('account-1', 'account', 'active', [], []);
        $contexts = new ContextFixture;
        $contexts->value = new TenantContext($account, null, true);
        $clock = new FrozenClock(new DateTimeImmutable('2026-09-26T12:00:00.000000+00:00'));
        $store = new SqlWebhookSubscriptionStore($connection, new GuardedTenantContextRestorer($contexts), $this->catalogue(), $clock, new PermitPublicEgressPolicy);
        $boxed = new DataScope('account-1', null, 'boxed:account-1', 'actor-1', hash('sha256', 'boxed-restore'), boxed: true);
        $created = $store->create($boxed, $this->subscription('boxed-sub', $clock->now()), true, true);

        self::assertSame('boxed-sub', $created->id);
        self::assertSame('api_tenant', $connection->getName());
        self::assertSame(':memory:', $connection->getDatabaseName());
        self::assertSame(1, $connection->table('sys_api_webhook_subscriptions')->count());

        try {
            $store->create(
                new DataScope('account-1', null, 'pgsql:production', 'actor-1', hash('sha256', 'boxed-restore'), boxed: true),
                $this->subscription('foreign-sub', $clock->now()),
                true,
                true,
            );
            self::fail('A foreign connection identity opened a subscription.');
        } catch (ScopeContextMismatch) {
            self::assertSame(1, $connection->table('sys_api_webhook_subscriptions')->count());
            self::assertSame(':memory:', $connection->getDatabaseName());
        }

        $contexts->value = new TenantContext($account, null, false);
        $cloud = new DataScope(
            'account-1',
            null,
            $connection->getDriverName().':'.$connection->getDatabaseName(),
            'actor-1',
            hash('sha256', 'cloud-restore'),
        );
        $store->create($cloud, $this->subscription('cloud-sub', $clock->now()), true, true);

        self::assertSame(2, $connection->table('sys_api_webhook_subscriptions')->count());
        self::assertSame(':memory:', $connection->getDatabaseName());
    }

    private function subscription(string $id, DateTimeImmutable $now): WebhookSubscription
    {
        return new WebhookSubscription($id, 0, 'v1', 'orders.placed', 1, 'https://hooks.example/receiver', 'active', 'personal', ['current'], $now, $now);
    }
}

final class WebhookLoadContourIntegrationLoadContourProjector implements WebhookPayloadProjector
{
    public function project(MessageEnvelope $message, WebhookTopic $topic): array
    {
        return ['id' => 'order-1'];
    }
}


WHOLE PRODUCT FILE M products/flex-crm/CHANGELOG.md
# Changelog

## Unreleased — deterministic UUID schema (P08.2)

- Models use Laravel HasUuids (UUIDv7); migrations use literal table names and UUID columns without configuration or model lookups.

Все заметные изменения `flex/crm` документируются здесь. Формат — [Keep a Changelog](https://keepachangelog.com/en/1.1.0/).
Пакет pre-1.0 — breaking-изменения публичных контрактов легальны в MINOR до 1.0 (D17).

## [Unreleased]

- Schema delivery now uses tagged `SchemaSetProvider` declarations and `corex:schema:migrate`; migration publishing is removed.

### Changed

- CRM entities enter the runtime registry and morph map through the compiled `CrmModule` manifest; the provider no longer depends on a Testing registry.

- Breaking (PLAN3.P02–P04, pre-1.0): CRM stays a `flex/crm` product with one `Flex\Crm` PSR-4 root; lead/contact/company operations use typed request/result DTOs and explicit Api/Spi declarations. CRM model persistence is Internal, its errors use package-owned codes, and kernel dependencies use the `axiomasoft` Composer vendor.

- **Breaking (next MINOR >=0.3.0, pre-1.0):** additive full enabled CRM storage parity,
  canonical sales enums/fields/relations, lost reasons and activity targets; explicit
  CreateLead and LogActivity named-argument migration. No guessed legacy status,
  author, metadata or contact identity conversion. See UPGRADE.md.
- Existing seven company/contact operations publish typed input/output schemas;
  boolean inputs reject numeric/string lookalikes instead of reaching strict DTOs.
- Sales Actions honor observer vetoes transactionally; nullable deal amount preserves
  mandatory currency, and conversion pivot IDs use the owning configured-ID seam.

- **Breaking (next MINOR, pre-1.0):** approved company/contact fields, B-13 `{source,id,name}`
  provenance, deterministic display/channels/search and typed contact/company M2M replace
  legacy scalar fields and single-company relationship. Create Actions now accept CompanyData/
  ContactData; CompanyCreated/ContactCreated include Actor. ConvertLead is adapted explicitly.
- New public `Contracts/CrmExecutionContext::current(): ExecutionContext` requires authenticated
  execution and CoreX account context/connection bindings. Reads fail closed; nullable account
  records require explicit capability, workspace membership is rechecked and locked on writes.
- Additive parity migration verifies identity/workspace prerequisites, permits duplicate INN,
  and enforces FK/pair/primary/index contracts. Populated legacy upgrade rejects before changing
  data; no invented actors or silent metadata truncation. See UPGRADE.md.
- Final insert/update attribute guards prevent late/quiet Eloquent observers from bypassing
  admission/provenance invariants; owning Actions roll back observer veto without success events.

- **Breaking:** пакет и namespace переименованы `corex/crm`/`CoreX\Crm\` → `flex/crm`/`Flex\Crm\`
  (доктрина слоёв, D70/D72). Consumer-код обновляет `use`/`require` на новые имена (P2.0).


WHOLE PRODUCT FILE M products/flex-crm/UPGRADE.md
# Full CRM storage parity upgrade

This is a pre-1.0 breaking MINOR candidate after monorepo v0.2.0. No release tag is implied.
Back up the account database before a separately authorized installation/upgrade.

Fresh or empty legacy installations: initialize the existing `users`, `wsp_workspaces`
and `wsp_workspace_members` owning schema with UUID keys, compile the
installed modules, then enable `flex/crm` in that account connection. The additive
`2026_09_14_000002_company_contact_parity` migration replaces only empty historical
company/contact tables and creates sources and the typed association. Earlier migration
files remain unchanged. PostgreSQL 15+ is required; FlexCRM models generate UUIDv7 identifiers.

Populated legacy installations are explicitly unsupported by automatic in-place upgrade.
The migration throws `CRM_PARITY_UPGRADE_REQUIRES_EXPORT` before changing CRM tables.
Legacy storage has no reliable `created_by`; it also contains `custom_fields` and
`merged_into_id`, which cannot be silently discarded or added to the approved target.
The safe conversion procedure is an offline export into a new isolated account database:
retain a read-only source backup; obtain a provenance mapping for every source row;
map `website` to domain only after extracting/validating the hostname, name components
to the new names, scalar channels to normalized lists and `company_id` to a primary
association; retain source IDs only under the same ID profile; export custom/merge
metadata separately until its owning storage is approved. Validate tax-ID widths, all
references, complete row counts and provenance before any separately authorized cutover.
Do not label unknown historical authors as system/import or overwrite the source database.
A production backfill/cutover tool is outside this fresh first-consumer slice.

Replace `CreateCompany::handle(...)` with `handle(CompanyData $data)` and
`CreateContact::handle(...)` with `handle(ContactData $data)`. There are no silent
legacy aliases. Replace `Contact::company()` / `company_id` with `companies()` and
`AssociateContactCompany`; `Company::contacts()` is now a typed BelongsToMany.
`CompanyCreated` and `ContactCreated` now also carry the verified `Actor`.
`ConvertLead` has been adapted to the DTOs and a primary association in its original
transaction; its authenticated execution context is mandatory. Malformed legacy phones,
invalid owners or absent context reject and roll back conversion explicitly.

Disable preserves data. Re-enable is idempotent. Parity `down()` refuses a destructive
loss of target data: restore a verified backup only in an authorized maintenance operation.
Do not use module purge as an upgrade or rollback mechanism.

The subsequent `2026_09_15_000001_full_storage_parity` migration admits the approved
PostgreSQL CRM profile for all enabled tables, including lost reasons, activity targets
and the deal/contact association required by the existing conversion Action. It checks
all affected tables, matching ID types and owning prerequisites before transactional DDL.
It refuses ANY populated affected table, including already canonical company/contact
data; enabling a module is not authorization to delete or convert that data.

Sales API compatibility (next pre-1.0 MINOR, not a release): `CreateLead` now requires
`title`, with explicit optional `contactName`, `email`, `phone`, `companyName`, `ownerId`
and `sourceId`. Lead title is never interpreted as a human name. LeadStatus is
`Open/Converted/Junk`; no aliases for New/Contacted/Qualified/Unqualified exist.
Pipelines require `entity_type`, `code` and localized `name`; stages require `code`,
localized `name` and `semantic=open/won/lost`, not `is_won/is_lost` flags. Deal amount
is nullable minor units with mandatory currency (Money values remain supported);
`Deal::contact()`/`contact_id` become `contacts()`. ActivityType contains only
Call/Email/Meeting: Note and Task have no implied conversion. Activity target reads
use `targets()` or entity `activities()`, while activity `subject` is now text.
`LogActivity` retains summary-to-subject, but the named `userId` argument is replaced
by `ownerId`; this is ownership, NOT historical authorship. Legacy `metadata` is
explicitly rejected, not discarded. All sales writes require trusted context and
preserve observer veto rollback and after-commit events. Product adapters must be
updated explicitly before a separately authorized application installation.

                $t->timestampTz('stage_entered_at', null)->useCurrent();
                $t->decimal('position', 20, 10)->default(0);
                $t->text('search')->nullable();
                $this->timestamps($t, soft: true);
            });
            Schema::table('crm_leads', function (Blueprint $t): void {
                $t->foreign('converted_deal_id')->references('id')->on('crm_deals')->nullOnDelete();
            });
            Schema::create('crm_deal_contacts', function (Blueprint $t): void {
                $t->uuid('id')->primary();
                $t->foreignUuid('deal_id')->constrained('crm_deals')->cascadeOnDelete();
                $t->foreignUuid('contact_id')->constrained('crm_contacts')->cascadeOnDelete();
                $t->string('label', 64)->nullable();
                $t->boolean('is_primary')->default(false);
                $this->timestamps($t);
                $t->unique(['deal_id', 'contact_id']);
                $t->index('contact_id', 'crm_dc_contact_ix');
            });
            DB::statement('CREATE UNIQUE INDEX crm_dc_primary_uq ON crm_deal_contacts (deal_id) WHERE is_primary');
            Schema::create('crm_activities', function (Blueprint $t): void {

                $t->timestampTz('created_at', null)->useCurrent();

            });

                if (DB::getDriverName() === 'pgsql') {
                    foreach ($checks as $i => $check) {
                        DB::statement("ALTER TABLE {$table} ADD CONSTRAINT {$table}_parity_{$i}_ck CHECK ({$check})");
                    }
                }
            }
            foreach (['crm_stages_pipeline_ix' => ['crm_stages', 'pipeline_id, position', 'deleted_at IS NULL'], 'crm_leads_kanban_ix' => ['crm_leads', 'pipeline_id, stage_id, position', 'deleted_at IS NULL'], 'crm_leads_owner_ix' => ['crm_leads', 'owner_id, status', 'deleted_at IS NULL'], 'crm_deals_kanban_ix' => ['crm_deals', 'pipeline_id, stage_id, position', 'deleted_at IS NULL'], 'crm_deals_owner_ix' => ['crm_deals', 'owner_id, status', 'deleted_at IS NULL'], 'crm_deals_company_ix' => ['crm_deals', 'company_id', 'deleted_at IS NULL'], 'crm_deals_ws_ix' => ['crm_deals', 'workspace_id', 'deleted_at IS NULL'], 'crm_deals_close_ix' => ['crm_deals', 'close_date', "status = 'open' AND deleted_at IS NULL"], 'crm_deals_forecast_ix' => ['crm_deals', 'workspace_id, owner_id, pipeline_id, close_date, forecast_category', "status = 'open' AND close_date IS NOT NULL AND deleted_at IS NULL"], 'crm_activities_planned_ix' => ['crm_activities', 'owner_id, starts_at', "status = 'planned' AND deleted_at IS NULL"], 'crm_activities_kind_ix' => ['crm_activities', 'kind, status', 'deleted_at IS NULL']] as $name => [$table, $columns, $where]) {
                DB::statement("CREATE INDEX {$name} ON {$table} ({$columns}) WHERE {$where}");
            }

            if (DB::getDriverName() === 'pgsql') {
                DB::statement('ALTER TABLE crm_stages ALTER COLUMN probability SET DEFAULT 0');
                foreach (['crm_leads', 'crm_deals'] as $table) {
                    DB::statement("ALTER TABLE {$table} ALTER COLUMN position SET DEFAULT 0");
                    DB::statement("ALTER TABLE {$table} ALTER COLUMN search TYPE tsvector USING search::tsvector");
                    DB::statement("CREATE INDEX {$table}_search_ix ON {$table} USING GIN (search)");
                }
                // Historical company/contact parity used timestamp(0); the approved
                // precision is unrestricted. All tables were proved empty above.
                foreach (['crm_sources', 'crm_companies', 'crm_contacts', 'crm_contact_company', 'crm_lost_reasons', 'crm_pipelines', 'crm_stages', 'crm_leads', 'crm_deals', 'crm_deal_contacts', 'crm_activities', 'crm_activity_targets'] as $table) {
                    /** @var list<array{name: string, type: string, type_name: string, collation: string|null, nullable: bool, default: mixed, auto_increment: bool, comment: string|null, generation: array{type: string, expression: string|null}|null}> $columns */
                    $columns = Schema::getColumns($table);
                    foreach ($columns as $column) {
                        if ($column['type_name'] === 'timestamptz') {
                            DB::statement("ALTER TABLE {$table} ALTER COLUMN {$column['name']} TYPE timestamptz");
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
        });
    }

    /** @param list<string> $columns */
    private function scopedUnique(string $table, string $name, array $columns, ?string $where = null): void
    {
        if (DB::getDriverName() === 'pgsql' && $where === null) {
            DB::statement("ALTER TABLE {$table} ADD CONSTRAINT {$name} UNIQUE NULLS NOT DISTINCT (".implode(', ', $columns).')');

            return;
        }
        $columns = array_map(static fn (string $c): string => DB::getDriverName() !== 'pgsql' && $c === 'workspace_id' ? "COALESCE(workspace_id, '')" : $c, $columns);
        $nulls = DB::getDriverName() === 'pgsql' ? ' NULLS NOT DISTINCT' : '';
        DB::statement("CREATE UNIQUE INDEX {$name} ON {$table} (".implode(', ', $columns)."){$nulls}".($where === null ? '' : " WHERE {$where}"));
    }

    private function workspace(Blueprint $t): void
    {
        $t->foreignUuid('workspace_id')->nullable()->constrained('wsp_workspaces')->cascadeOnDelete();
    }

    private function source(Blueprint $t): void
    {
        $t->foreignUuid('source_id')->nullable()->constrained('crm_sources')->nullOnDelete();
    }

    private function lostReason(Blueprint $t): void
    {
        $t->foreignUuid('lost_reason_id')->nullable()->constrained('crm_lost_reasons')->nullOnDelete();
    }

    private function provenance(Blueprint $t): void
    {
        $t->foreignUuid('owner_id')->nullable()->constrained('users')->nullOnDelete();
        $t->jsonb('created_by');
    }

    private function amount(Blueprint $t): void
    {
        $t->bigInteger('amount')->nullable();
        $t->char('currency', 3)->default('RUB');
    }

    private function timestamps(Blueprint $t, bool $soft = false): void
    {
        $t->timestampTz('created_at', null)->useCurrent();
        $t->timestampTz('updated_at', null)->useCurrent();

        if ($soft) {
            $t->timestampTz('deleted_at', null)->nullable();
        }
    }

    public function down(): void
    {
        throw new RuntimeException('CRM parity rollback requires a verified provenance-preserving backup.');
    }
};

WHOLE PRODUCT FILE M products/flex-crm/src/ApiShared/CrmIdentifier.php
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

namespace Flex\Crm\ApiShared;

use Illuminate\Validation\ValidationException;

final class CrmIdentifier
{
    public static function fromPath(string $value): string
    {
        return self::from($value);
    }

    public static function fromInput(mixed $value): string
    {
        return self::from($value);
    }

    private static function from(mixed $value): string
    {
        if (is_string($value)) {
            return $value;
        }

        throw ValidationException::withMessages(['id' => 'CRM identifier must be a string.']);
    }
}


WHOLE PRODUCT FILE M products/flex-crm/src/Contact/Models/ContactCompany.php
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

namespace Flex\Crm\Contact\Models;

use Flex\Crm\Company\Models\Company;
use Flex\Crm\Context\ContextGuard;
use Illuminate\Auth\Access\AuthorizationException;
use Illuminate\Database\Eloquent\Builder;
use Illuminate\Database\Eloquent\Concerns\HasUuids;
use Illuminate\Database\Eloquent\Relations\BelongsTo;
use Illuminate\Database\Eloquent\Relations\Pivot;
use Override;

/**
 * @property string $id
 * @property string $contact_id
 * @property string $company_id
 * @property ?string $label
 * @property bool $is_primary
 */
final class ContactCompany extends Pivot
{
    use HasUuids;

    protected $table = 'crm_contact_company';

    protected $guarded = ['id'];

    #[Override]
    protected static function booted(): void
    {
        self::addGlobalScope('crm-context', static function (Builder $builder): void {
            $ctx = app(ContextGuard::class)->current();

            if ($builder->getModel()->getConnection()->getName() !== $ctx->connectionName) {
                throw new AuthorizationException('CRM association query requires its context connection.');
            }
            $builder->whereHas('contact')->whereHas('company');
        });
        self::saving(static fn (self $link) => $link->assertContext());
    }

    private function assertContext(): void
    {
        $guard = app(ContextGuard::class);
        $ctx = $guard->current(lock: $this->getConnection()->transactionLevel() > 0);

        if ($this->getConnection()->getName() !== $ctx->connectionName) {
            throw new AuthorizationException('CRM association write requires its context connection.');
        }
        $contact = Contact::on($ctx->connectionName)->findOrFail($this->contact_id);
        $company = Company::on($ctx->connectionName)->findOrFail($this->company_id);

        if ($contact->workspace_id !== $company->workspace_id) {
            throw new AuthorizationException('CRM association requires identical workspace scopes.');
        }

        if ($contact->workspace_id === null && ! $ctx->allowAccountLevel) {
            throw new AuthorizationException('CRM account association requires account-level capability.');
        }
    }

    /** @return array<string, mixed> */
    #[Override]
    protected function getAttributesForInsert(): array
    {
        $this->assertContext();

        return parent::getAttributesForInsert();
    }

    /** @return array<string, mixed> */
    #[Override]
    protected function getDirtyForUpdate(): array
    {
        $this->assertContext();

        return parent::getDirtyForUpdate();
    }

    /** @return BelongsTo<Contact, $this> */
    public function contact(): BelongsTo
    {
        return $this->belongsTo(Contact::class);
    }

    /** @return BelongsTo<Company, $this> */
    public function company(): BelongsTo
    {
        return $this->belongsTo(Company::class);
    }

    #[Override]
    protected function casts(): array
    {
        return ['is_primary' => 'boolean'];
    }
}


WHOLE PRODUCT FILE M products/flex-crm/src/Context/ContextGuard.php
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

namespace Flex\Crm\Context;

use CoreX\Context\Contracts\AccountConnectionResolver;
use CoreX\Context\Contracts\TenantContextResolver;
use CoreX\Modules\Contracts\ModuleRegistry;
use Flex\Crm\Context\Contracts\CrmExecutionContext;
use Flex\Crm\Context\Data\ExecutionContext;
use Illuminate\Auth\Access\AuthorizationException;
use Illuminate\Database\Connection;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\DB;
use Illuminate\Support\Str;

/** @internal Mandatory package admission; consumer adapters can only narrow it. */
final readonly class ContextGuard
{
    public function __construct(
        private CrmExecutionContext $execution,
        private TenantContextResolver $tenants,
        private AccountConnectionResolver $connections,
        private ModuleRegistry $modules,
    ) {}

    public function current(bool $lock = false): ExecutionContext
    {
        $ctx = $this->execution->current();
        $tenant = $this->tenants->current();

        if ($tenant->isBoxed() || $tenant->account->status !== 'active' || $tenant->account->id !== $ctx->tenant->account->id || $tenant->workspace?->id !== $ctx->tenant->workspace?->id) {
            throw new AuthorizationException('CRM requires the active physical account/workspace context.');
        }
        $db = $this->connection($ctx);
        $expected = $this->connections->resolve($tenant->account);
        foreach (['driver', 'host', 'port', 'database', 'username'] as $key) {
            if ((string) $db->getConfig($key) !== (string) $expected->{$key}) {
                throw new AuthorizationException('CRM connection does not belong to the current account.');
            }
        }

        if ($db->getDriverName() === 'pgsql' && $db->selectOne('SELECT current_database() AS name')->name !== $expected->database) {
            throw new AuthorizationException('CRM active PDO database differs from the account connection.');
        }

        if (! $this->modules->isActive('flex/crm', $tenant)) {
            throw new AuthorizationException('CRM is not enabled in the current account.');
        }

        if ($ctx->actor->source === 'user' && $ctx->actor->id !== (string) $ctx->principalId) {
            throw new AuthorizationException('CRM actor does not match the authenticated principal.');
        }
        $this->user(ctx: $ctx, id: $ctx->principalId, lock: $lock);

        if ($tenant->workspace === null && ! $ctx->allowAccountLevel) {
            throw new AuthorizationException('An explicit account-level capability is required without a workspace.');
        }

        return $ctx;
    }

    public function connection(ExecutionContext $ctx): Connection
    {
        return DB::connection($ctx->connectionName);
    }

    public function user(ExecutionContext $ctx, string|int $id, bool $lock = false): void
    {
        $this->id($id);
        $db = $this->connection($ctx);
        $user = $db->table('users')->where('id', $id)->where('is_active', true)->whereNull('deleted_at');

        if ($lock) {
            $user->sharedLock();
        }

        if ($user->first() === null) {
            throw new AuthorizationException('CRM principal/owner must be an active user in this account.');
        }

        if ($ctx->tenant->workspace !== null) {
            $workspace = $db->table('wsp_workspaces')->where('id', $ctx->tenant->workspace->id)->whereNull('deleted_at');
            $membership = $db->table('wsp_workspace_members')->where('workspace_id', $ctx->tenant->workspace->id)->where('user_id', $id)->where('status', 'active');

            if ($lock) {
                $workspace->sharedLock();
                $membership->sharedLock();
            }

            if ($workspace->first() === null || $membership->first() === null) {
                throw new AuthorizationException('CRM workspace membership is missing or revoked.');
            }
        }
    }

    public function workspace(ExecutionContext $ctx, bool $accountLevel): ?string
    {
        if ($accountLevel) {
            if (! $ctx->allowAccountLevel) {
                throw new AuthorizationException('CRM account-level writes require an explicit capability.');
            }

            return null;
        }
        $workspace = $ctx->tenant->workspace ?? throw new AuthorizationException('CRM workspace write requires a workspace.');

        return $workspace->id;
    }

    public function id(string|int $id): void
    {
        $valid = is_string($id) && Str::isUuid($id);

        if (! $valid) {
            throw new AuthorizationException('CRM reference has an invalid UUID.');
        }
    }
}

WHOLE PRODUCT FILE M products/flex-crm/src/Lead/Actions/ConvertLead.php
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

namespace Flex\Crm\Lead\Actions;

use Brick\Money\Money;
use Flex\Crm\Company\Actions\CreateCompany;
use Flex\Crm\Company\Data\CompanyData;
use Flex\Crm\Contact\Actions\AssociateContactCompany;
use Flex\Crm\Contact\Actions\CreateContact;
use Flex\Crm\Contact\Data\ContactData;
use Flex\Crm\Contact\Models\Contact;
use Flex\Crm\Context\ContextGuard;
use Flex\Crm\Deal\Actions\CreateDeal;
use Flex\Crm\Deal\Models\Pipeline;
use Flex\Crm\Lead\Enums\LeadStatus;
use Flex\Crm\Lead\Events\LeadConverted;
use Flex\Crm\Lead\Models\Lead;
use Illuminate\Auth\Access\AuthorizationException;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\DB;
use Illuminate\Validation\ValidationException;

/**
 * Converts a lead into a Contact (+ optional Company, + optional Deal in the
 * given pipeline). Composite use-case: composes the create-* Actions inside one
 * transaction rather than via an orchestration service.
 */
final class ConvertLead
{
    public function __construct(
        private readonly CreateContact $createContact,
        private readonly CreateCompany $createCompany,
        private readonly CreateDeal $createDeal,
        private readonly AssociateContactCompany $associate,
        private readonly ContextGuard $guard,
    ) {}

    public function handle(Lead $lead, ?Pipeline $pipeline = null, bool $withCompany = false): Contact
    {
        $ctx = $this->guard->current();

        if ($lead->getConnection()->getName() !== $ctx->connectionName || ($pipeline !== null && $pipeline->getConnection()->getName() !== $ctx->connectionName)) {
            throw new AuthorizationException('Lead conversion requires the current trusted account connection.');
        }

        return DB::usingConnection($lead->getConnectionName(), fn (): Contact => $lead->getConnection()->transaction(function () use ($lead, $pipeline, $withCompany): Contact {
            $fresh = $lead->newQuery()->whereKey($lead->getKey())->lockForUpdate()->firstOrFail();
            $this->guard->current(lock: true);
            $lead->setRawAttributes($fresh->getAttributes(), true);

            if ($lead->status === LeadStatus::Converted) {
                return Contact::on($lead->getConnectionName())->findOrFail($lead->converted_contact_id);
            }

            if ($lead->status !== LeadStatus::Open || (($lead->contact_name === null || trim($lead->contact_name) === '') && ($lead->email === null || $lead->email === '') && ($lead->phone === null || $lead->phone === ''))) {
                throw ValidationException::withMessages(['lead' => 'Conversion requires an open lead and explicit contact identity.']);
            }

            $company = null;

            if ($withCompany && $lead->company_name !== null && $lead->company_name !== '') {
                $company = $this->createCompany->handle(new CompanyData(name: $lead->company_name, ownerId: $lead->owner_id, sourceId: $lead->source_id));
            }

            $contact = $this->createContact->handle(new ContactData(
                nameFirst: $lead->contact_name,
                emails: $lead->email === null || $lead->email === '' ? [] : [['value' => $lead->email]],
                phones: $lead->phone === null || $lead->phone === '' ? [] : [['value' => $lead->phone]],
                ownerId: $lead->owner_id,
                sourceId: $lead->source_id,
            ));

            if ($company !== null) {
                $this->associate->handle(contactId: $contact->id, companyId: $company->id, isPrimary: true);
            }

            $deal = null;

            if ($pipeline instanceof Pipeline) {
                $deal = $this->createDeal->handle(pipeline: $pipeline, title: $lead->title, amount: $lead->amount === null ? null : Money::ofMinor($lead->amount, $lead->currency), ownerId: $lead->owner_id);
                $deal->company_id = $company?->id;
                $deal->source_id = $lead->source_id;

                if (! $deal->save()) {
                    throw ValidationException::withMessages(['deal' => 'Deal update was vetoed.']);
                }
                $values = ['deal_id' => $deal->id, 'contact_id' => $contact->id, 'is_primary' => true, 'created_at' => now(), 'updated_at' => now()];

                $values['id'] = $deal->newUniqueId();
                $deal->getConnection()->table('crm_deal_contacts')->insert($values);
            }

            $lead->status = LeadStatus::Converted;
            $lead->converted_contact_id = $contact->id;
            $lead->converted_company_id = $company?->id;
            $lead->converted_deal_id = $deal?->id;
            $lead->converted_at = now();

            if (! $lead->save()) {
                throw ValidationException::withMessages(['lead' => 'Lead conversion was vetoed.']);
            }

            LeadConverted::dispatch($lead, $contact);

            return $contact;
        }));
    }
}


WHOLE PRODUCT FILE M products/flex-crm/tests/Feature/ApiOperationsTest.php
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

use CoreX\Context\Data\TenantContext;
use CoreX\Modules\Contracts\ModuleRegistry;
use CoreX\Modules\Internal\Compiler\RegistryCompiler;
use CoreX\Operations\Authorization\Contracts\OperationAuthorizer;
use CoreX\Operations\Authorization\Contracts\Principal;
use CoreX\Operations\Authorization\Contracts\PrincipalResolver;
use CoreX\Operations\Definition\Data\Operation;
use CoreX\Operations\Invocation\Contracts\ContextResolver;
use CoreX\Operations\Invocation\Contracts\RequestContext;
use CoreX\Operations\Invocation\Data\InvocationMetadata;
use CoreX\Operations\Invocation\Internal\OperationDispatcher;
use CoreX\Operations\Invocation\Laravel\ResolvedContexts;
use Flex\Crm\ApiShared\CrmIdentifier;
use Flex\Crm\CrmModule;
use Flex\Crm\Tests\Support\TestContext;
use Illuminate\Http\JsonResponse;
use Illuminate\Http\Request;
use Illuminate\Validation\ValidationException;

it('declares and dispatches company, contact and association operations through the platform boundary', function (): void {
    $compiled = app(RegistryCompiler::class)->compile(manifests: [(new CrmModule)->manifest()], installedPackages: []);
    $registry = Mockery::mock(ModuleRegistry::class);
    $registry->shouldReceive('compiled')->andReturn($compiled);
    $registry->shouldReceive('isActive')->with('flex/crm', Mockery::type(TenantContext::class))->andReturn(true);
    app()->instance(ModuleRegistry::class, $registry);

    $principal = new class implements Principal
    {
        public function id(): string
        {
            return 'trusted-user';
        }
    };
    app()->instance(PrincipalResolver::class, new class($principal) implements PrincipalResolver
    {
        public function __construct(private readonly Principal $principal) {}

        public function resolve(Request $request): ?Principal
        {
            return $this->principal;
        }
    });
    app()->instance(ContextResolver::class, new class($this->crmContext, $principal) implements ContextResolver
    {
        public function __construct(
            private readonly TestContext $context,
            private readonly Principal $principal,
        ) {}

        public function resolve(Request $request, Principal $principal, InvocationMetadata $metadata): RequestContext
        {
            return ResolvedContexts::make($this->context->current()->tenant, $this->principal, $metadata);
        }
    });
    app()->instance(OperationAuthorizer::class, new class implements OperationAuthorizer
    {
        public function allows(Operation $operation, RequestContext $context): bool
        {
            return str_starts_with($operation->ability, 'crm.');
        }
    });

    $dispatch = function (string $method, string $path, array $input = []): JsonResponse {
        $request = Request::create(
            uri: '/api/v1/'.$path,
            method: $method,
            parameters: $method === 'GET' ? $input : [],
            server: ['CONTENT_TYPE' => 'application/json'],
            content: $method === 'GET' ? null : json_encode($input, JSON_THROW_ON_ERROR),
        );

        return app(OperationDispatcher::class)->dispatch(request: $request, version: 'v1', path: $path);
    };

    $company = $dispatch('POST', 'companies', ['name' => 'Acme', 'inn' => '7700000000']);
    expect($company->getStatusCode())->toBe(201)
        ->and($company->getData(true)['data']['name'])->toBe('Acme');
    $companyId = $company->getData(true)['data']['id'];

    foreach ([0, 1, '0', '1'] as $notBoolean) {
        expect($dispatch('POST', 'companies', ['name' => 'Invalid flag', 'account_level' => $notBoolean])->getStatusCode())->toBe(422);
    }

    $contact = $dispatch('POST', 'contacts', ['name_first' => 'Jane', 'emails' => [['value' => 'jane@acme.test']]]);
    expect($contact->getStatusCode())->toBe(201)
        ->and($contact->getData(true)['data']['display_name'])->toBe('Jane');
    $contactId = $contact->getData(true)['data']['id'];

    expect($dispatch('POST', 'contacts/'.$contactId.'/companies', ['company_id' => $companyId, 'is_primary' => 1])->getStatusCode())->toBe(422);

    $association = $dispatch('POST', 'contacts/'.$contactId.'/companies', ['company_id' => $companyId, 'is_primary' => true]);
    expect($association->getStatusCode())->toBe(201)
        ->and($association->getData(true)['data']['is_primary'])->toBeTrue();
    $companies = $dispatch('GET', 'companies');
    expect($companies->getStatusCode())->toBe(200)
        ->and($companies->getData(true)['data']['items'])->toHaveCount(1);
    $contacts = $dispatch('GET', 'contacts', ['limit' => 1]);
    expect($contacts->getStatusCode())->toBe(200)
        ->and($contacts->getData(true)['data']['items'])->toHaveCount(1)
        ->and($contacts->getData(true)['data']['items'][0]['id'])->toBe($contactId);
    $shownContact = $dispatch('GET', 'contacts/'.$contactId);
    expect($shownContact->getStatusCode())->toBe(200)
        ->and($shownContact->getData(true)['data']['id'])->toBe($contactId);
    $forgedOwner = $dispatch('POST', 'companies', ['name' => 'Hidden', 'owner_id' => 'forged']);
    expect($forgedOwner->getStatusCode())->toBe(403)
        ->and($forgedOwner->getData(true)['code'])->toBe('operations.forbidden');
    $undeclared = $dispatch('POST', 'companies', ['name' => 'Hidden', 'actor' => 'forged']);
    expect($undeclared->getStatusCode())->toBe(422)
        ->and($undeclared->getData(true)['code'])->toBe('operations.undeclared_fields');
});

it('preserves string CRM identifiers and rejects non-string input', function (): void {
    $id = '01932f5a-0000-7000-8000-000000000001';

    expect(CrmIdentifier::fromPath($id))->toBe($id)
        ->and(CrmIdentifier::fromInput($id))->toBe($id)
        ->and(fn (): int|string => CrmIdentifier::fromInput(8))->toThrow(ValidationException::class);
});


WHOLE PRODUCT FILE M products/flex-crm/tests/Pg/ActivityIdTest.php
<?php

it('stores and resolves UUIDv7 activity subjects on PostgreSQL', function (): void {
    config(['database.default' => 'pgsql']);
    DB::statement('DROP SCHEMA IF EXISTS public CASCADE');
    DB::statement('CREATE SCHEMA public');
    AccountFixture::migrate();
    $this->migrateSchemaSets(['flex/crm:tenant'], DB::getDefaultConnection());
    $ctx = TestContext::seed('pgsql')->current();

    expect((string) $activity->fresh()->targets()->sole()->entity->getKey())->toBe((string) $subject->getKey())
        ->and($subject->getKey()[14])->toBe('7')
        ->and($activity->getKey()[14])->toBe('7');

WHOLE PRODUCT FILE M products/flex-crm/tests/Pg/ParityContractTest.php
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

use CoreX\Config\Data\CoreOptions;
use CoreX\Context\Contracts\AccountConnectionResolver;
use CoreX\Context\Data\DatabaseConnectionConfig;
use CoreX\Context\Data\TenantContext;
use CoreX\Context\Data\WorkspaceRef;
use Flex\Crm\Company\Actions\CreateCompany;
use Flex\Crm\Company\Data\CompanyData;
use Flex\Crm\Company\Events\CompanyCreated;
use Flex\Crm\Company\Models\Company;
use Flex\Crm\Contact\Actions\AssociateContactCompany;
use Flex\Crm\Contact\Actions\CreateContact;
use Flex\Crm\Contact\Data\ContactData;
use Flex\Crm\Contact\Events\ContactCompanyAssociated;
use Flex\Crm\Contact\Events\ContactCreated;
use Flex\Crm\Context\Data\Actor;
use Flex\Crm\Context\Data\ExecutionContext;
use Flex\Crm\Context\Models\Source;
use Flex\Crm\Tests\Support\AccountFixture;
use Flex\Crm\Tests\Support\TestContext;
use Illuminate\Auth\Access\AuthorizationException;
use Illuminate\Database\Eloquent\ModelNotFoundException;
use Illuminate\Database\QueryException;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\DB;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Event;
use Illuminate\Support\Str;
use Illuminate\Validation\ValidationException;

uses()->group('pg');

beforeEach(function (): void {
    config(['database.default' => 'pgsql']);
    app()->forgetInstance(CoreOptions::class);
    DB::statement('DROP SCHEMA IF EXISTS public CASCADE');
    DB::statement('CREATE SCHEMA public');
    AccountFixture::migrate();
    $this->migrateSchemaSets(['flex/crm:tenant'], DB::getDefaultConnection());
    $this->crmContext = TestContext::seed('pgsql');
});

it('matches approved PostgreSQL fields lengths defaults indexes and foreign keys', function (): void {
    $columns = collect(DB::select("SELECT table_name,column_name,data_type,character_maximum_length,is_nullable,column_default FROM information_schema.columns WHERE table_schema='public' AND table_name IN ('crm_companies','crm_contacts','crm_contact_company','crm_sources')"))->groupBy('table_name');
    $expected = [
        'crm_companies' => ['id', 'workspace_id', 'name', 'legal_name', 'domain', 'inn', 'kpp', 'ogrn', 'phones', 'emails', 'address', 'bank_details', 'source_id', 'owner_id', 'created_by', 'search', 'created_at', 'updated_at', 'deleted_at'],
        'crm_contacts' => ['id', 'workspace_id', 'name_last', 'name_first', 'name_middle', 'display_name', 'job_title', 'phones', 'emails', 'birthday', 'source_id', 'owner_id', 'created_by', 'search', 'created_at', 'updated_at', 'deleted_at'],
        'crm_contact_company' => ['id', 'contact_id', 'company_id', 'label', 'is_primary', 'created_at', 'updated_at'],
        'crm_sources' => ['id', 'workspace_id', 'code', 'name', 'is_active', 'position', 'created_at', 'updated_at'],
    ];
    foreach ($expected as $table => $names) {
        expect($columns[$table]->pluck('column_name')->all())->toEqualCanonicalizing($names);
        $map = $columns[$table]->keyBy('column_name');
        expect($map['id']->data_type)->toBe('uuid')->and($map['created_at']->data_type)->toBe('timestamp with time zone')
            ->and($map['created_at']->is_nullable)->toBe('NO')->and($map['created_at']->column_default)->toBe('CURRENT_TIMESTAMP');

        if (isset($map['phones'])) {
            expect($map['phones']->data_type)->toBe('jsonb')->and($map['phones']->column_default)->toBe("'[]'::jsonb")
                ->and($map['created_by']->is_nullable)->toBe('NO')->and($map['search']->data_type)->toBe('tsvector')
                ->and($map['workspace_id']->is_nullable)->toBe('YES');
        }
    }
    foreach (['domain' => 190, 'inn' => 12, 'kpp' => 9, 'ogrn' => 15] as $field => $length) {
        expect($columns['crm_companies']->keyBy('column_name')[$field]->character_maximum_length)->toBe($length);
    }
    foreach (['name_first' => 120, 'name_last' => 120, 'name_middle' => 120, 'display_name' => 255, 'job_title' => 190] as $field => $length) {
        expect($columns['crm_contacts']->keyBy('column_name')[$field]->character_maximum_length)->toBe($length);
    }
    $indexes = collect(DB::select("SELECT indexname,indexdef FROM pg_indexes WHERE schemaname='public'"))->keyBy('indexname');
    foreach (['crm_companies', 'crm_contacts'] as $table) {
        expect($indexes[$table.'_search_ix']->indexdef)->toContain('USING gin (search)')
            ->and($indexes[$table.'_phones_ix']->indexdef)->toContain('jsonb_path_ops')
            ->and($indexes[$table.'_emails_ix']->indexdef)->toContain('jsonb_path_ops')
            ->and($indexes[$table.'_ws_ix']->indexdef)->toContain('WHERE (deleted_at IS NULL)')
            ->and($indexes[$table.'_owner_ix']->indexdef)->toContain('WHERE (deleted_at IS NULL)');
    }
    expect($indexes['crm_companies_inn_ix']->indexdef)->not->toContain('UNIQUE')
        ->and($indexes['crm_companies_inn_ix']->indexdef)->toContain('inn IS NOT NULL')
        ->and($indexes['crm_cc_primary_uq']->indexdef)->toContain('UNIQUE', '(contact_id)', 'WHERE is_primary')
        ->and($indexes['crm_cc_company_ix']->indexdef)->toContain('(company_id)')
        ->and($indexes['crm_sources_workspace_id_code_unique']->indexdef)->toContain('NULLS NOT DISTINCT');
    $fks = collect(DB::select("SELECT conrelid::regclass::text AS tbl,pg_get_constraintdef(oid) AS def FROM pg_constraint WHERE contype='f'"));
    foreach (['crm_companies', 'crm_contacts'] as $table) {
        $defs = $fks->where('tbl', $table)->pluck('def')->implode(' ');
        expect($defs)->toContain('FOREIGN KEY (owner_id) REFERENCES users(id) ON DELETE SET NULL', 'FOREIGN KEY (source_id) REFERENCES crm_sources(id) ON DELETE SET NULL', 'FOREIGN KEY (workspace_id) REFERENCES wsp_workspaces(id) ON DELETE CASCADE');
    }
    expect($fks->where('tbl', 'crm_contact_company')->pluck('def')->implode(' '))->toContain('REFERENCES crm_contacts(id) ON DELETE CASCADE', 'REFERENCES crm_companies(id) ON DELETE CASCADE');
});

it('roundtrips all approved company and contact values and search', function (): void {
    $ctx = $this->crmContext->current();
    $source = Source::create(['workspace_id' => $ctx->tenant->workspace->id, 'code' => 'web', 'name' => ['ru' => 'Сайт']]);
    $company = app(CreateCompany::class)->handle(new CompanyData(name: ' Компания ', legalName: 'ООО Компания', domain: 'example.test', inn: '123456789012', kpp: '123456789', ogrn: '123456789012345', phones: [['value' => '+1 (650) 253-0000', 'type' => 'work', 'is_primary' => true]], emails: [['value' => 'USER@example.test']], address: ['city' => 'Москва'], bankDetails: ['bik' => '044525225'], sourceId: $source->id, ownerId: $ctx->principalId))->fresh();
    $contact = app(CreateContact::class)->handle(new ContactData(nameFirst: '  Иван  ', nameLast: 'Иванов', nameMiddle: 'Иванович', jobTitle: str_repeat('a', 190), birthday: '2000-02-29', phones: [['value' => '+16502530000']], emails: [['value' => 'C@example.test']], sourceId: $source->id, ownerId: $ctx->principalId))->fresh();
    expect($company->name)->toBe('Компания')->and($company->inn)->toBe('123456789012')->and($company->kpp)->toBe('123456789')->and($company->ogrn)->toBe('123456789012345')
        ->and($company->domain)->toBe('example.test')->and($company->phones[0]['norm'])->toBe('+16502530000')->and($company->address)->toBe(['city' => 'Москва'])
        ->and($company->bank_details)->toBe(['bik' => '044525225'])->and($company->owner_id)->toBe($ctx->principalId)->and($company->source_id)->toBe($source->id)
        ->and($contact->display_name)->toBe('Иванов Иван Иванович')->and($contact->birthday->format('Y-m-d'))->toBe('2000-02-29')
        ->and($contact->created_by)->toEqual($ctx->actor->toArray())->and($company->id[14])->toBe('7');
    expect(DB::selectOne('SELECT search @@ plainto_tsquery(\'russian\', ?) AS found FROM crm_companies WHERE id=?', ['Компания', $company->id])->found)->toBeTrue();
    $duplicate = app(CreateCompany::class)->handle(new CompanyData(name: 'Duplicate', inn: '123456789012'));
    expect($duplicate->fresh()->phones)->toBe([])->and($duplicate->emails)->toBe([]);
});

it('uses deterministic channel fallback and nullable whitespace components', function (): void {
    $contact = app(CreateContact::class)->handle(new ContactData(nameFirst: ' ', nameMiddle: ' ', emails: [['value' => 'b@example.test'], ['value' => 'A@example.test', 'is_primary' => true]]));
    expect($contact->name_first)->toBeNull()->and($contact->display_name)->toBe('a@example.test');
    $phone = app(CreateContact::class)->handle(new ContactData(phones: [['value' => '+1 650 253 0000']]));
    expect($phone->display_name)->toBe('+16502530000');
    $long = app(CreateContact::class)->handle(new ContactData(nameFirst: str_repeat('a', 120), nameLast: str_repeat('b', 120), nameMiddle: str_repeat('c', 120)));
    expect(mb_strlen($long->display_name))->toBe(255);
});

it('rejects malformed channels without storing a contact', function (array $channels, bool $phone): void {
    expect(fn () => app(CreateContact::class)->handle(new ContactData(nameFirst: 'Valid', phones: $phone ? $channels : [], emails: $phone ? [] : $channels)))->toThrow(ValidationException::class);
    expect(DB::table('crm_contacts')->count())->toBe(0);
})->with([
    [[['value' => '']], false], [[['value' => 'invalid']], false], [[['value' => 'a@example.test'], ['value' => 'A@example.test']], false],
    [[['value' => 'a@example.test', 'is_primary' => true], ['value' => 'b@example.test', 'is_primary' => true]], false],
    [[['value' => 'a@example.test', 'is_primary' => 'true']], false], [[['value' => 'a@example.test', 'unexpected' => 1]], false],
    [[['value' => '6502530000']], true], [[['value' => '+123']], true], [[['value' => '+16502530000 ext 1']], true],
    [[['value' => '+16502530000'], ['value' => '+1 650 253 0000']], true], [['value' => 'a@example.test'], false],
]);

it('rejects invalid dates and field widths', function (): void {
    expect(fn () => app(CreateContact::class)->handle(new ContactData(nameFirst: 'Valid', birthday: '2023-02-29')))->toThrow(ValidationException::class);
    expect(fn () => app(CreateCompany::class)->handle(new CompanyData(name: 'Valid', inn: str_repeat('1', 13))))->toThrow(ValidationException::class);
    expect(fn () => app(CreateContact::class)->handle(new ContactData(nameFirst: str_repeat('a', 121))))->toThrow(ValidationException::class);
});

it('fails closed for missing context and revoked membership on reads and writes', function (): void {
    $company = app(CreateCompany::class)->handle(new CompanyData(name: 'Visible'));
    $ctx = $this->crmContext->current();
    DB::table('wsp_workspace_members')->where('user_id', $ctx->principalId)->update(['status' => 'revoked']);
    expect(fn () => Company::find($company->id))->toThrow(AuthorizationException::class);
    expect(fn () => app(CreateCompany::class)->handle(new CompanyData(name: 'Denied')))->toThrow(AuthorizationException::class);

    expect(fn () => Company::count())->toThrow(AuthorizationException::class);
});

it('separates NULL account data current workspace and foreign workspaces', function (): void {
    $ctx = $this->crmContext->current();
    $local = app(CreateCompany::class)->handle(new CompanyData(name: 'Local'));
    expect(fn () => app(CreateCompany::class)->handle(new CompanyData(name: 'Denied NULL', accountLevel: true)))->toThrow(AuthorizationException::class);
    $this->crmContext->context = new ExecutionContext(tenant: $ctx->tenant, connectionName: 'pgsql', actor: $ctx->actor, principalId: $ctx->principalId, allowAccountLevel: true);
    $global = app(CreateCompany::class)->handle(new CompanyData(name: 'Global', accountLevel: true));
    $this->crmContext->context = $ctx;
    expect(Company::pluck('id')->all())->toBe([$local->id]);
    $ws = (string) Str::uuid7();
    DB::table('wsp_workspaces')->insert(['id' => $ws, 'slug' => 'other']);
    DB::table('wsp_workspace_members')->insert(['id' => (string) Str::uuid7(), 'workspace_id' => $ws, 'user_id' => $ctx->principalId, 'status' => 'active']);
    $otherTenant = new TenantContext($ctx->tenant->account, new WorkspaceRef(id: $ws, slug: 'other', type: 'main', path: '', parentId: null));
    $this->crmContext->context = new ExecutionContext(tenant: $otherTenant, connectionName: 'pgsql', actor: $ctx->actor, principalId: $ctx->principalId);
    expect(Company::count())->toBe(0);
    expect(fn () => app(AssociateContactCompany::class)->handle(contactId: (string) Str::uuid7(), companyId: $local->id))->toThrow(ModelNotFoundException::class);
    $this->crmContext->context = new ExecutionContext(tenant: new TenantContext($ctx->tenant->account), connectionName: 'pgsql', actor: $ctx->actor, principalId: $ctx->principalId, allowAccountLevel: true);
    expect(Company::pluck('id')->all())->toBe([$global->id]);
    expect(fn () => app(CreateCompany::class)->handle(new CompanyData(name: 'Missing workspace')))->toThrow(AuthorizationException::class);
});

    $ctx = $this->crmContext->current();
    $this->crmContext->context = new ExecutionContext(tenant: $ctx->tenant, connectionName: 'pgsql', actor: $ctx->actor, principalId: $ctx->principalId, allowAccountLevel: true);

});

it('rejects foreign or inactive source and owner references', function (): void {

    expect(fn () => app(CreateContact::class)->handle(new ContactData(nameFirst: 'Foreign', sourceId: (string) Str::uuid7())))->toThrow(AuthorizationException::class);

it('rejects forged actors and mismatched account connections', function (): void {
    $ctx = $this->crmContext->current();
    $this->crmContext->context = new ExecutionContext(tenant: $ctx->tenant, connectionName: 'pgsql', actor: new Actor(source: 'user', id: (string) Str::uuid7(), name: 'Forged'), principalId: $ctx->principalId);
    expect(fn () => app(CreateCompany::class)->handle(new CompanyData(name: 'Forgery')))->toThrow(AuthorizationException::class);
    $this->crmContext->context = $ctx;
    $resolver = Mockery::mock(AccountConnectionResolver::class);
    $resolver->shouldReceive('resolve')->andReturn(new DatabaseConnectionConfig(driver: 'pgsql', host: '127.0.0.1', port: 5434, database: 'foreign_account_test', username: 'corex'));
    app()->instance(AccountConnectionResolver::class, $resolver);
    expect(fn () => Company::count())->toThrow(AuthorizationException::class);
    expect(DB::table('crm_companies')->count())->toBe(0);
});

it('stores each admitted B13 actor kind through a trusted run-as context', function (string $source): void {
    $ctx = $this->crmContext->current();
    $actor = new Actor(source: $source, id: $source === 'user' ? (string) $ctx->principalId : 'verified-'.$source, name: 'Verified '.$source);
    $this->crmContext->context = new ExecutionContext(tenant: $ctx->tenant, connectionName: 'pgsql', actor: $actor, principalId: $ctx->principalId);
    $company = app(CreateCompany::class)->handle(new CompanyData(name: 'Actor'));
    expect($company->fresh()->created_by)->toEqual($actor->toArray());
})->with(['user', 'agent', 'api', 'workflow', 'import', 'system']);

it('dispatches mutation events only after the successful outer commit', function (): void {
    $events = [];
    foreach ([CompanyCreated::class, ContactCreated::class, ContactCompanyAssociated::class] as $event) {
        Event::listen($event, function ($value) use (&$events): void {
            $events[] = $value::class;
        });
    }
    $create = function () use (&$events): void {
        $company = app(CreateCompany::class)->handle(new CompanyData(name: 'Commit'));
        $contact = app(CreateContact::class)->handle(new ContactData(nameFirst: 'Commit'));
        app(AssociateContactCompany::class)->handle(contactId: $contact->id, companyId: $company->id);
        expect($events)->toBe([]);
    };
    expect(fn () => DB::transaction(function () use ($create): void {
        $create();

        throw new RuntimeException('rollback');
    }))->toThrow(RuntimeException::class);
    expect($events)->toBe([])->and(DB::table('crm_companies')->count())->toBe(0);
    DB::transaction($create);
    expect($events)->toBe([CompanyCreated::class, ContactCreated::class, ContactCompanyAssociated::class]);
});

it('keeps committed data when a synchronous after-commit listener fails', function (): void {
    Event::listen(CompanyCreated::class, static function (): void {
        throw new RuntimeException('listener');
    });
    expect(fn () => app(CreateCompany::class)->handle(new CompanyData(name: 'Committed')))->toThrow(RuntimeException::class, 'listener');
    expect(DB::table('crm_companies')->where('name', 'Committed')->count())->toBe(1);
});

it('preserves pivot on soft delete and applies hard delete SET NULL and CASCADE', function (): void {
    $ctx = $this->crmContext->current();
    $source = Source::create(['code' => 'account', 'name' => ['ru' => 'Account']]);
    $company = app(CreateCompany::class)->handle(new CompanyData(name: 'Company', sourceId: $source->id, ownerId: $ctx->principalId));
    $contact = app(CreateContact::class)->handle(new ContactData(nameFirst: 'Contact', sourceId: $source->id, ownerId: $ctx->principalId));
    app(AssociateContactCompany::class)->handle(contactId: $contact->id, companyId: $company->id);
    $company->delete();
    expect(DB::table('crm_contact_company')->count())->toBe(1)->and($contact->companies()->count())->toBe(0);
    DB::table('crm_sources')->where('id', $source->id)->delete();
    expect(DB::table('crm_contacts')->value('source_id'))->toBeNull();
    DB::table('users')->where('id', $ctx->principalId)->delete();
    expect(DB::table('crm_contacts')->value('owner_id'))->toBeNull()->and(DB::table('crm_companies')->value('owner_id'))->toBeNull();
    DB::table('crm_companies')->where('id', $company->id)->delete();
    expect(DB::table('crm_contact_company')->count())->toBe(0);
    DB::table('wsp_workspaces')->where('id', $ctx->tenant->workspace->id)->delete();
    expect(DB::table('crm_contacts')->count())->toBe(0);
});

it('enforces pair and primary constraints for direct database writers', function (): void {
    $company = app(CreateCompany::class)->handle(new CompanyData(name: 'One'));
    $other = app(CreateCompany::class)->handle(new CompanyData(name: 'Two'));
    $contact = app(CreateContact::class)->handle(new ContactData(nameFirst: 'Contact'));
    $action = app(AssociateContactCompany::class);
    $action->handle(contactId: $contact->id, companyId: $company->id, isPrimary: true);
    expect(fn () => $action->handle(contactId: $contact->id, companyId: $company->id))->toThrow(ValidationException::class);
    expect(fn () => $action->handle(contactId: $contact->id, companyId: $other->id, isPrimary: true))->toThrow(ValidationException::class);
    foreach ([$company->id, $other->id] as $id) {
        try {
            DB::table('crm_contact_company')->insert(['id' => (string) Str::uuid7(), 'contact_id' => $contact->id, 'company_id' => $id, 'is_primary' => true]);
            test()->fail('Unique constraint not enforced.');
        } catch (QueryException $exception) {
            expect($exception->errorInfo[0])->toBe('23505');
        }
    }
});

it('serializes competing primary actions and rejects the loser after commit', function (): void {
    $company = app(CreateCompany::class)->handle(new CompanyData(name: 'Winner'));
    $other = app(CreateCompany::class)->handle(new CompanyData(name: 'Loser'));
    $contact = app(CreateContact::class)->handle(new ContactData(nameFirst: 'Contact'));
    $ctx = $this->crmContext->current();
    config(['database.connections.primary_race' => config('database.connections.pgsql')]);
    DB::connection('primary_race')->beginTransaction();
    DB::connection('primary_race')->statement("SET LOCAL lock_timeout='50ms'");
    $fixture = $this->crmContext;
    $race = new ExecutionContext(tenant: $ctx->tenant, connectionName: 'primary_race', actor: $ctx->actor, principalId: $ctx->principalId);
    $observed = false;
    DB::listen(function ($query) use (&$observed, $fixture, $ctx, $race, $contact, $other): void {
        if ($observed || $query->connectionName !== 'pgsql' || ! str_contains($query->sql, '"crm_contacts"') || ! str_contains($query->sql, 'for update')) {
            return;
        }
        $observed = true;
        $fixture->context = $race;

        try {
            app(AssociateContactCompany::class)->handle(contactId: $contact->id, companyId: $other->id, isPrimary: true);
            test()->fail('Competing primary action did not block.');
        } catch (QueryException $exception) {
            expect($exception->errorInfo[0])->toBe('55P03');
        } finally {
            $fixture->context = $ctx;
        }
    });

    try {
        app(AssociateContactCompany::class)->handle(contactId: $contact->id, companyId: $company->id, isPrimary: true);
        $fixture->context = $race;
        expect(fn () => app(AssociateContactCompany::class)->handle(contactId: $contact->id, companyId: $other->id, isPrimary: true))->toThrow(ValidationException::class);
        expect($observed)->toBeTrue()->and(DB::table('crm_contact_company')->where('is_primary', true)->count())->toBe(1);
    } finally {
        $fixture->context = $ctx;
        DB::connection('primary_race')->rollBack();
        DB::purge('primary_race');
    }
});

declare(strict_types=1);

namespace Flex\Crm\Tests\Support;

use Illuminate\Database\Schema\Blueprint;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Schema;

final class AccountFixture
{
    public static function migrate(): void
    {
        Schema::create('users', function (Blueprint $table): void {
            $table->uuid('id')->primary();
            $table->string('name');
            $table->boolean('is_active')->default(true);
            $table->softDeletesTz();
        });
        Schema::create('wsp_workspaces', function (Blueprint $table): void {
            $table->uuid('id')->primary();
            $table->string('slug')->unique();
            $table->softDeletesTz();
        });
        Schema::create('wsp_workspace_members', function (Blueprint $table): void {
            $table->uuid('id')->primary();
            $table->foreignUuid('workspace_id')->constrained('wsp_workspaces')->cascadeOnDelete();
            $table->foreignUuid('user_id')->constrained('users')->cascadeOnDelete();
            $table->string('status')->default('active');
            $table->unique(['workspace_id', 'user_id']);
        });
    }
}


WHOLE PRODUCT FILE M products/flex-crm/tests/Support/TestContext.php
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

namespace Flex\Crm\Tests\Support;

use CoreX\Context\Contracts\AccountConnectionResolver;
use CoreX\Context\Contracts\TenantContextResolver;
use CoreX\Context\Data\AccountRef;
use CoreX\Context\Data\DatabaseConnectionConfig;
use CoreX\Context\Data\TenantContext;
use CoreX\Context\Data\WorkspaceRef;
use CoreX\Modules\Contracts\ModuleRegistry;
use Flex\Crm\Context\Contracts\CrmExecutionContext;
use Flex\Crm\Context\Data\Actor;
use Flex\Crm\Context\Data\ExecutionContext;
use Illuminate\Auth\Access\AuthorizationException;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\DB;
use Illuminate\Support\Str;
use Mockery;

final class TestContext implements AccountConnectionResolver, CrmExecutionContext
{
    public ?ExecutionContext $context = null;

    public function current(): ExecutionContext
    {
        return $this->context ?? throw new AuthorizationException('No authenticated CRM context.');
    }

    public function resolve(AccountRef $account): DatabaseConnectionConfig
    {
        $config = DB::connection($this->current()->connectionName)->getConfig();

        return new DatabaseConnectionConfig(driver: $config['driver'], host: $config['host'] ?? '', port: (int) ($config['port'] ?? 0), database: $config['database'], username: $config['username'] ?? '');
    }

    public static function seed(string $connectionName): self
    {
        $db = DB::connection($connectionName);
        $user = (string) Str::uuid7();
        $workspace = (string) Str::uuid7();
        $db->table('users')->insert(['id' => $user, 'name' => 'Trusted user']);
        $db->table('wsp_workspaces')->insert(['id' => $workspace, 'slug' => 'main']);
        $db->table('wsp_workspace_members')->insert(['id' => (string) Str::uuid7(), 'workspace_id' => $workspace, 'user_id' => $user, 'status' => 'active']);
        $self = new self;
        $self->context = new ExecutionContext(
            tenant: new TenantContext(new AccountRef(id: (string) Str::uuid7(), slug: 'account-a', status: 'active', features: [], limits: []), new WorkspaceRef(id: (string) $workspace, slug: 'main', type: 'main', path: '', parentId: null)),
            connectionName: $connectionName, actor: new Actor(source: 'user', id: (string) $user, name: 'Trusted user'), principalId: $user,
        );
        $self->bind();

        return $self;
    }

    public function bind(): void
    {
        app()->instance(CrmExecutionContext::class, $this);
        $self = $this;
        app()->instance(TenantContextResolver::class, new class($self) implements TenantContextResolver
        {
            public function __construct(private TestContext $fixture) {}

            public function current(): TenantContext
            {
                return $this->fixture->current()->tenant;
            }
        });
        app()->instance(AccountConnectionResolver::class, $this);
        $modules = Mockery::mock(ModuleRegistry::class);
        $modules->shouldReceive('isActive')->with('flex/crm', Mockery::type(TenantContext::class))->andReturn(true);
        app()->instance(ModuleRegistry::class, $modules);
    }
}


WHOLE PRODUCT FILE M scripts/anti-weakening-allowances.txt
# Anti-weakening-gate allowances (P2.15/D93).
#
# Формат (позиционный, ровно 4 первых поля + свободный текст причины):
#   <base-sha> <lane:sqlite|pg> <дельта, отрицательная> <D#> <причина>
#
# Скидка применяется гейтом ТОЛЬКО пока текущий BASE_REF (merge-base HEAD origin/main)
# равен sha этой строки (полное сравнение, не префикс) — как только origin/main уезжает,
# скидка авто-истекает. Постоянный floor (по образцу coverage-min-floor.txt) отвергнут
# владельцем: он позволил бы тихо накапливать и вычитать разницу тестов в будущем.
#
# Новая строка добавляется ТОЛЬКО когда дельта названа поимённо (D# + файл-источник +
# item, см. Code Guidance P2.15) — allowance без D# запрещена.

WHOLE PRODUCT FILE M scripts/mutation-check.sh
#!/usr/bin/env bash
# Mutation-check harness (P1.14, plan.md D45).
#
# Применяет патч-саботаж scripts/mutations/<item>-<case>.patch к рабочему
# дереву, гоняет ЗАРЕГИСТРИРОВАННУЮ для этого кейса тестовую команду и требует
# НЕнулевой exit-код (тест обязан покраснеть под саботажем — граница пинится).
# Патч всегда откатывается (успех или провал), рабочее дерево не остаётся мутированным.
#
# 3-way-фоллбэк (P2.19, D97): текстовые проходы по коду (докблоки и т.п.) сдвигают
# ТОЛЬКО контекст хунка, не саботаж — строгий `git apply --check` тогда отказывает,
# хотя граница цела. Строгий apply остаётся ПЕРВИЧНЫМ путём; при его отказе кейс
# пробует `git apply -3` (трёхсторонняя склейка от pre-image-blob патча) — успех
# печатает ГРОМКУЮ строку с именем кейса (гейт зелёный, но говорит «реанкорь»),
# отказ обеих схем — честное «граница сдвинулась», return 2. Откат зеркалит режим
# наложения и ДОКАЗЫВАЕТ (слепок хэшей таргет-путей до/после), что дерево вернулось
# в исходное состояние — расхождение печатает ГРОМКО и падает `exit 3`.
#
# Использование:
#   bash scripts/mutation-check.sh <item> <case>
#   bash scripts/mutation-check.sh --list
#   bash scripts/mutation-check.sh --all   (все зарегистрированные кейсы подряд, для CI-джоба, P3.7)
set -euo pipefail

cd "$(git rev-parse --show-toplevel)"

# Реестр кейсов: "<item>-<case>" -> тестовая команда, которая обязана
# ПОКРАСНЕТЬ под применённым патчем-саботажем.
declare -A TEST_CMD=(
    [P06-egress-skip-on-delivery]="vendor/bin/pest --exclude-group=pg packages/webhooks/tests/Feature/WebhookEgressPolicyTest.php --filter=revocation"
    [P10-pg-outside-group]="PGSQL_PORT=1 vendor/bin/pest --exclude-group=pg packages/webhooks/tests/Feature/WebhookFanoutTest.php"
    [API-default-deny]="vendor/bin/pest --exclude-group=pg packages/operations/tests/Feature/PlatformTest.php --filter=test_default_authentication_and_authorization_deny"
    [API-module-activation]="vendor/bin/pest --exclude-group=pg packages/operations/tests/Feature/PlatformTest.php --filter=test_context_and_module_state_are_read_on_every_dispatch"
    [API-physical-context]="vendor/bin/pest --exclude-group=pg packages/operations/tests/Feature/PlatformTest.php --filter=test_missing_boxed_and_disabled_contexts_fail_closed"
    [API-undeclared-input]="vendor/bin/pest --exclude-group=pg packages/operations/tests/Feature/PlatformTest.php --filter=test_malformed_or_forged_input_is_rejected"
    [P1.14-pg-lane-split]="PGSQL_PORT=1 vendor/bin/pest --exclude-group=pg tests/Feature/PubSubDatabasePollTest.php"
    [P1.14-foreign-prefix-fp]="vendor/bin/pest tests/PHPStan/ModuleBoundaryRulesTest.php --filter=test_foreign_prefix_sql_no_false_positive"
    [P1.15-drop-entities]="vendor/bin/pest --exclude-group=pg tests/Feature/ModuleRegistryTest.php --filter='persists the entity|projects the compiled entities'"
    [P1.15-rename-check]="vendor/bin/pest --exclude-group=pg tests/Feature/ModuleRegistryTest.php --filter='leaves the destination intact'"
    [P1.15-silent-empty]="vendor/bin/pest --exclude-group=pg tests/Feature/ModuleRegistryTest.php --filter='fail-loud'"
    [P1.16-tags-back]="vendor/bin/pest --exclude-group=pg tests/Feature/ModuleRegistryTest.php --filter='non-taggable file store'"
    [P1.16-no-epoch-bump]="vendor/bin/pest --group=pg tests/Feature/ModuleLifecycleTest.php --filter='shared-DB epoch'"
    [P1.16-statereader-bare-binding]="vendor/bin/pest --group=pg tests/Feature/ModuleLifecycleTest.php --filter='resolves ModuleStateReader from the registered ModulesOptions connection'"
    [P1.17-tableprefix-skip]="vendor/bin/pest --exclude-group=pg tests/Feature/RegistryCompilerTest.php --filter='no tablePrefix'"
    [P1.17-stage-order-swap]="vendor/bin/pest --exclude-group=pg tests/Feature/RegistryCompilerTest.php --filter='AC-18'"
    [P1.18-purge-order]="vendor/bin/pest --group=pg tests/Feature/ModuleLifecycleTest.php --filter='reverse creation order'"
    [P1.18-enable-swallow]="vendor/bin/pest --group=pg tests/Feature/ModuleLifecycleTest.php --filter='all-or-nothing'"
    [P1.19-drop-precision]="vendor/bin/pest --group=pg tests/Feature/SchemaSetsPgTest.php --filter='precision'"
    [P1.21-getattributes-create]="vendor/bin/pest --group=pg tests/Feature/AuditLogTest.php --filter='omits guarded'"
    [P1.21-killswitch]="vendor/bin/pest --group=pg tests/Feature/AuditLogTest.php --filter='observer isolation'"
    [P1.21-always-excluded-removed]="vendor/bin/pest --group=pg tests/Feature/AuditLogTest.php --filter='guarded = \[\] makes them fillable'"
    [P1.21-killswitch-writeseam]="vendor/bin/pest --group=pg tests/Feature/AuditLogTest.php --filter='write seam'"
    [P1.22-drop-unique-index]="vendor/bin/pest --group=pg tests/Feature/SettingsTest.php --filter='rejects a duplicate account-level row'"
    [P1.22-drop-workspace-branch]="vendor/bin/pest --group=pg tests/Feature/FeatureFlagsTest.php --filter='AC-22 \(a\)|AC-22 \(b\)'"
    [P1.23-stale-read]="vendor/bin/pest --group=pg tests/Feature/DepartmentTest.php --filter='re-read guard'"
    [P1.23-event-in-transaction]="vendor/bin/pest --group=pg tests/Feature/DepartmentTest.php --filter='after-commit'"
    [P1.23-move-workspace-filter]="vendor/bin/pest --group=pg tests/Feature/DepartmentTest.php --filter='never a same-path row'"
    [P1.24-bare-runninginconsole-guard]="vendor/bin/pest --exclude-group=pg tests/Feature/PubSubTest.php --filter='refuses a faked Octane runtime'"
    [P1.24-cursor-start-zero]="vendor/bin/pest --group=pg tests/Feature/PubSubDatabasePollTest.php --filter='does NOT replay pre-existing history'"
    [P1.25-domainevent-drop]="vendor/bin/pest --group=pg tests/Feature/ModuleLifecycleTest.php --filter='carries a UUIDv7'"
    [P1.25-null-provider-binding]="vendor/bin/pest --group=pg tests/Feature/SettingsTest.php --filter='resolves a manifest-declared DefaultSettingDefinition'"
    [P1.29-upgrade-swallow]="vendor/bin/pest --group=pg tests/Feature/ModuleLifecycleTest.php --filter='catch-up migration fails'"
    [P1.29-move-parent-workspace]="vendor/bin/pest --group=pg tests/Feature/DepartmentTest.php --filter='in ANOTHER workspace is rejected'"
    [P05-scoped-to-singleton]="vendor/bin/pest --exclude-group=pg tests/Feature/ResidentLifecycleTest.php"
)

if [ "${1:-}" = "--list" ]; then
    for key in "${!TEST_CMD[@]}"; do
        echo "$key"
    done | sort
    exit 0
fi

run_case() {
    local KEY="$1" PATCH CMD STATUS APPLY_MODE BEFORE_HASH AFTER_HASH
    PATCH="scripts/mutations/${KEY}.patch"

    if [ ! -f "$PATCH" ]; then
        echo "mutation-check: патч не найден: $PATCH" >&2
        return 2
    fi

    CMD="${TEST_CMD[$KEY]:-}"
    if [ -z "$CMD" ]; then
        echo "mutation-check: для кейса '$KEY' не зарегистрирована тестовая команда (см. TEST_CMD в этом скрипте)" >&2
        return 2
    fi

    local -a TARGETS=()
    while IFS= read -r line; do
        [ -n "$line" ] && TARGETS+=("$line")
    done < <(git apply --numstat "$PATCH" 2>/dev/null | awk '{print $3}')

    # Preserve exact dirty bytes and index entries, including failed 3-way attempts.
    local SNAPSHOT_DIR
    SNAPSHOT_DIR=$(mktemp -d)
    BEFORE_HASH=$(git hash-object -- "${TARGETS[@]}")
    git ls-files --stage -- "${TARGETS[@]}" > "$SNAPSHOT_DIR/index"
    for target in "${TARGETS[@]}"; do
        mkdir -p "$SNAPSHOT_DIR/$(dirname "$target")"
        cp -p -- "$target" "$SNAPSHOT_DIR/$target"
    done

    cleanup() {
        for target in "${TARGETS[@]}"; do
            cp -p -- "$SNAPSHOT_DIR/$target" "$target"
        done
        git update-index --index-info < "$SNAPSHOT_DIR/index"
        AFTER_HASH=$(git hash-object -- "${TARGETS[@]}")
        if [ "$AFTER_HASH" != "$BEFORE_HASH" ]; then
            echo "mutation-check[$KEY]: original bytes could not be restored" >&2
            exit 3
        fi
    }
    trap cleanup RETURN

    APPLY_MODE=strict
    if ! git apply --check --whitespace=nowarn "$PATCH" 2>/dev/null; then
        APPLY_MODE=3way
    fi
    if [ "$APPLY_MODE" = "3way" ]; then
        if ! git apply --whitespace=nowarn -3 "$PATCH" 2>/dev/null; then
            echo "mutation-check: patch $PATCH cannot apply; original bytes retained" >&2
            return 2
        fi
        echo "mutation-check[$KEY]: stale patch context; 3-way fallback applied, reanchor required" >&2
    else
        echo "mutation-check[$KEY]: применяю $PATCH"
        git apply --whitespace=nowarn "$PATCH"
    fi

    echo "mutation-check[$KEY]: гоняю: $CMD"
    set +e
    bash -c "$CMD" > "$SNAPSHOT_DIR/test.log" 2>&1
    STATUS=$?
    cat "$SNAPSHOT_DIR/test.log"
    set -e

    # rg is not required. A missing grep or an unreadable log is exit 2, same as
    # a carrier that matched nothing (Pest prints "No tests found" and exits 0).
    local guard_status
    set +e
    command grep -Eq 'No tests found|"tests"[[:space:]]*:[[:space:]]*0([,}])' "$SNAPSHOT_DIR/test.log"
    guard_status=$?
    set -e
    if [ "$guard_status" -eq 0 ]; then
        echo "mutation-check[$KEY]: FAIL — carrier executed no tests" >&2
        return 2
    fi
    if [ "$guard_status" -ne 1 ]; then
        echo "mutation-check[$KEY]: FAIL — test-log guard failed (grep exit=$guard_status)" >&2
        return 2
    fi

    if [ "$STATUS" -eq 0 ]; then
        echo "mutation-check[$KEY]: FAIL — тест остался зелёным под патчем-саботажем (граница НЕ пинится, exit=0)" >&2
        return 1
    fi

    echo "mutation-check[$KEY]: OK — тест покраснел под патчем-саботажем (exit=$STATUS), граница пинится"
}

if [ "${1:-}" = "--all" ]; then
    FAILED=()
    for key in $(for k in "${!TEST_CMD[@]}"; do echo "$k"; done | sort); do
        if ! run_case "$key"; then
            FAILED+=("$key")
        fi
    done
    if [ "${#FAILED[@]}" -gt 0 ]; then
        echo "mutation-check --all: ${#FAILED[@]} кейс(ов) НЕ покраснели/не применились: ${FAILED[*]}" >&2
        exit 1
    fi
    echo "mutation-check --all: все ${#TEST_CMD[@]} кейса(ов) покраснели под саботажем — OK"
    exit 0
fi

ITEM="${1:?usage: mutation-check.sh <item> <case>  (или --list, --all)}"
CASE="${2:?usage: mutation-check.sh <item> <case>  (или --list, --all)}"
run_case "${ITEM}-${CASE}"

WHOLE PRODUCT FILE M tests/Arch/CoreLitmusTest.php
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

use CoreX\Tests\Support\SourceScanner;

// P6.3 — Foundation litmus (A29, D34). The file list is an explicit constant
// of the live Foundation map, not "every file except another zone".
// Condition 3 is fail-closed outside LITMUS_ELOQUENT. A new allowlist
// path needs a decision record. Condition 1 below the grep bar is a warning.
// An unobservable reference is unresolved, not a failure. Condition 5 above
// 200 lines outside Contracts, Enums, or Data is unresolved (P2 §7.1).

/**
 * Foundation paths. Mirrors the live map in docs/architecture/core-zones.md
 * after P6.1. The design baseline of 109 files is not this list.

 * @var list<string>

const LITMUS_ELOQUENT = [
    'packages/core/src/Database/Laravel/BaseModel.php',
];

test('foundation membership is an explicit constant of the live zone map', function (): void {
    $expected = LITMUS_FOUNDATION;
    $live = litmusLiveFoundationFiles();
    sort($expected);
    sort($live);

    expect($expected)->toBe($live)
        ->and(LITMUS_FOUNDATION)->not->toBe([]);
});

test('LITMUS_ELOQUENT contains only the BaseModel identity path', function (): void {

        ->and(LITMUS_ELOQUENT[0])->toEndWith('Database/Laravel/BaseModel.php');
});

test('foundation litmus hard conditions are green on the clean tree', function (): void {
    expect(litmusRl3Offenders())->toBe([])
        ->and(litmusNameOffenders())->toBe([])
        ->and(litmusNotesOffenders())->toBe([])
        ->and(litmusZoneOffenders())->toBe([]);
});

test('condition 1 below the grep bar warns and does not fail the gate', function (): void {

        ->and(litmusTypeAdoption('packages/core/src/Modules/Contracts/ModuleRegistrar.php', 'ModuleRegistrar'))->toBe('warning')
