test('condition 5 above 200 lines outside Contracts Enums and Data is unresolved', function (): void {
    $rule = 'packages/core/src/Messaging/PHPStan/Rules/DurablePayloadRule.php';
    $absolute = litmusAbsolute($rule);
    $original = (string) file_get_contents($absolute);

    try {
        file_put_contents($absolute, $original.str_repeat("\n", 201));
        expect(litmusSizeStatus($rule))->toBe('unresolved');
    } finally {
        file_put_contents($absolute, $original);
    }

    expect(litmusSizeStatus($rule))->toBe('pass')
        ->and(litmusSizeStatus('packages/core/src/Database/Laravel/BaseModel.php'))->toBe('pass')
        ->and(litmusRl3Offenders())->toBe([])
        ->and(litmusNameOffenders())->toBe([]);
});

test('R-L3 planting in Foundation RecordRef names the file and Eloquent', function (): void {
    $relative = 'packages/core/src/Data/Data/RecordRef.php';
    $absolute = SourceScanner::repoRoot().'/'.$relative;
    $original = (string) file_get_contents($absolute);
    $needle = "declare(strict_types=1);\n";
    $count = 0;
    $planted = str_replace(
        $needle,
        $needle."\nuse Illuminate\\Database\\Eloquent\\Model;\n",
        $original,
        $count,
    );

    if ($count !== 1) {
        throw new RuntimeException('R-L3 planting could not insert a use into '.$relative);
    }

    file_put_contents($absolute, $planted);

    try {
        $hits = array_values(array_filter(
            litmusRl3Offenders(),
            static fn (string $line): bool => str_contains($line, 'RecordRef.php')
                && str_contains($line, 'Eloquent'),
        ));

        expect($hits)->not->toBe([])
            ->and($hits[0])->toContain('R-L3')
            ->and($hits[0])->toContain('RecordRef.php')
            ->and($hits[0])->toContain('Eloquent');
    } finally {
        file_put_contents($absolute, $original);
    }
});

/**
 * @return list<string>
 */
function litmusRl3Offenders(): array
{
    $offenders = [];

    foreach (LITMUS_FOUNDATION as $relative) {
        if (in_array($relative, LITMUS_ELOQUENT, true)) {
            continue;
        }

        foreach (litmusImports(litmusAbsolute($relative)) as $import) {
            if (! litmusImportForbidden($import)) {
                continue;
            }

            $offenders[] = 'R-L3 '.$relative.' imports '.$import;
        }
    }

    sort($offenders);

    return $offenders;
}

/**
 * @return list<string>
 */
function litmusNameOffenders(): array
{
    $offenders = [];

    foreach (LITMUS_FOUNDATION as $relative) {
        $text = (string) file_get_contents(litmusAbsolute($relative));
        $namespace = '';

        if (preg_match('/^namespace\s+([^;]+);/m', $text, $matches) === 1) {
            $namespace = $matches[1];
        }

        $types = litmusDeclaredTypes($text);

        if ($types === []) {
            $types = [basename($relative, '.php')];
        }

        foreach ($types as $type) {
            $segments = array_values(array_filter(explode('\\', $namespace.'\\'.$type)));

            foreach ($segments as $segment) {
                if (str_contains($segment, 'Audit') && $relative !== 'packages/core/src/Audit/Enums/ActorType.php') {
                    $offenders[] = $relative.' litmus-2 segment '.$segment;

                    continue;
                }

                foreach (LITMUS_FORBIDDEN_SEGMENTS as $term) {
                    if (str_contains($segment, $term)) {
                        $offenders[] = $relative.' litmus-2 segment '.$segment;

                        break;
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }

    $offenders = array_values(array_unique($offenders));
    sort($offenders);

    return $offenders;
}

/**
 * @return list<string>
 */
function litmusNotesOffenders(): array
{
    $offenders = [];

    foreach (LITMUS_FOUNDATION as $relative) {
        $text = (string) file_get_contents(litmusAbsolute($relative));

        if (str_contains($text, 'product-only')) {
            $offenders[] = $relative.' litmus-4 product-only';
        }
    }

    return $offenders;
}

/**
 * @return list<string>
 */
function litmusZoneOffenders(): array
{
    $neighbors = [];

    foreach (glob(SourceScanner::repoRoot().'/packages/*', GLOB_ONLYDIR) ?: [] as $dir) {
        $name = strtolower(basename($dir));

        if ($name !== 'core') {
            $neighbors[] = $name;
        }
    }

    if (! in_array('foundation', $neighbors, true)) {
        return [];
    }

    return array_map(
        static fn (string $path): string => $path.' litmus-6 zone Foundation matches a package',
        LITMUS_FOUNDATION,
    );
}

function litmusTypeAdoption(string $relative, string $short): string
{
    $sites = litmusDeclarationSites($short);

    if (count($sites) !== 1) {
        return 'unresolved';
    }

    $packages = [];
    $pattern = '/\b'.preg_quote($short, '/').'\b/';

    foreach (litmusSrcCorpus() as [$package, $body]) {
        if (preg_match($pattern, $body) === 1) {
            $packages[$package] = true;
        }
    }

    $bar = litmusAdoptionBar($relative);

    return count($packages) >= $bar ? 'pass' : 'warning';
}

function litmusSizeStatus(string $relative): string
{
    $lines = file(litmusAbsolute($relative));
    $count = is_array($lines) ? count($lines) : 0;
    $under = substr($relative, strlen('packages/core/src/'));
    $segments = explode('/', $under);

    if (in_array('Contracts', $segments, true)
        || in_array('Enums', $segments, true)
        || in_array('Data', $segments, true)
        || $count <= 200) {
        return 'pass';
    }

    return 'unresolved';
}

function litmusAdoptionBar(string $relative): int
{
    $under = substr($relative, strlen('packages/core/src/'));

    foreach (['Contracts/', 'Enums/', 'Exceptions/'] as $prefix) {
        if (str_starts_with($under, $prefix)) {
            return 2;
        }
    }

    return 3;
}

function litmusAbsolute(string $relative): string
{
    return SourceScanner::repoRoot().'/'.$relative;
}

/**
 * Files the live map places in Foundation. R-L3 iterates LITMUS_FOUNDATION;
 * this scan only fails the suite when that constant drifts.
 *
 * @return list<string>
 */
function litmusLiveFoundationFiles(): array
{
    $prefixes = ['Messaging/PHPStan/', 'Data/', 'Time/'];
    $named = [
        'Data/Contracts/DataScopeAuthorizer.php',
        'Data/Contracts/DataScopeCodec.php',
        'Messaging/Contracts/DurablePayload.php',
        'Modules/Contracts/ModuleRegistrar.php',
        'Data/DataScopeAccessDecision.php',
        'Audit/Enums/ActorType.php',
        'Messaging/Contracts/Cancellable.php',
        'Messaging/Events/DomainEvent.php',
        'Data/Exceptions/InvalidScopePayload.php',
        'Database/Laravel/BaseModel.php',
    ];
    $root = SourceScanner::packagePath('core', 'src');
    $prefix = SourceScanner::repoRoot().'/packages/core/src/';
    $files = [];
    $iterator = new RecursiveIteratorIterator(
        new RecursiveDirectoryIterator($root, FilesystemIterator::SKIP_DOTS),
    );

    /** @var SplFileInfo $file */
    foreach ($iterator as $file) {
        if ($file->getExtension() !== 'php') {
            continue;
        }

        $path = $file->getPathname();
        $under = str_starts_with($path, $prefix) ? substr($path, strlen($prefix)) : $path;
        $matched = in_array($under, $named, true);

        if (! $matched) {
            foreach ($prefixes as $pathPrefix) {
                if (str_starts_with($under, $pathPrefix)) {
                    $matched = true;

                    break;
                }
            }
        }

        if ($matched) {
            $files[] = 'packages/core/src/'.$under;
        }
    }

    sort($files);

    return $files;
}

function litmusImportForbidden(string $import): bool
{
    return $import === 'Illuminate\\Database\\Eloquent'
        || str_starts_with($import, 'Illuminate\\Database\\Eloquent\\')
        || $import === 'Illuminate\\Http'
        || str_starts_with($import, 'Illuminate\\Http\\')
        || $import === 'Illuminate\\Foundation\\Application'
        || str_starts_with($import, 'Illuminate\\Foundation\\Application\\');
}

/**
 * @return list<string>
 */
function litmusImports(string $absolute): array
{
    $contents = (string) file_get_contents($absolute);
    preg_match_all('/^\s*use\s+(?:function\s+|const\s+)?([^;]+);/m', $contents, $matches);
    $imports = [];

    foreach ($matches[1] as $clause) {
        $clause = trim($clause);

        if (preg_match('/^(.+?)\\\\?\{(.+)\}$/', $clause, $parts) === 1) {
            $prefix = rtrim($parts[1], '\\');

            foreach (explode(',', $parts[2]) as $name) {
                $name = trim(explode(' as ', trim($name), 2)[0]);

                if ($name !== '') {
                    $imports[] = $prefix.'\\'.$name;
                }
            }

            continue;
        }

        $imports[] = trim(explode(' as ', $clause, 2)[0]);
    }

    return $imports;
}

/**
 * @return list<string>
 */
function litmusDeclaredTypes(string $text): array
{
    preg_match_all(
        '/^(?:(?:abstract|final|readonly)\s+)*(?:class|interface|trait|enum)\s+(\w+)/m',
        $text,
        $matches,
    );

    return $matches[1];
}

/**
 * @return list<string>
 */
function litmusDeclarationSites(string $short): array
{
    $sites = litmusDeclarationIndex();

    return $sites[$short] ?? [];
}

/**
 * @return array<string, list<string>>
 */
function litmusDeclarationIndex(): array
{
    static $index = null;

    if ($index !== null) {
        return $index;
    }

    $index = [];

    foreach (litmusSrcCorpus() as [$package, $body]) {
        foreach (litmusDeclaredTypes($body) as $type) {
            $index[$type][] = $package;
        }
    }

    return $index;
}

/**
 * @return list<array{0: string, 1: string}>
 */
function litmusSrcCorpus(): array
{
    static $corpus = null;

    if ($corpus !== null) {
        return $corpus;
    }

    $corpus = [];
    $root = SourceScanner::repoRoot().'/packages';

    foreach (glob($root.'/*', GLOB_ONLYDIR) ?: [] as $packageDir) {
        $src = $packageDir.'/src';

        if (! is_dir($src)) {
            continue;
        }

        $package = basename($packageDir);
        $iterator = new RecursiveIteratorIterator(
            new RecursiveDirectoryIterator($src, FilesystemIterator::SKIP_DOTS),
        );

        /** @var SplFileInfo $file */
        foreach ($iterator as $file) {
            if ($file->getExtension() !== 'php') {
                continue;
            }

            $corpus[] = [$package, (string) file_get_contents($file->getPathname())];
        }
    }

    return $corpus;
}

test('every core src file is classified into a zone or composition/testing', function (): void {
    expect(rz0Offenders())->toBe([])
        ->and(zoneOf('packages/core/src/Messaging/Internal/RedisPubSub.php'))->toBe('Messaging')
        ->and(CORE_ZONE_ALLOWED['Foundation'])->toBe([])
        ->and(zoneOf('packages/core/src/Config/Laravel/ConfigurationValidator.php'))->toBe('Foundation')
        ->and(zoneOf('packages/core/src/Config/Exceptions/InvalidConfiguration.php'))->toBe('Foundation')
        ->and(zoneOf('packages/core/src/Config/Data/SecretRef.php'))->toBe('Foundation')
        ->and(zoneOf('packages/core/src/Errors/Contracts/DescribesError.php'))->toBe('Foundation')
        ->and(zoneOf('packages/core/src/Errors/Data/ErrorDescriptor.php'))->toBe('Foundation')
        ->and(zoneOf('packages/core/src/Errors/Enums/ErrorCategory.php'))->toBe('Foundation')
        ->and(zoneOf('packages/core/src/Errors/Enums/UnavailabilityReason.php'))->toBe('Foundation')
        ->and(coreZoneKind('packages/core/src/Config/Data/CoreOptions.php'))->toBe('composition')
        ->and(coreZoneKind('packages/core/src/Modules/Contracts/PackageRegistrar.php'))->toBe('composition')
        ->and(coreZoneKind('packages/core/src/Modules/Laravel/RegistersNothingOnBoot.php'))->toBe('composition');
});

test('zone files import only allowed CoreX zones (R-Z1)', function (): void {
    expect(rz1Offenders())->toBe([]);
});

test('foreign files do not import zone Internal (R-Z2)', function (): void {
    expect(rz2Offenders())->toBe([]);
});

test('R-Z1 planting in Foundation RecordRef names the file and symbol', function (): void {
    $line = coreZonePlant(
        'packages/core/src/Data/Data/RecordRef.php',
        'CoreX\\Panels\\SavedViews\\Internal\\SqlSavedViewRepository',
        rz1Offenders(...),
    );

    expect($line)->toContain('R-Z1')
        ->and($line)->toContain('RecordRef.php')
        ->and($line)->toContain('SqlSavedViewRepository');
});

test('R-Z1 planting in TenancyContracts AccountRef names the file and symbol', function (): void {
    $line = coreZonePlant(
        'packages/core/src/Context/Data/AccountRef.php',
        'CoreX\\Panels\\SavedViews\\Internal\\SqlSavedViewRepository',
        rz1Offenders(...),
    );

    expect($line)->toContain('R-Z1')
        ->and($line)->toContain('AccountRef.php')
        ->and($line)->toContain('SqlSavedViewRepository');
});

test('R-Z2 planting in ActorType names the file and symbol', function (): void {
    $line = coreZonePlant(
        'packages/core/src/Audit/Enums/ActorType.php',
        'CoreX\\Panels\\SavedViews\\Internal\\SqlSavedViewRepository',
        rz2Offenders(...),
    );

    expect($line)->toContain('R-Z2')
        ->and($line)->toContain('ActorType.php')
        ->and($line)->toContain('SqlSavedViewRepository');
});

test('R-Z0 planting of an unclassified error file names the path', function (): void {
    $relative = 'packages/core/src/Errors/Data/UnclassifiedError.php';
    $absolute = SourceScanner::repoRoot().'/'.$relative;
    $contents = <<<'PHP'
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

namespace CoreX\Errors\Data;

final class UnclassifiedError
{
}
PHP;

    file_put_contents($absolute, $contents);

    try {
        $hits = array_values(array_filter(
            rz0Offenders(),
            static fn (string $line): bool => str_contains($line, 'Errors/Data/UnclassifiedError.php'),
        ));

        expect($hits)->not->toBe([])
            ->and($hits[0])->toContain('R-Z0')
            ->and($hits[0])->toContain('Errors/Data/UnclassifiedError.php');
    } finally {
        if (is_file($absolute)) {
            unlink($absolute);
        }
    }
});

test('R-Z0 planting of an unclassified Support file names the file', function (): void {
    $relative = 'packages/core/src/Config/Probe.php';
    $absolute = SourceScanner::repoRoot().'/'.$relative;
    $contents = <<<'PHP'
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

namespace CoreX\Config;

final class Probe
{
}
PHP;

    file_put_contents($absolute, $contents);

    try {
        $hits = array_values(array_filter(
            rz0Offenders(),
            static fn (string $line): bool => str_contains($line, 'Config/Probe.php'),
        ));

        expect($hits)->not->toBe([])
            ->and($hits[0])->toContain('R-Z0')
            ->and($hits[0])->toContain('Probe.php');
    } finally {
        if (is_file($absolute)) {
            unlink($absolute);
        }
    }
});

/**
 * @return list<string>
 */
function corePhpFiles(): array
{
    $root = SourceScanner::packagePath('core', 'src');
    $prefix = SourceScanner::repoRoot().'/';
    $files = [];

    $iterator = new RecursiveIteratorIterator(
        new RecursiveDirectoryIterator($root, FilesystemIterator::SKIP_DOTS),
    );

    /** @var SplFileInfo $file */
    foreach ($iterator as $file) {
        if ($file->getExtension() !== 'php') {
            continue;
        }

        $path = $file->getPathname();
        $files[] = str_starts_with($path, $prefix) ? substr($path, strlen($prefix)) : $path;
    }

    sort($files);

    return $files;
}

function zoneOf(string $relative): ?string
{
    $kind = coreZoneKind($relative);

    if ($kind === 'testing' || $kind === 'composition' || $kind === 'unclassified') {
        return null;
    }

    return $kind;
}

function coreZoneKind(string $relative): string
{
    $prefix = 'packages/core/src/';

    if (! str_starts_with($relative, $prefix)) {
        return 'unclassified';
    }

    $under = substr($relative, strlen($prefix));

    if (str_starts_with($under, 'Testing/')) {
        return 'testing';
    }

    if (in_array($relative, CORE_ZONE_COMPOSITION, true)) {
        return 'composition';
    }

    if (in_array($under, CORE_ZONE_FOUNDATION_SUPPORT, true)) {
        return 'Foundation';
    }

    if (isset(CORE_ZONE_DUMP[$under])) {
        return CORE_ZONE_DUMP[$under];
    }

    foreach (CORE_ZONE_PREFIX as $pathPrefix => $zone) {
        if (str_starts_with($under, $pathPrefix)) {
            return $zone;
        }
    }

    return CORE_ZONE_DUMP[$under] ?? 'unclassified';
}

function coreZoneIsExempt(string $relative): bool
{
    $kind = coreZoneKind($relative);

    return $kind === 'testing' || $kind === 'composition';
}

/**
 * @return list<string>
 */
function rz0Offenders(): array
{
    $offenders = [];

    foreach (corePhpFiles() as $relative) {
        if (coreZoneIsExempt($relative)) {
            continue;
        }

        if (zoneOf($relative) === null) {
            $offenders[] = 'R-Z0 '.$relative.' unclassified';
        }
    }

    return $offenders;
}

/**
 * @return list<string>
 */
function rz1Offenders(): array
{
    $offenders = [];

    foreach (corePhpFiles() as $relative) {
        $sourceZone = zoneOf($relative);

        if ($sourceZone === null) {
            continue;
        }

        $absolute = SourceScanner::repoRoot().'/'.$relative;

        foreach (coreZoneCoreXImports($absolute) as $import) {
            $target = coreZoneImportTarget($import);

            if ($target === null) {
                continue;
            }

            $targetKind = coreZoneKind($target);

            if ($targetKind === 'composition' || $targetKind === 'testing') {
                continue;
            }

            if (coreZoneExactImport($relative, $import)) {
                continue;
            }

            if ($targetKind === 'unclassified' || ! coreZoneImportAllowed($relative, $sourceZone, $targetKind)) {
                $offenders[] = 'R-Z1 '.$relative.' imports '.$import;
            }
        }
    }

    sort($offenders);

    return $offenders;
}

function coreZoneExactImport(string $relative, string $import): bool
{
    return in_array($import, CORE_ZONE_EXACT_IMPORTS[$relative] ?? [], true);
}

function coreZoneImportAllowed(string $relative, string $sourceZone, string $targetKind): bool
{
    if ($targetKind === $sourceZone) {
        return true;
    }

    if ($relative === CORE_ZONE_E_BASEMODEL && $targetKind === 'Audit') {
        return true;
    }

    return in_array($targetKind, CORE_ZONE_ALLOWED[$sourceZone], true);
}

/**
 * @return list<string>
 */
function rz2Offenders(): array
{
    $offenders = [];

    foreach (corePhpFiles() as $relative) {
        if (coreZoneKind($relative) === 'composition') {
            continue;
        }

        $absolute = SourceScanner::repoRoot().'/'.$relative;

        foreach (coreZoneCoreXImports($absolute) as $import) {
            if (preg_match('/^CoreX\\\\.+\\\\Internal\\\\/', $import) !== 1) {
                continue;
            }

            $target = coreZoneImportTarget($import);
            $zone = $target === null ? 'unclassified' : coreZoneKind($target);

            if (! coreZoneExactImport($relative, $import) && ($zone === 'unclassified' || ! coreZoneFileInZoneDirectories($relative, $zone))) {
                $offenders[] = 'R-Z2 '.$relative.' imports '.$import;
            }
        }
    }

    sort($offenders);

    return $offenders;
}

function coreZoneFileInZoneDirectories(string $relative, string $zone): bool
{
    return coreZoneKind($relative) === $zone;
}

/**
 * @return list<string>
 */
function coreZoneCoreXImports(string $absolute): array
{
    $contents = (string) file_get_contents($absolute);
    preg_match_all('/^\s*use\s+(?:function\s+|const\s+)?(CoreX\\\\[^;]+);/m', $contents, $matches);

    $imports = [];

    foreach ($matches[1] as $import) {
        $imports[] = trim(explode(' as ', $import, 2)[0]);
    }

    return $imports;
}

function coreZoneImportTarget(string $import): ?string
{
    if (! str_starts_with($import, 'CoreX\\')) {
        return null;
    }

    $rest = substr($import, strlen('CoreX\\'));

    return 'packages/core/src/'.str_replace('\\', '/', $rest).'.php';
}

/**
 * Insert one CoreX import, collect the matching diagnostic, then restore the file.
 *
 * @param  callable(): list<string>  $collect
 */
function coreZonePlant(string $relative, string $import, callable $collect): string
{
    $absolute = SourceScanner::repoRoot().'/'.$relative;
    $original = (string) file_get_contents($absolute);
    $needle = "declare(strict_types=1);\n";
    $count = 0;
    $planted = str_replace($needle, $needle."\nuse {$import};\n", $original, $count);

    if ($count !== 1) {
        throw new RuntimeException('R-Z planting could not insert a use into '.$relative);
    }

    file_put_contents($absolute, $planted);

    try {
        $symbol = substr($import, strrpos($import, '\\') + 1);
        $hits = array_values(array_filter(
            $collect(),
            static fn (string $line): bool => str_contains($line, basename($relative))
                && str_contains($line, $symbol),
        ));

        expect($hits)->not->toBe([]);

        return $hits[0];
    } finally {
        file_put_contents($absolute, $original);
    }
}

/** Tenant resolver whose current workspace can be mutated between dispatch and handle. */
final class MutableTenantResolver implements TenantContextResolver
{
    public ?string $workspaceId = null;

    public function current(): TenantContext
    {
        $account = new AccountRef('00000000-0000-7000-8000-0000000000aa', 'acct', 'active', [], []);
        $workspace = $this->workspaceId !== null
            ? new WorkspaceRef($this->workspaceId, 'wsp', 'team', '/wsp', null)
            : null;

        return new TenantContext($account, $workspace);
    }
}

/** AuditRetention with a fixed cutoff, for the prune test. */
final class FixedAuditRetention implements AuditRetention
{
    public function __construct(private readonly ?DateTimeImmutable $cutoff) {}

    public function olderThan(): ?DateTimeImmutable
    {
        return $this->cutoff;
    }
}

it('omits guarded/non-fillable attributes from the diff on create (A17)', function (): void {
    Schema::create('audit_sensitive_stubs', function ($table): void {
        $table->uuid('id')->primary();
        $table->string('name');
        $table->string('secret')->nullable();
        $table->string('password')->nullable();
        $table->string('remember_token')->nullable();
        $table->timestamps();
    });

    // forceFill so the guarded attributes are actually SET on the model —
    // create() would silently drop them (that is the point: they must never
    // reach the audit diff even when present as raw attributes).
    $model = (new AuditSensitiveStub)->forceFill([
        'name' => 'bob',
        'secret' => 's3cret',
        'password' => 'hashed-password',
        'remember_token' => 'a-token',
    ]);
    $model->save();

    $entry = AuditLog::on(AUDIT_TEST_CONNECTION)->where('action', 'created')->sole();

    expect($entry->changes)->toHaveKey('name')
        ->and($entry->changes['name']['new'])->toBe('bob')
        // Guarded fields are ABSENT (not masked) — B-10 §7.4 п.8.
        ->and($entry->changes)->not->toHaveKey('password')
        ->and($entry->changes)->not->toHaveKey('remember_token')
        ->and($entry->changes)->not->toHaveKey('id')
        // Fillable-but-sensitive field is masked, not omitted.
        ->and($entry->changes['secret']['new'])->toBe('••••••');
});

it('excludes password/remember_token even when $guarded = [] makes them fillable (D63 Blocker)', function (): void {
    Schema::create('audit_unguarded_stubs', function ($table): void {
        $table->uuid('id')->primary();
        $table->string('name');
        $table->string('password')->nullable();
        $table->string('remember_token')->nullable();
        $table->timestamps();
    });

    // $guarded = [] means isFillable() returns true for EVERY attribute — the
    // exclusion must not depend on that check alone (ALWAYS_EXCLUDED).
    $model = AuditUnguardedStub::create([

    $created = AuditLog::on(AUDIT_TEST_CONNECTION)->where('action', 'created')->sole();

    expect($created->changes)->toHaveKey('name')
        ->and($created->changes)->not->toHaveKey('password')
        ->and($created->changes)->not->toHaveKey('remember_token');

    $model->update(['password' => 'new-hashed-password']);

    $updated = AuditLog::on(AUDIT_TEST_CONNECTION)->where('action', 'updated')->sole();

    expect($updated->changes)->not->toHaveKey('password');
});

it('killswitch silences a direct AuditLogger::record() at the write seam, not only the observer (D63/D66 defense-in-depth)', function (): void {
    config(['corex.audit.enabled' => false]);
    app()->forgetInstance(CoreOptions::class);

    // Bypass the observer entirely — call the AuditLogger contract directly, as
    // any caller resolving it could. app(AuditObserver::class)->record()'s guard (A60) does
    // not cover this path; the write seam must refuse on its own. Sync queue in
    // this suite means an un-guarded record() would write the row synchronously.
    auditLogger()->record(new AuditEntry(
        action: 'created',
        actorType: ActorType::System,
        entityType: 'stub.thing',
        entityId: '00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000009',
    ));

    expect(AuditLog::on(AUDIT_TEST_CONNECTION)->count())->toBe(0);
});

it('killswitch aborts in the observer itself — an unconditional logger stays untouched (P1.21 observer isolation)', function (): void {
    config(['corex.audit.enabled' => false]);
    app()->forgetInstance(CoreOptions::class);

    Schema::create('stub_auditable_things', function ($table): void {
        $table->uuid('id')->primary();
        $table->string('name');
        $table->string('secret')->nullable();
        $table->timestamps();
    });

    // Unconditional spy: records EVERY call, with NO killswitch of its own. This
    // isolates AuditObserver's guard from QueuedAuditLogger's write-seam guard
    // (P1.21-killswitch-writeseam pins the latter separately). The existing
    // 'killswitch silences' tests assert AuditLog stays empty — but that holds
    // even if the observer forgets to guard, because the real logger's write-seam
    // catches it. Only a logger with no guard of its own can prove the observer
    // short-circuits on its own: if it doesn't, this spy captures the call.
    $spy = new class implements AuditLogger
    {
        public int $calls = 0;

        public function record(AuditEntry $entry): void
        {
            $this->calls++;
        }
    };
    $this->app->instance(AuditLogger::class, $spy);

    $registry = new ArrayEntityRegistry;
    $registry->register(auditEntityFor(StubAuditableThing::class, 'stub_auditable_things', 'stub.auditable_thing'));

    AuditCapabilityWiring::wire($registry);

    StubAuditableThing::create(['name' => 'alpha', 'secret' => 'top-secret']);

    // The observer must short-circuit at its OWN killswitch (AuditObserver::record),
    // never reaching app(AuditLogger::class). Mutating that guard (&& false) lets
    // it through → the spy captures → this reddens.
    expect($spy->calls)->toBe(0);
});

it('snapshots occurred_at and workspace_id at enqueue, not in the worker (A17)', function (): void {
    Queue::fake();

    $resolver = new MutableTenantResolver;
    $resolver->workspaceId = $eventWorkspace = (string) Str::uuid7();

    $logger = app()->make(QueuedAuditLogger::class, ['connection' => AUDIT_TEST_CONNECTION, 'tenantContext' => $resolver]);

    Carbon::setTestNow('2026-07-14 10:00:00');

    $logger->record(new AuditEntry(
        action: 'created',
        actorType: ActorType::System,
        entityType: 'stub.thing',
        entityId: (string) Str::uuid7(),
    ));

    $captured = null;
    Queue::assertPushed(RecordAuditEntry::class, function (RecordAuditEntry $job) use (&$captured): bool {
        $captured = $job;

        return true;
    });

    // Tenant context and clock move AFTER dispatch but BEFORE the worker runs —
    // a worker that resolved tenant/now itself would write the wrong values.
    $resolver->workspaceId = (string) Str::uuid7();
    Carbon::setTestNow('2026-07-14 12:00:00');

    app()->call([$captured, 'handle']);

    $row = AuditLog::on(AUDIT_TEST_CONNECTION)->sole();

    expect($row->workspace_id)->toBe($eventWorkspace)
        ->and($row->occurred_at->format('Y-m-d H:i:s'))->toBe('2026-07-14 10:00:00');
});

it('records restored and forceDeleted, folding the redundant delete of a forceDelete (A17)', function (): void {
    $widget = Widget::create(['name' => 'x']);
    $widget->delete();
    $widget->restore();
    $widget->forceDelete();

    $actions = AuditLog::on(AUDIT_TEST_CONNECTION)
        ->where('entity_id', $widget->id)
        ->orderBy('seq')
        ->pluck('action')
        ->all();

    // restored and forceDeleted are both recorded. forceDelete() fires 'deleted'
    // AND 'forceDeleted' — the redundant 'deleted' is folded, so the single
    // 'deleted' seen here is the soft delete only (without the fold it would be 2).
    expect($actions)->toContain('restored')
        ->and($actions)->toContain('forceDeleted')
        ->and(collect($actions)->filter(static fn (string $a): bool => $a === 'deleted')->count())->toBe(1)
        ->and(collect($actions)->filter(static fn (string $a): bool => $a === 'forceDeleted')->count())->toBe(1);
});

it('paginates the audit journal by the monotonic seq cursor in insertion order (AC-23/D42)', function (): void {
    // entity_ids are deliberately NOT in sorted order: the keyset must return
    // rows in INSERTION order (what the clock-free bigserial seq encodes), not
    // sorted-by-id order. The old id-cursor test held for any id type (A65) —
    // this one fails if ordering falls back to any non-insertion column.
    $entityIds = [
        '00000000-0000-7000-8000-0000000000c1', // inserted 1st
        '00000000-0000-7000-8000-0000000000a2', // inserted 2nd
        '00000000-0000-7000-8000-0000000000b3', // inserted 3rd
    ];

    foreach ($entityIds as $entityId) {
        auditLogger()->record(new AuditEntry(
            action: 'created',
            actorType: ActorType::System,
            entityType: 'stub.thing',
            entityId: $entityId,
        ));
    }

    $ordered = AuditLog::on(AUDIT_TEST_CONNECTION)->orderBy('seq')->get(['seq', 'entity_id']);
    $seqs = $ordered->pluck('seq')->map(static fn (mixed $s): int => (int) $s)->all();

    expect($ordered->pluck('entity_id')->all())->toBe($entityIds)
        ->and($seqs[0])->toBeLessThan($seqs[1])
        ->and($seqs[1])->toBeLessThan($seqs[2]);

    $after = AuditLog::on(AUDIT_TEST_CONNECTION)
        ->where('seq', '>', $seqs[0])
        ->orderBy('seq')
        ->pluck('entity_id')
        ->all();

    expect($after)->toBe([$entityIds[1], $entityIds[2]]);
});

it('corex:audit:prune deletes rows older than the retention boundary and keeps fresh ones (A64)', function (): void {
    Carbon::setTestNow('2026-06-01 00:00:00');
    auditLogger()->record(new AuditEntry(
        action: 'created',
        actorType: ActorType::System,
        entityType: 'stub.thing',
        entityId: (string) Str::uuid7(),
    ));

    Carbon::setTestNow('2026-07-14 00:00:00');
    auditLogger()->record(new AuditEntry(
        action: 'created',
        actorType: ActorType::System,
        entityType: 'stub.thing',
        entityId: (string) Str::uuid7(),
    ));

    Carbon::setTestNow();

    expect(AuditLog::on(AUDIT_TEST_CONNECTION)->count())->toBe(2);

    $this->app->instance(AuditRetention::class, new FixedAuditRetention(new DateTimeImmutable('2026-07-01 00:00:00')));

    $this->artisan('corex:audit:prune', ['--database' => AUDIT_TEST_CONNECTION])->assertSuccessful();

    expect(AuditLog::on(AUDIT_TEST_CONNECTION)->count())->toBe(1)
        ->and(AuditLog::on(AUDIT_TEST_CONNECTION)->sole()->occurred_at->format('Y-m-d'))->toBe('2026-07-14');
});

    auditLogger()->record(new AuditEntry(
        action: 'created',
        actorType: ActorType::System,
        entityType: 'stub.thing',
        entityId: (string) Str::uuid7(),
    ));

    expect(app(AuditRetention::class))->toBeInstanceOf(NullAuditRetention::class);

    $this->artisan('corex:audit:prune', ['--database' => AUDIT_TEST_CONNECTION])->assertSuccessful();

    expect(AuditLog::on(AUDIT_TEST_CONNECTION)->count())->toBe(1);
});

WHOLE PRODUCT FILE M tests/Feature/ConfigCacheTest.php
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

use CoreX\Config\Data\CoreOptions;
use CoreX\CoreServiceProvider;
use Illuminate\Contracts\Debug\ExceptionHandler;
use Illuminate\Filesystem\Filesystem;
use Illuminate\Foundation\Application;
use Illuminate\Foundation\Bootstrap\BootProviders;
use Illuminate\Foundation\Bootstrap\LoadConfiguration;
use Illuminate\Foundation\Bootstrap\RegisterFacades;
use Illuminate\Foundation\Bootstrap\RegisterProviders;
use Illuminate\Foundation\Exceptions\Handler;
use Illuminate\Support\DefaultProviders;

/**
 * `php artisan config:cache` serialises the merged configuration with var_export into a PHP
 * file, so every value must survive that file being included: no closures, no objects other
 * than enum cases (CFG-004). Package configs are loaded the way config:cache loads them (a plain require).
 *
 * @return array<string, array<array-key, mixed>> config file name => array
 */
function configCacheDiscoverPackageConfigs(): array
{
    $configs = [];
    $files = glob(dirname(__DIR__, 2).'/packages/*/config/*.php') ?: [];
    sort($files);

    foreach ($files as $file) {
        $configs[basename($file, '.php')] = require $file;
    }

    return $configs;
}

/**
 * @param  array<array-key, mixed>  $config
 * @return array<array-key, mixed>
 */
function configCacheRoundTrip(array $config, string $label): array
{
    configCacheAssertSerialisable($config, $label);

    // config:cache writes exactly this shape: `<?php return var_export(...);` and the
    // application includes that file.
    $path = sys_get_temp_dir().'/corex-config-cache-'.bin2hex(random_bytes(8)).'.php';
    file_put_contents($path, '<?php return '.var_export($config, true).';');

    try {
        /** @var array<array-key, mixed> $loaded */
        $loaded = include $path;
    } finally {
        unlink($path);
    }

    return $loaded;
}

/** @param  array<array-key, mixed>  $config */
function configCacheAssertSerialisable(array $config, string $path): void
{
    foreach ($config as $key => $value) {
        $here = $path.'.'.$key;

        if ($value instanceof Closure) {
            throw new LogicException($here.' is a Closure: config:cache cannot serialise it');
        }

        if (is_object($value) && ! $value instanceof UnitEnum) {
            throw new LogicException($here.' is an instance of '.$value::class.': config:cache cannot serialise it');
        }

        if (is_resource($value)) {
            throw new LogicException($here.' is a resource: config:cache cannot serialise it');
        }

        if (is_array($value)) {
            configCacheAssertSerialisable($value, $here);
        }
    }
}

it('round-trips every package config file through var_export without loss', function (): void {
    $configs = configCacheDiscoverPackageConfigs();

    expect($configs)->not->toBeEmpty();

    foreach ($configs as $name => $config) {
        expect(configCacheRoundTrip($config, $name))->toBe($config, $name.' changed after var_export/eval');
    }
});

it('rejects a config that holds a closure or an object', function (): void {
    expect(fn () => configCacheRoundTrip(['probe' => ['handler' => static fn (): int => 1]], 'probe'))
        ->toThrow(LogicException::class, 'probe.probe.handler is a Closure')
        ->and(fn () => configCacheRoundTrip(['probe' => new stdClass], 'probe'))
        ->toThrow(LogicException::class, 'stdClass');
});

it('boots from a cached configuration repository instead of the config files', function (): void {
    $cached = $this->app['config']->all();

    foreach (configCacheDiscoverPackageConfigs() as $name => $config) {
        $cached[$name] = array_replace_recursive($config, $cached[$name] ?? []);
    }

    $cached['corex']['settings']['cache_prefix'] = 'corex_settings_from_cache';
    $cached['app']['providers'] = [...(new DefaultProviders)->toArray(), CoreServiceProvider::class];

    $base = sys_get_temp_dir().'/corex-config-cache-'.bin2hex(random_bytes(6));
    mkdir($base.'/bootstrap/cache', 0777, true);
    mkdir($base.'/storage/framework', 0777, true);
    file_put_contents(
        $base.'/bootstrap/cache/config.php',
        '<?php return '.var_export(configCacheRoundTrip($cached, 'cache'), true).';'.PHP_EOL,
    );

    try {
        $app = new Application($base);
        $app->singleton(ExceptionHandler::class, Handler::class);
        (new LoadConfiguration)->bootstrap($app);
        (new RegisterFacades)->bootstrap($app);
        (new RegisterProviders)->bootstrap($app);
        (new BootProviders)->bootstrap($app);

        expect($app->configurationIsCached())->toBeTrue()
            ->and($app->make(CoreOptions::class))->toBeInstanceOf(CoreOptions::class)
            ->and($app->make(CoreOptions::class)->settingsCachePrefix)->toBe($cached['corex']['settings']['cache_prefix']);
    } finally {
        (new Filesystem)->deleteDirectory($base);
    }
});


WHOLE PRODUCT FILE D tests/Feature/ConfigurableIdTest.php
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

use CoreX\Config\Data\CoreOptions;
use CoreX\Data\Laravel\JsonDocuments;
use CoreX\Database\Enums\IdStrategy;
use CoreX\Database\Laravel\IdColumns;
use CoreX\Settings\Data\SettingsScope;
use CoreX\Settings\Internal\DatabaseSettingsRepository;
use CoreX\Tests\Stubs\StubSettingDefaultsProvider;
use CoreX\Tests\Stubs\Widget;
use Illuminate\Database\Schema\Blueprint;
use Illuminate\Support\Arr;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\DB;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Schema;
use Illuminate\Support\Str;

it('model key behaviour follows config(corex.ids.strategy)', function (IdStrategy $strategy, string $keyType, bool $incrementing): void {
    config(['corex.ids.strategy' => $strategy]);
    app()->forgetInstance(CoreOptions::class);

    $widget = new Widget;

    expect($widget->getKeyType())->toBe($keyType)
        ->and($widget->getIncrementing())->toBe($incrementing);
})->with([
    'ulid' => [IdStrategy::Ulid, 'string', false],
    'uuid' => [IdStrategy::Uuid, 'string', false],
    'uuid7' => [IdStrategy::Uuid7, 'string', false],
    'int' => [IdStrategy::Int, 'int', true],
]);

it('generates the id kind matching the strategy', function (): void {
    config(['corex.ids.strategy' => IdStrategy::Ulid]);
    app()->forgetInstance(CoreOptions::class);
    expect(Str::isUlid((new Widget)->newUniqueId()))->toBeTrue();

    config(['corex.ids.strategy' => IdStrategy::Uuid]);
    app()->forgetInstance(CoreOptions::class);
    expect(Str::isUuid((new Widget)->newUniqueId()))->toBeTrue();

    config(['corex.ids.strategy' => IdStrategy::Uuid7]);
    app()->forgetInstance(CoreOptions::class);
    expect(Str::isUuid((new Widget)->newUniqueId()))->toBeTrue();

    config(['corex.ids.strategy' => IdStrategy::Int]);
    app()->forgetInstance(CoreOptions::class);
    expect((new Widget)->uniqueIds())->toBe([]);
});

it('config/corex.php declares the uuid7 strategy', function (): void {
    expect(config('corex.ids.strategy'))->toBe(IdStrategy::Uuid7);
});

// A22: app(CoreOptions::class)->idStrategy must fall back to the SAME default as
// config/corex.php. A host app that publishes the config file with a
// mergeConfigFrom() flat-array replace (no 'ids.strategy' key at all) hits
// app(CoreOptions::class)->idStrategy's own default, not the package config file's — the
// two must not diverge. Simulate a genuinely missing key (offsetUnset), not
// a config('corex.ids.strategy', ...) read that would just echo whatever the
// package config file already merged in.
it('app(CoreOptions::class)->idStrategy defaults to uuid7 when the key is entirely absent', function (): void {
    // Repository::offsetUnset() on a dot key only nulls the leaf (Arr::set
    // semantics), it does not remove it — has() would still report the key
    // present. Replace the whole 'corex.ids' node with one that never had
    // 'strategy' in it, so the key is genuinely absent, the way a host app's
    // mergeConfigFrom() flat-array replace (A22) would leave it.
    config(['corex.ids' => Arr::except(config('corex.ids', []), ['strategy'])]);
    app()->forgetInstance(CoreOptions::class);

    expect(config()->has('corex.ids.strategy'))->toBeFalse()
        ->and(app(CoreOptions::class)->idStrategy)->toBe(IdStrategy::Uuid7);
});

it('uuid7 ids are time-sortable (monotonic across successive generations)', function (): void {
    config(['corex.ids.strategy' => IdStrategy::Uuid7]);
    app()->forgetInstance(CoreOptions::class);

    $widget = new Widget;
    $ids = array_map(static fn () => $widget->newUniqueId(), range(1, 5));
    $sorted = $ids;
    sort($sorted, SORT_STRING);

    expect($ids)->toBe($sorted);
});

it('IdColumns emits integer key columns under the int strategy', function (): void {
    config(['corex.ids.strategy' => IdStrategy::Int]);
    app()->forgetInstance(CoreOptions::class);

    Schema::create('id_cols_probe', function (Blueprint $table): void {
        IdColumns::primary($table);
        IdColumns::reference($table, 'ref_id')->nullable();
        IdColumns::foreign($table, 'fk_id')->nullable();
    });

    expect(Schema::getColumnType('id_cols_probe', 'id'))->toBe('integer')
        ->and(Schema::getColumnType('id_cols_probe', 'ref_id'))->toBe('integer')
        ->and(Schema::getColumnType('id_cols_probe', 'fk_id'))->toBe('integer');

    Schema::drop('id_cols_probe');
});

// A4: sqlite collapses uuid and char(26) down to the same 'varchar' storage
// class — a getColumnType() assertion there cannot tell uuid7 apart from
// ulid, so a IdStrategy::Uuid7 => $table->ulid() regression in IdColumns
// would stay green forever on that lane. The real type only exists on PG,
// read here straight from information_schema.columns.data_type.
it('IdColumns emits the id-strategy-correct native column type on PG (information_schema)', function (IdStrategy $strategy, string $expectedDataType): void {
    config(['corex.ids.strategy' => $strategy]);
    app()->forgetInstance(CoreOptions::class);

    Schema::connection('pgsql')->dropIfExists('id_cols_pg_probe');
    Schema::connection('pgsql')->create('id_cols_pg_probe', function (Blueprint $table): void {
        IdColumns::primary($table);
        IdColumns::reference($table, 'ref_id')->nullable();
        IdColumns::foreign($table, 'fk_id')->nullable();
    });

    $types = DB::connection('pgsql')
        ->table('information_schema.columns')
        ->where('table_schema', 'public')
        ->where('table_name', 'id_cols_pg_probe')
        ->whereIn('column_name', ['id', 'ref_id', 'fk_id'])
        ->pluck('data_type', 'column_name');

    expect($types['id'])->toBe($expectedDataType)
        ->and($types['ref_id'])->toBe($expectedDataType)
        ->and($types['fk_id'])->toBe($expectedDataType);

    Schema::connection('pgsql')->drop('id_cols_pg_probe');
})->with([
    'uuid7 -> uuid' => [IdStrategy::Uuid7, 'uuid'],
    'uuid -> uuid' => [IdStrategy::Uuid, 'uuid'],
    'ulid -> character(26)' => [IdStrategy::Ulid, 'character'],
    'int -> bigint' => [IdStrategy::Int, 'bigint'],
])->group('pg');

// Validation (г), P1.19: the ulid seam is only real if the actual sys_*
// migrations build under it AND a write lands. This MUST run on PG, never
// sqlite — sqlite collapses column types, so a ulid written into a hardcoded
// uuid('id') column would pass there and prove nothing (that is exactly defect
// A4). On PG a ulid string in a uuid column raises SQLSTATE 22P02, so the
// insert below is what pins IdColumns::primary against a hardcoded `uuid`
// regression (mutation-check: restore uuid('id') in sys_settings → this reds).
it('core tables build and accept a settings row under the ulid id strategy', function (): void {
    config(['corex.ids.strategy' => IdStrategy::Ulid]);
    app()->forgetInstance(CoreOptions::class);

    DB::connection('pgsql')->statement('DROP SCHEMA IF EXISTS public CASCADE');
    DB::connection('pgsql')->statement('CREATE SCHEMA public');
    DB::purge('pgsql');

    $this->migrateSchemaSets(['axiomasoft/corex:tenant'], 'pgsql');

    // The id column must be the ulid type (PG char(26) → 'bpchar'), NOT 'uuid'
    // — a hardcoded uuid('id') regression would report 'uuid' here.
    expect(Schema::connection('pgsql')->getColumnType('sys_settings', 'id'))->toBe('bpchar');

    $repository = app()->make(DatabaseSettingsRepository::class, [
        'defaults' => new StubSettingDefaultsProvider([]),
        'connection' => 'pgsql',
    ]);

    $repository->set('crm', 'theme', JsonDocuments::capture('dark'), SettingsScope::account());

    $id = DB::connection('pgsql')->table('sys_settings')->value('id');

    expect(Str::isUlid((string) $id))->toBeTrue()
        ->and(JsonDocuments::decode($repository->get('crm', 'theme', SettingsScope::account(), JsonDocuments::capture('fallback'))))->toBe('dark');
})->group('pg');


WHOLE PRODUCT FILE M tests/Feature/PubSubDatabasePollTest.php
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

use CoreX\Messaging\Contracts\WorkerRuntime;
use CoreX\Messaging\Internal\DatabasePollPubSub;
use Illuminate\Database\Connection;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\DB;

uses()->group('pg');

const PUBSUB_DB_POLL_TEST_CONNECTION = 'pgsql';

function pubsubPg(): Connection
{
    return DB::connection(PUBSUB_DB_POLL_TEST_CONNECTION);
}

/** Fakes a console worker (not Octane) — the only runtime the guard allows by default (P1.24). */
final class WorkerConsoleRuntime implements WorkerRuntime
{
    public function isOctane(): bool
    {
        return false;
    }

    public function runningInConsole(): bool
    {
        return true;
    }
}

beforeEach(function (): void {
    // D12: sys_pubsub_messages/sys_pubsub_cursors are PG-only. Clean schema per
    // test (mirrors FeatureFlagsTest's/DepartmentTest's pg-lane pattern).
    pubsubPg()->statement('DROP SCHEMA IF EXISTS public CASCADE');
    pubsubPg()->statement('CREATE SCHEMA public');
    DB::purge(PUBSUB_DB_POLL_TEST_CONNECTION);

    $this->migrateSchemaSets(['axiomasoft/corex:tenant'], PUBSUB_DB_POLL_TEST_CONNECTION);
});

function pollingPubSub(): DatabasePollPubSub
{
    return app()->make(DatabasePollPubSub::class, [
        'connection' => PUBSUB_DB_POLL_TEST_CONNECTION,
        'runtime' => new WorkerConsoleRuntime,
    ]);
}

it('AC-25(a)/(б) publish→subscribe delivers the payload via the poll cursor, from a console worker context', function (): void {
    $pubsub = pollingPubSub();

    $pubsub->publish('module.enabled', ['module' => 'crm']);
    $pubsub->publish('module.enabled', ['module' => 'tasks']);
    $pubsub->publish('other.channel', ['module' => 'ignored']);

    $received = [];
    // A brand-new subscriber bootstraps its cursor at the channel's current
    // max(id) (skips pre-existing backlog) — pass replayBacklog to see it.
    $pubsub->subscribe('module.enabled', function (array $payload) use (&$received): void {
        $received[] = $payload;
    }, maxPolls: 1, replayBacklog: true);

    expect($received)->toBe([
        ['module' => 'crm'],
        ['module' => 'tasks'],
    ]);
});

it('persists rows in the configured table with a bigint cursor', function (): void {
    $pubsub = pollingPubSub();
    $pubsub->publish('module.enabled', ['module' => 'crm']);

    $row = pubsubPg()->table('sys_pubsub_messages')->first();

    expect($row->channel)->toBe('module.enabled')
        ->and(json_decode((string) $row->payload, true))->toBe(['module' => 'crm'])
        ->and($row->id)->toBeInt();
});

it('(г) D44: a new subscriber does NOT replay pre-existing history by default (skips straight to max(id))', function (): void {
    $pubsub = pollingPubSub();
    $pubsub->publish('module.enabled', ['module' => 'crm']);
    $pubsub->publish('module.enabled', ['module' => 'tasks']);
    $pubsub->publish('module.enabled', ['module' => 'sales']);

    $received = [];
    $pubsub->subscribe('module.enabled', function (array $payload) use (&$received): void {
        $received[] = $payload;
    }, maxPolls: 1, subscriber: 'invalidation-worker');

    expect($received)->toBe([]);
});

it('(г) D44: a rolling restart of the SAME subscriber resumes from its persisted cursor, not from 0 or from a fresh max(id)', function (): void {
    $pubsub = pollingPubSub();

    $pubsub->publish('module.enabled', ['module' => 'crm']);
    $pubsub->publish('module.enabled', ['module' => 'tasks']);

    // First run: bootstraps the cursor at max(id) — no history is replayed.
    $firstRun = [];
    $pubsub->subscribe('module.enabled', function (array $payload) use (&$firstRun): void {
        $firstRun[] = $payload;
    }, maxPolls: 1, subscriber: 'invalidation-worker');
    expect($firstRun)->toBe([]);

    // A message is published during the "restart window" (the process is down).
    $pubsub->publish('module.enabled', ['module' => 'sales']);

    // Second run (the restarted process): must see the restart-window message —
    // NOT skip it (max(id) start on every subscribe(), the rejected D44
    // design) and NOT replay 'crm'/'tasks' again (cursor-at-0 fallback).
    $secondRun = [];
    $pubsub->subscribe('module.enabled', function (array $payload) use (&$secondRun): void {
        $secondRun[] = $payload;
    }, maxPolls: 1, subscriber: 'invalidation-worker');

    expect($secondRun)->toBe([['module' => 'sales']]);
});

it('(д) corex:messaging:prune-pubsub truncates rows older than the threshold, leaving fresh rows intact', function (): void {
    pubsubPg()->table('sys_pubsub_messages')->insert([
        ['channel' => 'module.enabled', 'payload' => '{"module":"stale"}', 'created_at' => now()->subDays(60)],
        ['channel' => 'module.enabled', 'payload' => '{"module":"fresh"}', 'created_at' => now()],
    ]);

    $this->artisan('corex:messaging:prune-pubsub', [
        '--older-than' => '30 days',
        '--database' => PUBSUB_DB_POLL_TEST_CONNECTION,
    ])->assertSuccessful();

    $remaining = pubsubPg()->table('sys_pubsub_messages')->pluck('payload');

    expect($remaining)->toHaveCount(1)
        ->and(json_decode((string) $remaining->first(), true))->toBe(['module' => 'fresh']);
});


WHOLE PRODUCT FILE M tests/Feature/SettingsTest.php
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

use CoreX\Context\Data\TenantContext;
use CoreX\Data\Laravel\JsonDocuments;
use CoreX\Modules\Contracts\ModuleRegistry;
use CoreX\Modules\Data\ModuleManifest;
use CoreX\Modules\Internal\Compiler\RegistryCompiler;
use CoreX\Modules\Internal\Lifecycle\DatabaseModuleStateReader;
use CoreX\Modules\Internal\Registry\CompiledModuleRegistry;
use CoreX\Modules\Internal\Registry\RepositoryModuleRegistryCache;
use CoreX\Modules\Internal\Settings\DefaultSettingDefinition;
use CoreX\Modules\Internal\Support\ModulesOptions;
use CoreX\Modules\ModulesServiceProvider;
use CoreX\Settings\Contracts\SettingDefaultsProvider;
use CoreX\Settings\Contracts\SettingsRepository;
use CoreX\Settings\Data\SettingsScope;
use CoreX\Settings\Events\SettingChanged;
use CoreX\Settings\Exceptions\SettingValueTooLargeException;
use CoreX\Settings\Exceptions\UnsupportedScopeException;
use CoreX\Settings\Internal\DatabaseSettingsRepository;
use CoreX\Tests\Stubs\StubSettingDefaultsProvider;
use Illuminate\Database\Connection;
use Illuminate\Database\QueryException;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Cache;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\DB;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Event;
use Illuminate\Support\Str;

uses()->group('pg');

const SETTINGS_TEST_CONNECTION = 'pgsql';
const SETTINGS_USER_1 = '00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000001';
const SETTINGS_USER_2 = '00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000002';
const SETTINGS_WORKSPACE_1 = '00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000101';

function settingsPg(): Connection
{
    return DB::connection(SETTINGS_TEST_CONNECTION);
}

/**
 * @param  array<string, mixed>  $defaults
 * @param  list<string>  $sensitive
 */
function settingsRepositoryFor(array $defaults = [], array $sensitive = []): SettingsRepository
{
    return app()->make(DatabaseSettingsRepository::class, ['defaults' => new StubSettingDefaultsProvider($defaults, $sensitive), 'connection' => SETTINGS_TEST_CONNECTION]);
}

beforeEach(function (): void {
    // D12: sys_settings is PG-only (jsonb/CHECK/UNIQUE NULLS NOT DISTINCT).
    // Reset to a clean public schema so each test starts with no migration
    // history (mirrors ModuleLifecycleTest's pg-lane pattern).
    settingsPg()->statement('DROP SCHEMA IF EXISTS public CASCADE');
    settingsPg()->statement('CREATE SCHEMA public');
    DB::purge(SETTINGS_TEST_CONNECTION);

    $this->migrateSchemaSets(['axiomasoft/corex:tenant'], SETTINGS_TEST_CONNECTION);
});

it('cascades user over workspace over account over manifest default', function (): void {
    $repository = settingsRepositoryFor(defaults: ['crm.theme' => 'default-theme']);

    // Nothing stored anywhere yet: manifest default wins.
    expect(JsonDocuments::decode($repository->get('crm', 'theme', SettingsScope::user(SETTINGS_USER_1), JsonDocuments::capture('fallback'))))->toBe('default-theme');

    $repository->set('crm', 'theme', JsonDocuments::capture('account-theme'));
    expect(JsonDocuments::decode($repository->get('crm', 'theme', SettingsScope::user(SETTINGS_USER_1))))->toBe('account-theme');

    $repository->set('crm', 'theme', JsonDocuments::capture('workspace-theme'), SettingsScope::workspace(SETTINGS_WORKSPACE_1));
    expect(JsonDocuments::decode($repository->get('crm', 'theme', SettingsScope::workspace(SETTINGS_WORKSPACE_1))))->toBe('workspace-theme');

    $repository->set('crm', 'theme', JsonDocuments::capture('user-theme'), SettingsScope::user(SETTINGS_USER_1));
    expect(JsonDocuments::decode($repository->get('crm', 'theme', SettingsScope::user(SETTINGS_USER_1))))->toBe('user-theme');

    // Account level is untouched by the narrower writes above.
    expect(JsonDocuments::decode($repository->get('crm', 'theme')))->toBe('account-theme');

    // No default when nothing is stored and no manifest default exists.
    expect(JsonDocuments::decode($repository->get('crm', 'missing', null, JsonDocuments::capture('fallback'))))->toBe('fallback');
});

it('lets a narrower scope override a broader one without leaking across ids', function (): void {
    $repository = settingsRepositoryFor();

    $repository->set('crm', 'locale', JsonDocuments::capture('ru'), SettingsScope::user(SETTINGS_USER_1));

    expect(JsonDocuments::decode($repository->get('crm', 'locale', SettingsScope::user(SETTINGS_USER_1), JsonDocuments::capture('en'))))->toBe('ru')
        ->and(JsonDocuments::decode($repository->get('crm', 'locale', SettingsScope::user(SETTINGS_USER_2), JsonDocuments::capture('en'))))->toBe('en')
        ->and(JsonDocuments::decode($repository->get('crm', 'locale', null, JsonDocuments::capture('en'))))->toBe('en');
});

it('encrypts a sensitive value at rest and masks it in SettingChanged', function (): void {
    // Faking ONLY SettingChanged (not a blanket Event::fake()): a bare fake
    // also intercepts Eloquent's internal model events, which the persistence
    // path and audit integration still need.
    Event::fake([SettingChanged::class]);
    $repository = settingsRepositoryFor(sensitive: ['crm.api_key']);

    $repository->set('crm', 'api_key', JsonDocuments::capture('super-secret'));

    $raw = settingsPg()->table('sys_settings')
        ->where('namespace', 'crm')->where('key', 'api_key')
        ->value('value');

    expect($raw)->not->toContain('super-secret')
        ->and(JsonDocuments::decode($repository->get('crm', 'api_key')))->toBe('super-secret');

    Event::assertDispatched(SettingChanged::class, function (SettingChanged $event): bool {
        return $event->namespace === 'crm'
            && $event->key === 'api_key'
            && $event->sensitive === true
            && JsonDocuments::decode($event->old) === '••••••'
            && JsonDocuments::decode($event->new) === '••••••';
    });
});

it('updates in place through the repository rather than duplicating a row', function (): void {
    $repository = settingsRepositoryFor();

    $repository->set('crm', 'dup', JsonDocuments::capture('first'));
    $repository->set('crm', 'dup', JsonDocuments::capture('second'));

    expect(JsonDocuments::decode($repository->get('crm', 'dup')))->toBe('second')
        ->and(settingsPg()->table('sys_settings')->where('namespace', 'crm')->where('key', 'dup')->count())->toBe(1);
});

it('rejects a duplicate account-level row at the DB constraint (UNIQUE NULLS NOT DISTINCT), bypassing the repository', function (): void {
    // A57/A63: the repository always goes through updateOrCreate(), which
    // never emits a second INSERT for an existing row — that path can never
    // exercise the constraint. Hit the table directly with a raw duplicate
    // insert so the CHECK is the thing actually under test.
    settingsPg()->table('sys_settings')->insert([
        'id' => (string) Str::uuid7(),
        'namespace' => 'crm',
        'key' => 'dup-raw',
        'scope_type' => 'account',
        'scope_id' => null,
        'value' => json_encode('first', JSON_THROW_ON_ERROR),
        'is_sensitive' => false,
        'created_at' => now(),
        'updated_at' => now(),
    ]);

    expect(fn () => settingsPg()->table('sys_settings')->insert([
        'id' => (string) Str::uuid7(),
        'namespace' => 'crm',
        'key' => 'dup-raw',
        'scope_type' => 'account',
        'scope_id' => null,
        'value' => json_encode('second', JSON_THROW_ON_ERROR),
        'is_sensitive' => false,
        'created_at' => now(),
        'updated_at' => now(),
    ]))->toThrow(QueryException::class);
});

it('does not fire SettingChanged when set() re-writes the same value', function (): void {
    // The dispatcher is injected: fake it before the repository is built.
    Event::fake([SettingChanged::class]);
    $repository = settingsRepositoryFor();
    $repository->set('crm', 'theme', JsonDocuments::capture('stable'));
    $repository->set('crm', 'theme', JsonDocuments::capture('stable'));

    Event::assertDispatchedTimes(SettingChanged::class, 1);
});

it('fires SettingChanged once per actual value change', function (): void {
    Event::fake([SettingChanged::class]);
    $repository = settingsRepositoryFor();
    $repository->set('crm', 'theme', JsonDocuments::capture('first'));
    $repository->set('crm', 'theme', JsonDocuments::capture('second'));

    Event::assertDispatchedTimes(SettingChanged::class, 2);
});

it('all() merges the cascade with the narrower scope winning per key', function (): void {
    $repository = settingsRepositoryFor();

    $repository->set('crm', 'theme', JsonDocuments::capture('account-theme'));
    $repository->set('crm', 'locale', JsonDocuments::capture('en'));
    $repository->set('crm', 'theme', JsonDocuments::capture('user-theme'), SettingsScope::user(SETTINGS_USER_1));

    expect(JsonDocuments::decodeTree($repository->all('crm', SettingsScope::user(SETTINGS_USER_1))))
        ->toBe(['theme' => 'user-theme', 'locale' => 'en'])
        ->and(JsonDocuments::decodeTree($repository->all('crm')))->toBe(['theme' => 'account-theme', 'locale' => 'en']);
});

it('forget() removes exactly its own scope level, leaving other levels intact', function (): void {
    $repository = settingsRepositoryFor();

    $repository->set('crm', 'theme', JsonDocuments::capture('account-theme'));
    $repository->set('crm', 'theme', JsonDocuments::capture('user-theme'), SettingsScope::user(SETTINGS_USER_1));

    $repository->forget('crm', 'theme', SettingsScope::user(SETTINGS_USER_1));

    expect(JsonDocuments::decode($repository->get('crm', 'theme', SettingsScope::user(SETTINGS_USER_1))))->toBe('account-theme')
        ->and(JsonDocuments::decode($repository->get('crm', 'theme')))->toBe('account-theme');
});

it('rejects a department scope on every method (sys_settings has no department level, D38)', function (): void {
    $repository = settingsRepositoryFor();
    $department = SettingsScope::department('00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000301');

    expect(fn () => JsonDocuments::decode($repository->get('crm', 'theme', $department)))->toThrow(UnsupportedScopeException::class)
        ->and(fn () => $repository->set('crm', 'theme', JsonDocuments::capture('x'), $department))->toThrow(UnsupportedScopeException::class)
        ->and(fn () => $repository->forget('crm', 'theme', $department))->toThrow(UnsupportedScopeException::class)
        ->and(fn () => JsonDocuments::decodeTree($repository->all('crm', $department)))->toThrow(UnsupportedScopeException::class);
});

it('rejects a value over the 64KB quota', function (): void {
    $repository = settingsRepositoryFor();

    $tooLarge = str_repeat('x', 70_000);

    expect(fn () => $repository->set('crm', 'huge', JsonDocuments::capture($tooLarge)))
        ->toThrow(SettingValueTooLargeException::class);
});

it('resolves a manifest-declared DefaultSettingDefinition through the container-bound SettingDefaultsProvider once axiomasoft/corex is registered (P1.25, A68, test в)', function (): void {
    // Registering axiomasoft/corex must rebind the boxed Null default
    // (CoreServiceProvider::register()) to CompiledSettingDefaultsProvider —
    // reading the manifest default without any row in sys_settings is
    // exactly the wiring gap A68/Pending Work-P1 left open.
    $this->app->register(ModulesServiceProvider::class);

    $manifest = ModuleManifest::make('corex/stub-settings')
        ->title('Stub Settings')
        ->tablePrefix('sst')
        ->settings([
            DefaultSettingDefinition::make('theme', 'manifest-default')->sensitive(),
        ]);

    $compiled = (new RegistryCompiler)->compile([$manifest]);
    $path = sys_get_temp_dir().'/corex_modules_settings_test_'.bin2hex(random_bytes(4)).'.php';
    CompiledModuleRegistry::writeAtomic($path, [], $compiled);

    try {
        $this->app->instance(ModuleRegistry::class, new CompiledModuleRegistry(
            cachePath: $path,
            stateReader: app()->make(DatabaseModuleStateReader::class, ['connection' => SETTINGS_TEST_CONNECTION]),
            cache: new RepositoryModuleRegistryCache(
                Cache::store(),
                ModulesOptions::fromArray(['cache' => ['ttl' => 3600, 'prefix' => 'corex.modules']]),
            ),
            versionResolver: static fn (TenantContext $ctx): int => 0,
        ));

        // Pulled straight from the container (not hand-built) — the seam the
        // mutation-check pins: reverting ModulesServiceProvider's rebind to
        // NullSettingDefaultsProvider makes this resolve to Null instead.
        /** @var SettingDefaultsProvider $defaults */
        $defaults = $this->app->make(SettingDefaultsProvider::class);

        expect($defaults->defaultFor('sst', 'theme'))->toBe('manifest-default')
            ->and($defaults->isSensitive('sst', 'theme'))->toBeTrue()
            ->and($defaults->defaultFor('sst', 'missing'))->toBeNull();

        // sys_settings carries no row for it — the manifest default alone
        // reaches SettingsRepository::get().
        $repository = app()->make(DatabaseSettingsRepository::class, ['defaults' => $defaults, 'connection' => SETTINGS_TEST_CONNECTION]);

        expect(JsonDocuments::decode($repository->get('sst', 'theme')))->toBe('manifest-default')
            ->and(settingsPg()->table('sys_settings')->where('namespace', 'sst')->where('key', 'theme')->exists())->toBeFalse();
    } finally {
        unlink($path);
    }
});


WHOLE PRODUCT FILE D tests/Feature/UlidGenerationTest.php
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

use CoreX\Config\Data\CoreOptions;
use CoreX\Database\Enums\IdStrategy;
use CoreX\Tests\Stubs\Widget;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Schema;
use Illuminate\Support\Str;

// A21: forcing the strategy in this beforeEach() alone is not enough — by
// this point RefreshDatabase has already migrated `widgets` under whichever
// strategy was the global default (now Uuid7), so the id column is uuid,
// not ulid. On sqlite that mismatch is invisible (both collapse to a text
// storage class and Str::isUlid() just checks string shape), which is
// exactly how the A4/A21 regression stayed green. Rebuild the table here,
// AFTER the config flip and BEFORE any assertion, so the schema itself is
// actually ulid-typed for the rest of the test (see the PG-typed variant of
// this same table build in ConfigurableIdTest for the assertion that would
// catch this on PG).
beforeEach(function (): void {
    config(['corex.ids.strategy' => IdStrategy::Ulid]);
    app()->forgetInstance(CoreOptions::class);

    Schema::dropIfExists('widgets');
    (require __DIR__.'/../database/migrations/2026_06_14_100000_create_widgets_table.php')->up();
});

it('uses ULID string keys', function (): void {
    $widget = Widget::create(['name' => 'alpha']);

    expect($widget->getKeyName())->toBe('id')
        ->and($widget->getKeyType())->toBe('string')
        ->and($widget->getIncrementing())->toBeFalse()
        ->and(Str::isUlid($widget->id))->toBeTrue();
});

it('soft deletes', function (): void {
    $widget = Widget::create(['name' => 'alpha']);

    $widget->delete();

    expect(Widget::count())->toBe(0)
        ->and(Widget::withTrashed()->count())->toBe(1)
        ->and($widget->trashed())->toBeTrue();
});

WHOLE PRODUCT FILE M tests/Stubs/StubAuditableThing.php
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

namespace CoreX\Tests\Stubs;

use CoreX\Audit\Contracts\Auditable;
use Illuminate\Database\Eloquent\Concerns\HasUuids;
use Illuminate\Database\Eloquent\Model;

/**
 * EntityRegistry-driven model (AuditLogTest AC-20) — deliberately does NOT
 * use HasAuditLog, exercising the auto-observer path wired by
 * CoreX\Modules\Internal\Audit\AuditCapabilityWiring instead of the manual trait. Uuid
 * key (HasUuids) because entity_id in sys_audit_log is a uuid column.
 */
final class StubAuditableThing extends Model implements Auditable
{
    use HasUuids;

    protected $table = 'stub_auditable_things';

    protected $guarded = [];

    public function auditExcept(): array
    {
        return ['created_at', 'updated_at'];
    }

    public function auditSensitive(): array
    {
        return ['secret'];
    }
}
